## 总集成（Fable）

**定位（owner 2026-07-26/27 三次裁决的落地）**：五个维度 = ①多模态知识（D1 供给/证据/观察制造）、②多模态记忆（D2 跨实例存储）、③多模态技能（D3 动作库）、④多模态智能体系统（D4 静态组成）、⑤**多模态智能体系统进化**（D5 改进动力学）。D5 的研究对象是**进化现象本身**（系统不改权重随实例/时间变强）；**training-free RL 是我们选定的实现路线**（owner 原话："training free rl 只是实现进化的一个我们选定的研究方式"）——梯度式进化（agentic RL/SFT，如 OmniAgent-native/TAURA）是要"零梯度打平"的镜像对照，不是我们的赛道。每个子方向按 owner 07-27 指令给出：定义 → 语音域状态 / **视觉多模态是否存在** / **文本智能体是否存在** 三栏对照 → 开放增量 → **按其他领域协议改编的实验设计**（含三件必备设计件：答案等价估计、oracle 替代、声学条件键控）。

**规模**：**32/32 子方向全量交付**（D1 6、D2 7、D3 6、D4 7、D5 6；其中 D2.4-D2.7 与 D3.3-D3.6 由补齐轮 wf_cafdaeba-493 于 2026-07-27 交付并已插入正文——早先各处 FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED 标记所指的子方向现均已在文内，标记保留作交付时序的诚实记录），全部读集范围限定（cut 2026-07-26）；证据底座 = 本域 35 篇全文级条目（d0-d5：d0=5、d1=6、d2=3、d3=3、d4=9、d5=9）+ 跨域 73 篇供体（d6）+ 三张汇聚表（T1 37 行/T2 50 行/T3 42 行）。

**P 槽位归并（提案组合，owner 未签）**：24 个子方向归并为 **10 篇方向纲领书**——P1 供给定价与控制（D1.1/2/3/5/6）、P2 音频检索决策效用（D1.4）、P3 声学键控记忆（D2.1-3）、P5 工具信用与输出仲裁（D3.1-2）、P6 集成系统：奖励裁决 vs 手工裁决（D4.1-6）、P7 验证载体（D4.7）、P8 reward-as-controller（D5.3 修复/弃权决策权 + D5.5 跨实例 TFRL 学习规则；**原 P4 并入此处与 P3 接缝**）、P9 E1 决策校准门禁（D5.1 无标签 headroom 估计器 + D5.2 可用性门）、P10 选择压力下的 reward hacking（D5.6）、**P11（新槽位候选）停止/弃权/预算校准（D5.4）**——它有本组合里最厚的横切供体席（MARS/CITE/Knowing-When-to-Quit/Doomed-from-the-Start/Agentic-Abstention/Teach-it-to-stop），建议独立成篇；若 owner 倾向收敛为 9 篇可并入 P9。**NEW_SLOT_CANDIDATE 完整清单**（本组合内除 P11 外还有三处子方向正文提出候选新槽位，一并列出供 owner 裁决，而非仅 P11 一项）：D1.2 观察分支仲裁（D1 侧唯一在正文/供体两端均无占位的候选，当前无槽位承接）、D3-SD6 headroom 核算（pending delivery，正文自评为本组合候选强度最高项）、D4.5 非文本证据溯源/注入防护（当前挂靠 P6，三个视觉供体已占位而语音侧零占据）。

**跨维接缝（写提案时的引用规则）**：D1 拥有"是否购买观察"，D2 拥有"写入与键控"，D3 拥有"哪个工具执行与信用"，D4 拥有"决策权分配与非回归架构"，D5 拥有"信号何时可掌舵与如何随经验变强"；G1-G11（C1 报告契约，OFFLINE 报告义务）被所有维度的实验设计导入，不重复定义；E1 运行时准入门（RUNTIME 前置条件，按声学分层门限）归 P9（D5.2）独家定义，两者不是同一层。D3 本次交付 2/6 个子方向（D3.1/D3.2）；技能归纳/退役（SD5）与其余待交付子方向并未折叠进 D3.1/D3.2 的实验设计，其补齐已同日委托（见「规模」条）；正文中每处指向未交付子方向（D2-4..D2-7、D3-SD3..SD6）的引用一律标 FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED。标签语义统一：ACQUISITION_ITEM = 在 T2 中已有行但需下载/构建的资产；NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION = T2 中完全不存在、仅被点名的资产；两者不得混用。每个载体数据集只允许一份命名、版本化、blob 登记的校准切分（carve），被触及该载体的所有子方向共享同一切分；全组合维护唯一的累计金标暴露台账；执行期以 file-open hook 机械阻断金标读取（2607.13347 的做法）。

**表覆盖告知**：本文件中一部分承重的成本/方差/门槛数字（种子数、预算阶梯形状、阻断幅度报告的预注册假设门、FPR 目标）锚定在未进入 T1/T2/T3 三张汇聚表的供体论文上（至少含 2607.03436、2606.03054、2605.05701、2604.14853、2607.17136、2604.06240、2602.16699，分布于 D1.1/D1.4/D3.1/D3.2/D5.1/D5.4/D5.5），这些数字在下文标注为「d6-sourced，未入汇聚表」，与本域语音侧锚点（对 T1 三张表逐一核对过）证据层级不同。

**阅读顺序建议**：本节 → 各维「维度纲领」段（边界声明）→ 感兴趣子方向的三栏对照与实验设计 → 文末各维度综合。纪律申明：方向层文档，无 novelty 判定；占据/缺席声称一律带读集范围；供体只借方法带域标签（H5 WITHHELD）；实验设计=设计非执行，金标件圈死离线一次性校准。
# D1 多模态知识（供给/证据/检索/观察制造）— how evidence and observations are ACQUIRED and SUPPLIED to a frozen black-box speech/omni core

## 维度纲领

D1 owns the acquisition-and-supply side of the control plane: every decision determining what evidence the frozen core sees, in what form, in what order, how much, when to stop buying, and whether the purchase was worth anything. IN SCOPE: external-knowledge retrieval for audio tasks; re-manufacture of the observation itself (waveform operators, re-decode, re-segmentation, alternative front-ends, re-listening); decomposition as an evidence-acquisition act; supply topology (order, format, granularity, density, prompt position of audio vs instruction); anticipatory/prefetched supply; and the instruments that evaluate a supply act AS supply (did this acquisition change the answer, and in which direction). OUT OF SCOPE to avoid overlap: what persists across instances and how it is keyed/evicted (D2/P3 — D1 owns only the decision to pay, D2 owns the write and the key); which tool executes and how tool credit is assigned (D3/P5 — D1 owns how-many/which/when-to-stop over an already-given surface); answer-level repair and keep-or-revert over produced ANSWERS (D5/P8 — D1 keeps only repair whose OBJECT is the observation); the integrated-system charter and its end-to-end numbers (D4/P6); the calibration/admissibility contract for whatever scalar prices supply (D5/P9 — D1 imports G1-G11, does not define them). READ-SET SCOPE for every occupancy/absence claim: D1 read set = 6 papers (2602.10656, 2603.02206, 2509.21749, 2604.14520, 2604.09121, 2605.29430), cut 2026-07-26; adjacent audio evidence from the D0 audit quintet (2509.16971 AGR, 2510.02995 AudioToolAgent, 2603.09714 MUGEN, 2607.11433 Omni-Decision, 2606.15141 EChO-Agent) and the T1 37-row audio/omni corpus; donor read set = 73 cross-domain entries (40 text/code, 33 vision-GUI/VLM/embodied), cut 2026-07-26. Donors are METHOD sources carrying explicit domain labels; H5 withheld — no cross-modality effectiveness conclusion is drawn anywhere below. Level discipline: directions only, no novelty verdicts, no innovation locks. THE ONE-LINE D1 STATE OF PLAY: all three supply channels (retrieve / re-manufacture the observation / prefetch) plus supply topology are OCCUPIED as mechanisms with published numbers; in the D1 read set every acquisition decision is a free-form LLM classification, every quantity is a hard-coded constant (K_max=5, n=3-5, tau=0.40, max-iter 3, tool-cap 20), stop is either model-internal or gold-owned, abstain is absent in all six D1 read-set papers【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】, and no paper measures whether an acquisition changed the answer. The open layer is the decision layer over pre-built channels, and the missing instrument is the supply-effect discriminator.

## D1.1 供给定价与采集预算分配（priced acquisition / VoI over a heterogeneous speech supply menu）

**定义**：Treat every acquisition as a priced action: an explicit menu of supply acts over a frozen speech/omni core (re-decode at a different temperature/prompt-view, apply a waveform operator, re-listen to a span, request an alternative front-end/ASR view, issue a retrieval hop, ask a clarifying turn), each with a cost, and a policy that decides WHICH channel to spend on, HOW MUCH, WHEN to stop, and WHEN to abstain — by an argmax over an estimated value-per-unit-cost rather than by free-form generation. Direction covers the pricing machinery and the budget-allocation right; it does NOT cover which operators/tools exist (D3) nor the calibration contract of the scalar (P9).

**语音域状态**：ABSENT as pricing; OCCUPIED as unpriced mechanism (D1 read set, 6 papers, cut 2026-07-26). Every acquisition quantity is a constant: TwS 2509.21749 K_max=5 with an average 2.8 steps actually used and ~2.3x overhead; VoiceAgentRAG 2603.02206 n=3-5 predictions, top-k, tau=0.40, 0.5s rate limit; AudioRAG 2602.10656 unbounded hops with no tool-call/latency/search-cost accounting at all. Adjacent audio (D0/T1, same cut): AGR 2509.16971 max-iter 3, and its own sweep shows over-refinement (best round is 2 on MMAU-mini, 3 on MMAR, chosen in hindsight; round 4 drops); AudioToolAgent 2510.02995 tool-call cap 20, 5-10 typical, latency 30-60s/example vs 1-5s direct; Omni-DeepSearch (T1 2605.08762) budget (15,5) DEGRADES vs (10,3) on VIDEO/AMBIENT via over-search drift. T1 field fact 1: no scalar reward acts as an inference-time controller in the entire 37-row audio/omni corpus except the disqualified train-time cases. Speech-side oracle-ceiling asset that makes pricing measurable: 2605.29430 Table II publishes a per-dataset per-round GOLD-CONDITIONED trajectory (CS-Dialogue S2ER 19.73->4.16; GigaSpeech 21.47->12.35->7.00->3.49) whose stop is owned by a judge consuming Y_GT — classified EVAL-ONLY-GOLD in the T1 legality census — so regret-against-oracle-stop is a directly computable target and no new ceiling has to be built.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED (donor read set, 73 entries, cut 2026-07-26). 2605.01345 FOVEA/S-BOED (vision-VLM) formalizes acquisition as sequential Bayesian optimal experimental design, admits the Bellman form intractable, and derives the tractable surrogate U_t(d) ~ H_t(y)*J_t(d) with J = coverage x resolution (Prop 3.5), plus a training-free instantiation scoring candidate crops by a binary resolvability probe; it names 'adaptive invocation' — the meta-gate deciding whether to spend on acquisition at all — as its own future work. 2606.03054 ToolGate (vision-GUI/VLM) defines pre-call control and writes the target as VoI = Pr(correct|execute) - Pr(correct|skip) (Eq. 1), explicitly noting it is unobservable from a logged trajectory; its gate is a FITTED logistic regression (tau=0.5 tuned constant), matched-domain +1.65 but strict cross-domain only +0.32/+0.08, and tool identity alone supplies +1.31 of the +1.65 (most of the signal is a static per-tool prior). 2605.03231 cotomi Act prices whether to acquire more of a withheld observation (lazy observation, 471s->184s per task at comparable accuracy) but leaves the trigger to model discretion with no pricing. BASELINE_ANCHOR 2606.28864 (vision) shows the regime boundary: extra test-time compute helps reasoning benchmarks and HURTS perception-only benchmarks, with a 300-vs-1024-token cap ablation separating the two regimes and no controller exploiting it.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED, and this is the thickest donor lane (P1 primary-slot 11/73). 2602.16699 Calibrate-Then-Act formalizes cost-aware exploration as a POMDP with a parameterized discount D_theta carrying the cost, splits actions into exploration vs commit, and injects an estimated prior p-hat(Z|x); the black-box-legal half is verbalized confidence + isotonic recalibration (ECE 0.618->0.029 on PopQA) and the closed-form oracle rule is 'retrieve iff p_ret*gamma >= p_da'. Its structural finding is load-bearing for our positioning and is text-domain-scoped: end-to-end RL collapses to a static cost-insensitive policy (0% code-first traces at every rho) while an externally injected calibrated prior stays adaptive; but on realistic tasks the contract-eligible gains are small (QA 0.283->0.293, FILEREADING 0.229->0.240) and the dramatic 94%-vs-23% optimal-match is synthetic. 2605.05701 VOI-search gives the operational form: utility minus a budget-dependent penalty, normalized to value-per-budget, deterministic guard operators, under DUAL hard budgets, with a harm constraint E[L_harm] <= rho_harm. 2604.14853 AdaCompute makes the Lagrange multiplier the explicit unit price of compute, proves cost-monotonicity so binary search hits any average budget exactly, and reports realized cost in every cell so a method cannot win by overspending. 2605.17609 ADAP gives the formal core: the optimal verify/discard rule is a single break-even threshold tau* solving c_ver*E[max(h*(R)-tau*,0)] = tau**c_rew, with J* = c_ver/tau*, a 400x competitive ratio under RANK monotonicity only, and a centered-star-number lower bound proving the structural tie between proxy score and verified success is NECESSARY. 2604.04373 supplies the information-gain formalism where I(Y;C|X) can be NEGATIVE for misleading context.

**开放增量**：The direction-level step open for speech/omni: a supply policy for an ALREADY-DELIVERED observation, where the priced act is re-REPRESENTATION rather than acquisition of new material. The donors price where-to-look (FOVEA), which-action (VOI-search), whether-to-reveal-more (cotomi Act), granularity (Region4Web) and whether-to-verify (ADAP) — all under the assumption of a static artifact with a withheld remainder that can be re-acquired at higher fidelity. A waveform has no withheld remainder and re-listening returns the identical signal, so the coverage-x-resolution product has no speech instantiation and none of the donors supplies one. The scoped increment is: (i) enumerate a heterogeneous speech supply menu whose members are re-representations, not re-acquisitions; (ii) price each by a deployment-legal value estimate over an ESTIMATED answer-equivalence relation; (iii) make the price and the accept threshold acoustic-condition-keyed rather than global constants; (iv) evaluate at EQUAL SUPPLY COST against the published wrappers and against best-fixed-supply, never against the raw model, since raw-model deltas are already claimed. Explicitly NOT claimed: any cross-modality effectiveness inheritance from the donor numbers.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY (cheapest, largest published dynamic range): MELD + MELD-Hard1k — T2 row 'MELD / MELD-Hard1k (perturbed)', local_status needs_download (MELD) / must-be-reconstructed (Hard1k, not byte-identical), MELD publicly available under a research-use license to check at source, Hard1k not distributed but fully specified by 2509.21749 Table 3 (p=0.3 per perturbation; additive noise SNR U[0,25] dB over {white,pink,brown} with Bernoulli(0.2) temporal mask; reverb RT60 log-uniform [100,800] ms, room U[20,200] m^3; pitch shift U[+-4] semitones with Bernoulli(0.7) formant preservation via PSOLA; time stretch U[0.7,1.3] phase vocoder; 1000 utterances, seed 1337 perturb / 42 sample). Because our reconstruction cannot be byte-identical, BOTH arms are regenerated on our build and their absolute values are approximate references, not targets. SECOND: AudioRAG 500-item benchmark — T2 local_status needs_download, released at github.com/jingru-lin/AudioRAG with per-sample provenance, per-source licenses mixed (MMAU/CinePile/MNSC/FMA/Jazznet/MusicNet/iNaturalist/CHEER) and must be checked before any redistribution; requires a PINNED retrieval snapshot (paid Google Search API is nondeterministic and time-varying) and 80% of items derive from public dataset metadata, so retrieval-side contamination is live. THIRD (the oracle-ceiling carrier for the evidence-action menu): GigaSpeech Test (T2 metadata_only, public/standard) and CS-Dialogue Test-dagger (T2 needs_download, reconstructible from the stated code-switching filter rule; sizes not published so we re-derive and report our own split). ACQUISITION_ITEM（T2 中已有行、需下载/构建，批量下载须在下一步提案单独批准）：MELD（needs_download）、MELD-Hard1k（must-be-reconstructed）、AudioRAG 500-item（needs_download）、CS-Dialogue Test-dagger（needs_download）——本子方向的四个载体全部需要，因此不是零获取。NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION（T2 中完全不存在，仅被点名）：none required for this sub-direction.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and -3B (T3 A_frozen_core, open weight; direct-readout anchors from 2509.21749 Table 1: clean MELD 47.65 / 50.18, MELD-Hard1k 12.36 / 27.44). Audio-Flamingo-3-7B and Voxtral-3B/24B as same-family extension arms (T3; Hard1k 18.71 / 30.05 / 24.55). Qwen3-ASR-1.7B front-end for the ASR carrier (T3; Loop-0 anchors GigaSpeech WER 11.92 / S2ER 21.47 journal vs 12.25 / 14.12 conference — the discrepancy is judge-protocol-dependent and is itself a pinning requirement). LOCAL LANE: llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B GGUF (T3 access_mode: open weight, -ngl 28 resident llama-server with input_audio; HF/vLLM int4 OOMs in our environment) is CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY and never load-bearing. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT, stated explicitly: per T2/T3 digest cross-cutting hazard 4, our local lane is a weak-core verification lane and no lift measured on it may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction; ATLAS's own cross-benchmark weak-vs-strong asymmetry evidence is direction-unstable (2 of 3 benchmarks favour weak-core gain, HLE-Verified reverses). CAPABILITY PRECONDITION inherited from 2509.21749: the lane must be verified to emit tool calls ALONGSIDE input_audio; if it cannot, the decision function phi is externalized — and that externalization is itself a control-plane arm, arguably the more interesting one, not a failure.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS, always. (1) DIRECT READOUT: single pass with a standardized prompt identical to the wrapper's final-answer prompt, adopting 2604.14520 Sec 5.1's own hygiene rule so gains cannot be prompt-engineering. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: uniform random choice of supply action at matched expected supply cost (donor shape: 2604.14853's 'Random matched on E[b]', 2605.18652 Table 4's Random Episodic Context arm). (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: unweighted majority / MBR-chrF over the executed arms with no pricing and no external score — the empirically dominant free signal (T1 field fact 4; 2606.04730: MBR costs 0 model calls and shows 0% spurious-candidate selection). (4) ORACLE: per-instance best supply action over the executed menu, plus the always-acquire and never-acquire corners. This row is published by NO paper in the D1 read set — T1 records oracle_if_published NONE for 2602.10656, 2603.02206, 2509.21749, 2604.14520, 2604.09121 and 2605.29430 — so producing it is part of the deliverable. PUBLISHED ANCHORS, quoted with ids and compared at EQUAL SUPPLY COST rather than against raw models: 2509.21749 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B MELD-Hard1k 12.36 -> 48.97 (+36.61), operator leave-one-out full 48.97 / w-o denoise 33.17 / w-o enhancement 41.77 / w-o normalization 45.57 / w-o analysis 47.23, per-perturbation additive noise 8.7->43.9 and reverb 11.3->40.0; 2602.10656 Qwen3-Omni-30B raw 37.0 -> agentic 46.2 (+9.2 abs / +24.9% rel), Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 32.2 -> 39.5, Gemini-2.5-Flash 45.0; 2605.29430 Loop-0/1/3/10 curves as the GOLD-CONDITIONED CEILING (T1 legality: EVAL-ONLY-GOLD; both stop and evidence-generation consume Y_GT) with the 32B->8B corrector price list (2.11-4.07 S2ER points at Loop 10) as the small-model-recovery target. TWO MANDATORY EXTRA CONTROLS: (a) BLIND-FULL-CHAIN — apply every operator unconditionally — inherited from 2509.21749, whose operator set is matched one-to-one to the perturbations it injected; without this control a selection gain is indistinguishable from an operator-coverage gain, and this is the single highest-risk item in the sub-direction; (b) BEST-FIXED-SUPPLY, inherited from 2604.14520 Table 6, where the learned router LOSES to the single best fixed topology on 3 of 6 columns. PROMPT-LEVEL NULLS from 2606.03054 Table 3 (vision-GUI donor, shape only): a 'VoI self-report' arm that simply asks the core whether the supply is worthwhile, and a 'sparse prompt' arm — in the donor domain the self-report arm INCREASED calls to 5.88/episode and dropped accuracy to 60.0, which is why these are the right nulls for any external-controller claim.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL, usable in the runtime policy: discrete semantic entropy / cluster-count over k decodes (T3 B_evaluator_signal: legal in counts-only form; anchor 2604.25591 MMAU Qwen2.5-Omni-7B AUROC/AURAC 0.85/0.90 vs predictive-entropy 0.64/0.81 — and the probability-mass, predictive and length-normalized variants are ILLEGAL under the black-box contract because they require guaranteed logprobs); vote-rate P(True) self-verification (legal in vote-rate form, but 2604.25591 shows it collapsing to 0.52 = chance on 3B/AQUA-Bench and 0.44 = below chance on AF3/Audio-Hallucination, which is precisely why it must be condition-keyed rather than global); MBR / chrF consensus over candidates (T3: 0 model calls, contract-legal); S2ER binary semantic-equivalence judging in HYPOTHESIS-VS-HYPOTHESIS form — T3's own row states S2ER is deployment-legal as a judge prompt but every published study compares to gold, so the deployable form substitutes the competing hypothesis {Y_{t-1}} for Y_GT. FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION ON A SELF-CARVED SPLIT (gold-consuming, never in the loop): the isotonic recalibrator of verbalized confidence (2602.16699's calibrator is fit on a validation set with correctness labels — a supervised component inside an otherwise training-free method, and the paper never reports how many labels it needs or how it degrades under shift); the forced-answer-probe labels (2606.03054); AudioRAG's correctness filter and error-type judge, both of which receive the gold audio attribute (Tables 4-5); MMAR-Rubrics (T2: offline-calibration-only, answer-gated, deployment-illegal as an in-loop reward); the entire Agentic-ASR gold-conditioned stopper and user simulator (T1: EVAL-ONLY-GOLD); and the per-instance oracle row itself. MECHANICAL ISOLATION per gate G9: gold files are opened only by the offline calibration script behind a logged file-open hook, following 2607.13347's design, which the T1 census records as the strictest instance in the corpus.
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 headroom (no decision-utility claim without a per-instance oracle row over the executed menu plus a recovery fraction realized/oracle — the reference for how brutal this is: 2606.04730's realized +3.71 against an oracle +14.12 is 26% recovery, with 8 of 10 strategies net WORSE than plain greedy); G2 direct-readout floor as a first-class policy row; G3 the three nulls (random-among-N, unweighted majority with the scorer deleted, within-instance score permutation with the candidate distribution kept) — 2606.09925's below-random critic still gaining +6.9 over the random aggregator proves these are not optional; G5 margin honesty (report what the policy actually bought and skipped, not just the delta); G6 harm (worsen/improve share, up-mass vs down-mass, exact McNemar with Holm, never means alone); G7 heterogeneity (decompose before aggregating); G9 information boundary; G11 metric honesty (two metric families on the same output before declaring a task has no room). PAIRED TOKEN + SEMANTIC METRICS: MELD carrier reports 7-way accuracy alongside a free-form arm scored by the equivalence estimator; ASR carrier reports WER/CER/MER PAIRED WITH S2ER, justified by the strongest published instance of token-metric under-reporting on speech — 2605.29430 CS-Dialogue S2ER 19.73->4.16 (4.7x) while MER moves only 14.44->13.28 (~8% relative); AudioRAG carrier reports judge accuracy alongside the A/B/C/D error taxonomy used as a per-decision-right diagnostic (B tracks the audio-supply right, C the retrieval right, D the stopping right). PER-BUCKET STRATIFIED DELTAS: acoustic condition (clean / SNR band / RT60 band / pitch-shift / time-stretch on the reconstructed Hard1k; estimated SNR, speaker-change count, code-switch flag and entity density on the real corpora), reported before any aggregate.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: adopt the S2ER judge prompt with the gold argument replaced by the competing hypothesis, under the three-round BIDIRECTIONAL voting protocol from 2605.29430 Eq. 12-13 (each round queried twice with reversed argument order, positive only if both directions agree, majority over 3 rounds), operating at majority-3 because their own ablation gives single 0.8543 -> majority-3 0.8628 with majority-5/7 worse. The judge model and the prompt must be PINNED: the same group reports Loop-0 GigaSpeech S2ER as 14.12 and 21.47 on identical data across two versions, so an unpinned S2ER number means nothing. Validate on a self-carved split following their published recipe shape (120 items, 40 per condition, balanced equivalent/non-equivalent, binary labels, averaged human consensus as target, Pearson r for the judge AND for each expert so the judge is benchmarked against the human ceiling, 5 repeated runs with std reported); their reference band is r 0.8914/0.8280/0.9031 with std <= 0.029, exceeding experts by +0.016 to +0.038. Report the estimator's own tie/merge error and propagate it into every consensus statistic — over-merging hides competing answers, under-merging blocks any agreement signal from ever firing. Use the published Case A / Case B pair (WER 42.9% but semantically fine; WER 16.7% but semantically broken) as a two-item unit test. GATE ON A HETEROGENEOUS JUDGE: 2604.09121/2605.29430 use one Qwen3-32B as corrector, simulator AND judge, and 2606.09925's SelfGap shows the two strongest critics that are also generators carry the largest self-blindness (-24.6 and -25.2 PRMScore points on their own traces). (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: on the MCQ carriers (MELD 7-way, AudioRAG-500) the answer key provides a deterministic oracle usable OFFLINE ONLY — to fill the per-instance oracle row, to fit the isotonic calibrator, and to estimate base rates; on open-output carriers the substitute is the equivalence estimator with a DECLARED noise floor measured by repeated-scoring reproducibility (Kendall's W) plus paired significance, so any delta smaller than the noise floor is reported as such. Adopt ADAP's Appendix A.2 reward-validation diagnostics as the admissibility check on the price signal itself: rank-vs-correctness monotonicity, top-k coverage against a hypergeometric random-k baseline, and the per-prompt AUC distribution — the donor is explicit that only RANK monotonicity is required, not calibration, which is exactly the weakening a black-box setting needs. (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the supply price and the accept threshold are indexed by a condition key computed from the waveform alone (estimated SNR, reverb proxy, speaker-change count, code-switch flag, duration). The global-constant baselines are a mandatory comparison arm — VoiceAgentRAG's single global tau=0.40, TwS's global K_max=5, AudioRAG's unbounded hops — and the falsifiable claim is that keying beats the constant. Rationale for the piece, stated as a boundary not a result: a false-positive rate measured on clean audio does not constrain noisy audio, and the donors' latent variables (a file schema, a fact, a DOM node) are discrete, task-intrinsic and stable whereas the acoustic condition is continuous, drifts within a session, and is only partially observable.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS, five components reported separately and never merged: (a) frozen-core decode passes, (b) operator/DSP invocations, (c) retrieval hops plus pinned-snapshot bytes, (d) judge/estimator calls, (e) wall-clock and API dollars. Equal-supply-cost comparison is the PRIMARY axis. Adopt 2605.05701's HARD DUAL-BUDGET AUDIT — any item exceeding EITHER cap counts as FAILED, not averaged away — over a 4-level budget ladder crossed with carriers and cores, reported as per-cell win counts with an explicit refusal of a dominance claim. Report realized cost in every cell (2604.14853's discipline) so no method wins by quietly overspending, and use average realized cost as a degeneracy detector (a controller that collapses to 'always cheapest' shows up immediately). Known cost priors to beat: TwS ~2.3x inference overhead at 2.8 of 5 steps used; AudioRAG 4xA100 per component plus paid search; AudioToolAgent 30-60s/example vs 1-5s direct; 2604.14520 Table 8 shows a supply wrapper can be SLOWER when nothing is pruned (OmniBench per-token 0.682 -> 0.763 s). VARIANCE: >= 3 seeds for every sampled arm, bootstrap 95% CI on paired deltas, exact McNemar with Holm correction on binary outcomes. We explicitly do NOT inherit 2509.21749's greedy single-run-per-cell or 2604.14520's T=0 fixed-seed protocol — CoM's reported +0.6/+0.8/+0.9 deltas sit inside plausible single-run noise, and that is precisely the failure we must not reproduce.
- 击杀条件：(i) If, at equal supply cost, the priced policy fails to beat BOTH best-fixed-supply and the blind-full-chain control with non-overlapping CIs on at least half the acoustic-condition buckets, kill the sub-direction. (ii) If on a carrier the per-instance oracle over the supply menu is smaller than the equivalence-estimator noise floor, drop that carrier — there is no headroom to price, and pricing it would be measuring the estimator. (iii) If the recovery fraction (realized/oracle) does not exceed the random-among-N row, kill. (iv) If the condition-keyed threshold does not beat the global constant, downgrade to 'constants suffice on this surface' and publish it as a scoped negative result rather than reframing. (v) If the blind-full-chain control matches the selective policy, the observed effect is operator coverage and the selection claim is withdrawn.

**P 槽位**：P1 (evidence-state / knowledge-supply control for speech tasks). Secondary consumer: P9 supplies the calibration/admissibility contract (G1-G11) that this sub-direction imports rather than defines; P3 receives the acoustic-condition key as shared machinery.

## D1.2 观察分支与跨观察共识（observation branching + cross-observation consensus as a runtime controller signal）

**定义**：Execute k DIFFERENT observations of the same item — different operator subsets, permuted supply order, alternative segmentation/window, ASR-view vs audio-view, permuted multi-audio option order — and arbitrate among the resulting answers with a black-box agreement/margin statistic. This is categorically distinct from consensus over repeated decodes of a FIXED observation, the only form deployed in the D1 read set (6 papers, cut 2026-07-26)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】. Direction owns the construction of the branch set (a supply act) and the use of cross-branch agreement as a runtime selection/abstain signal; it does not own the reward mechanism itself (D5/P8) nor the tool surface the branches draw on (D3).

**语音域状态**：ABSENT in the branch-over-observations form (D1 read set, 6 papers, cut 2026-07-26). TwS 2509.21749 keeps ONE destructively-overwritten audio state (Alg. 1 line 9: x_a <- T[m](x_a)) with no branching, no revert and no side-by-side comparison of original vs processed observation. CoM 2604.14520 commits to one topology from a text-only prior, never executes two and compares; its own conflict diagnostic — A!=V disagreement on 77.23% of Music-AVQA items, joint model following the WRONG modality 41.78% (visual) vs 10.31% (audio) — is computed OFFLINE from unimodal probes and discarded at runtime, and it emits a <final_confidence> field in its own trace that it consumes nowhere. Neither ASR paper generates a candidate set at all: the frozen recognizer emits ONE hypothesis per turn, so no selection signal exists anywhere in the pipeline. PARTIAL in adjacent audio (D0 quintet, same cut): MUGEN 2603.09714 is the nearest occupant — audio-permutation self-consistency permutes the ORDER OF AUDIO OPTIONS in a 5/6-way audio-as-option MCQ, K fixed at 10, unweighted majority vote, Qwen2.5-Omni 28.69 -> 30.69 (APSC) -> 31.31 (APSC+CoT) against 20% chance, Gemini-3-pro +5.37 to +6.74, while PLAIN self-consistency is NEGATIVE for the open model (28.69 -> 28.00). What is demonstrated there is positional debiasing under a fixed budget, not consensus selection in general; no K sweep, no cost curve, no weighted or reward-based aggregation, no abstention. AudioToolAgent 2510.02995 triggers targeted re-queries on conflicting tool outputs but the arbitration is prompt-instructed LLM judgement described only qualitatively — no vote count, no K, no margin statistic, no calibration. EChO-Agent 2606.15141 does dual-pass arbitration over two REASONING configurations (not two observations), best-of-2, and its +1.9 verification ablation is confounded with rule-based format repair; the authors themselves attribute part of it to sampling rather than judging. VISA (T1 2606.07264) instantiates K=3 self-consistency voting inside a published audio agent but leaves it unnamed and un-ablated.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED in a related but structurally different form (donor read set, cut 2026-07-26). 2607.04334 (vision-GUI) builds a source-aware CONSISTENCY GATE from cross-CHANNEL agreement (pixels vs serialized structure) and reports it against a four-arm mitigation comparison on identical probes with an explicit cost column: the gate cuts task error 24-44 points at 1.8-2.0 queries/action while the blanket-certificate arm refuses 0.59-0.77 of conflicted actions with task error unchanged — a template for scoring an arbitration policy so a defense cannot win by refusing everything. 2605.01345 Appendix F.2 runs a SELECTOR ABLATION on a FIXED candidate pool (Probe vs VLM-direct-joint-choice vs Random: QA 30/20/10%, hit-centre 52/34/12, IoU 0.260/0.188/0.061), isolating the scoring mechanism from candidate generation — the exact select-vs-supply attribution design we need. 2606.28864 (BASELINE_ANCHOR, vision) includes Self-Consistency and Self-Aggregation as arms in a nine-method grid with per-method forward-pass accounting, and contains NO external-reward-guided Best-of-N arm at all.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED for same-observation consensus, and it supplies the sharpest cautions. 2605.04236 DASE (text) does adaptive consensus via sequential evidence accumulation and reports an inverted-U: every injection-based deliberation method peaks near ~30 inferences and DECAYS thereafter while independent self-consistency rises monotonically through 70; and dense injection IMPROVES headline accuracy by +1.3pp while HALVING right-wall coverage and collapsing the routing gap 30.7 -> 18.9pp — an intervention that improves the metric you report while destroying the signal you deploy. It also gives the coverage-matched gap-equivalence test (compare two admissibility signals at MATCHED coverage, bootstrap the CI on the gap DIFFERENCE, then report per-item disagreement separately: 25.5 vs 25.7pp, p=0.873, 37% disagree) as the way to argue complementarity rather than substitution. 2601.21484 ETS proves I=1, lambda->0 is formally identical to Best-of-N. 2606.04730 KIT (speech-adjacent but text-signal) shows MBR costs 0 model calls and has 0% spurious-candidate selection. 2605.03231 cotomi Act majority-votes N<=5 sampled ACTIONS pre-execution — variance reduction, explicitly not reward-guided selection.

**开放增量**：The scoped speech/omni increment: build the branch set from SUPPLY variables expressible on a frozen speech/omni core, and test whether cross-OBSERVATION agreement is a better selection and abstain signal than same-observation repeated-decode agreement AT MATCHED CALL BUDGET. That budget-matched isolation is the whole experiment — it is what separates observation diversity from decode diversity, and it appears nowhere in either read set. Two further scoped pieces: (a) permutation as a FREE reliability probe — 2604.14520 Table 3 proves the frozen core is order-sensitive (Music-AVQA Exist(7B) 58.0 -> 49.0 under a bare A->V / V->A swap, the largest shift in that table; Loc(7B) 40.9 -> 33.8; Count(7B) 68.4 -> 63.2) and frames that sensitivity as a pathology; it can instead be harvested as an instance-level reliability signal obtainable in 2 black-box calls with no gold, with disagreement as a natural abstain/escalate trigger; (b) branching as the missing REVERT path for observation repair — TwS's destructive in-place update makes a bad operator unrecoverable, and holding the original observation as a live branch converts an unrecoverable error into a scored choice. What we do NOT inherit: any claim that vision's two-channel consistency gate transfers, since speech has no free near-deterministic second channel (an ASR transcript is itself a model output, so 'edit exactly one channel' has no clean analogue and any transcript perturbation confounds the intervention with transcriber error).

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY: MELD-Hard1k reconstruction (T2 must-be-reconstructed; recipe in 2509.21749 Table 3) — the operator-branch arm set, where k branches are k distinct operator subsets applied to the SAME source waveform rather than one destructive chain. Huge published dynamic range (12.36 -> 48.97) and same-family cores. SECOND: Music-AVQA and AVHBench A-Hal/V-Hal (T2 rows, both metadata_only, public) for the permutation-branch probe, where the branch variable is supply ORDER and the published order-sensitivity is already quantified. THIRD: AudioRAG 500-item set (T2 needs_download) for query-framing branches — the same audio queried under k different natural-language framings through the <begin audio analysis> envelope, which is precisely the residual error class (type B) the wrapper does not fix. FOURTH (multi-audio permutation): MUGEN's 1,750-instance audio-as-option set is NOT in T2 and is therefore named only as NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION (benchmark and code stated at github.com/danielqwer/MUGEN per the D0 transcription); until acquired, the permutation-branch arm runs on the audio-visual carriers above. ACQUISITION_ITEM（T2 中已有行、需下载/构建，批量下载须在下一步提案单独批准）：MELD-Hard1k（must-be-reconstructed）、Music-AVQA / AVHBench（metadata_only, public）、AudioRAG 500-item（needs_download）。【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and -3B (T3; MELD-Hard1k anchors 12.36 / 27.44 and clean 47.65 / 50.18). Audio-Flamingo-3-7B and Voxtral-3B/24B as the same-family breadth arms (T3). Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B via the local llama.cpp GGUF lane (T3) for CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY, never load-bearing: per T2/T3 hazard 4 it is a weak-core lane and no lift measured on it may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction. ARM-SPACE AVAILABILITY IS CORE-DEPENDENT AND MUST BE ENUMERATED BEFORE DESIGNING: 2604.14520 records that Ola-7B 'lacks native support for interleaved modality tokens, thereby restricting the Planner's optimization space', i.e. which branches are even expressible is a property of the deployed core; our llama.cpp lane's expressible branch set (multiple input_audio segments? repeated audio? tool-call-with-audio?) must be enumerated first, and any branch it cannot express is dropped from the design rather than simulated.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS. (1) DIRECT READOUT: one observation, one decode, standardized prompt (2604.14520 Sec 5.1 hygiene rule). Anchors: 2509.21749 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B Hard1k 12.36; 2604.14520 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B Music-AVQA A/V/A-V 77.9/82.2/62.9 and AVHBench A-Hal/V-Hal 73.2/66.8. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: pick one of the k executed branches uniformly at random — the honest null (2605.18652 Table 4's Random arm shape). (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: unweighted majority / MBR over the k branch answers with no external score. MANDATORY BUDGET-MATCHED TWIN: the same k calls spent as k decodes of ONE fixed observation. The delta between these two rows IS the sub-direction's central quantity. (4) ORACLE: per-instance best branch. No such row exists in the D1 read set (T1: oracle_if_published NONE for all six D1 rows) nor in 2604.14520 over its own three topologies. PUBLISHED ANCHORS: 2509.21749 TwS 48.97 (single destructive chain, model-self-selected) as the same-budget comparator once both arms are regenerated on our reconstruction; 2604.14520 Table 6 fixed-topology triple (Sequential 77.2/82.0/63.4/75.2/69.9/54.8, Parallel 74.5/85.4/64.8/83.7/72.8/43.7, Interleaved 77.9/82.2/62.9/73.2/66.8/55.5, CoM 78.8/85.8/63.7/78.2/79.7/55.4) as the best-fixed control the router loses to on 3 of 6 columns; MUGEN's APSC deltas (Qwen2.5-Omni 28.69 -> 31.31; plain SC NEGATIVE at 28.00) as the permutation-vs-plain-consensus reference shape, cited with its D0-recorded caveats (K fixed at 10, no sweep, two model families only). BLIND-FULL-CHAIN control carried over from sub-direction 1 wherever operator branches are used.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL runtime signals: branch-agreement count and margin over an ESTIMATED equivalence relation; discrete semantic entropy / cluster count across branches (T3: legal in counts-only form; the probability-mass and predictive variants are ILLEGAL, requiring guaranteed logprobs); MBR/chrF consensus (T3: 0 model calls); S2ER in hypothesis-vs-hypothesis form as a margin arbiter ON TOP of the consensus signal — never as a replacement, because consensus is the empirically dominant component across every dimension that measures it (T1 field fact 4) and judge scalar quality alone is necessary but not sufficient. FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION on a self-carved split: the per-instance oracle branch label; the gold-argument form of S2ER; any correctness-labelled fit of the agreement threshold; 2604.14520's Align_A/Align_V/Error_A/Error_V conflict-diagnostic instrument, which needs the gold answer to distinguish 'followed the wrong modality' from 'followed the right one' and is therefore an offline evaluation of an arbitration policy, not a runtime signal. Adversarial-robustness note carried from the donors and NOT assumed to transfer: 2606.03054 shows prompt-level skepticism is not a defense against injected evidence (belief collapse 0.993 -> 0.142, skeptical prompt recovers only to 0.461); the speech analogue (a transcript overriding an acoustic impression) may behave differently and must be measured, not inherited.
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 (per-instance oracle over the branch set + recovery fraction), G2 (direct-readout floor), G3 (the three nulls — random-among-N, unweighted majority with the scorer deleted, within-instance score permutation), G4 fidelity/resolution on the arbitration signal (tie rate on distinct-output pairs, tie rate restricted to pairs separated by at least epsilon on the target metric, rank reproducibility across repeated scoring via Kendall's W, and positional bias P(pick A | B better) - P(pick B | A better); reference failure values to beat from the corpus: 49.1%/37.5% tie rates, W = 0.36, 68.6% self-agreement at temperature 0, +35.3pp sequential-pairwise A-bias with Round-Robin reducing it to +3.2pp), G5 margin honesty (report how many items the arbiter actually moved — an arbiter that avoids harm by moving 12 of 476 is a disguised no-op), G6 harm, G7 heterogeneity, G9 boundary, G10 self-bias (never let the generator score its own branches without measuring SelfGap; 2606.09925 records -24.6 and -25.2 for the two strongest critics that are also generators). SEMANTIC ALONGSIDE TOKEN: accuracy on MCQ carriers paired with an equivalence-estimator-scored free-form arm; on any ASR-flavoured branch set, WER/CER/MER paired with S2ER. PER-BUCKET: acoustic-condition buckets as in sub-direction 1, plus a branch-disagreement-magnitude bucket so we can report where agreement is informative and where it is merely noise.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: this sub-direction is the one where the estimator is load-bearing rather than incidental — 'agreement across branches' is UNDEFINED without it for free-form speech output, and the donors all quietly assume a discrete answer identity (DASE counts plurality, cotomi Act majority-votes actions, ETS majority-votes an extractable discrete answer, MUGEN votes MCQ letters). Same construction as sub-direction 1 (S2ER prompt, gold argument replaced by the competing hypothesis, three-round bidirectional voting at the majority-3 operating point r 0.8628, pinned judge + prompt, validated on a self-carved 120-item split with 5 repeated runs and an expert-comparison column). ADDITIONAL REQUIREMENT UNIQUE HERE: report the clustering error in BOTH directions and propagate it — over-merging hides genuinely competing branches and inflates apparent agreement; under-merging makes every item look contested and the signal never fires. Report agreement statistics under at least two merge thresholds so the conclusion cannot ride on one clustering choice. (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: MCQ carriers (MELD 7-way, Music-AVQA, AVHBench, AudioRAG-500) supply a deterministic per-instance best-branch oracle OFFLINE ONLY, which is what fills the headroom row no paper in the D1 read set publishes【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】; for free-form branches the substitute is the equivalence estimator with a declared noise floor from repeated-scoring Kendall's W plus paired significance, and any branch-selection delta below that floor is reported as indistinguishable rather than as a small win. Gold never enters the runtime arbiter (G9, hook-logged). (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: report the agreement-vs-correctness relation per acoustic-condition bucket and key the abstain threshold by condition. Justification is empirical from the corpus rather than assumed: 2604.25591 shows P(True) collapsing from useful to chance (0.52) to below chance (0.44) as the core and benchmark change, so a single global agreement threshold is exactly the failure mode being avoided; and 2509.21749's own per-perturbation table (additive noise 8.7 -> 43.9, reverb 11.3 -> 40.0, pitch 19.2 -> 37.5, stretch 23.4 -> 35.5) shows the observation-repair regime differs by condition, which means the branch set's usefulness does too.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS: branch construction cost (operator/DSP invocations, re-segmentation passes) is reported SEPARATELY from decode cost, because the central comparison is budget-matched at DECODE calls while branch construction may be nearly free (a permutation) or expensive (source separation). Report frozen-core decode passes, operator invocations, judge calls, wall-clock, and dollars; run a k-sweep (k in {2,3,5,8}) with the accuracy-vs-cost curve per policy family, which neither MUGEN (K fixed at 10) nor TwS (K_max fixed at 5) provides. Carry the donors' regime warning explicitly as a hypothesis to test rather than a result to inherit: 2605.04236 (text) reports an inverted U where injection-based deliberation peaks near ~30 inferences and decays while independent self-consistency keeps rising through 70; whether branch diversity has an analogous peak on speech is exactly what the k-sweep measures. VARIANCE: >= 3 seeds per sampled arm, bootstrap 95% CI on paired deltas, exact McNemar with Holm. Note that MUGEN's own numbers sit near chance headroom (28.69 -> 31.31 against 20% chance on 5 options) — a reminder that on this surface CIs, not point deltas, decide.
- 击杀条件：(i) If cross-observation agreement does not beat same-observation repeated-decode agreement at MATCHED decode budget, with non-overlapping CIs on at least half the acoustic-condition buckets, the sub-direction is dead — that single comparison is the whole claim. (ii) If the per-instance branch oracle is not meaningfully above the consensus-only row, there is nothing for an arbiter to recover and the carrier is dropped. (iii) If the equivalence estimator's tie rate on distinct-output pairs exceeds the G4 reference failure band, the agreement statistic is uninterpretable and the estimator must be fixed before any selection claim is made. (iv) If a permutation-only branch set (free, 2 calls) captures most of the effect, report that and DROP the expensive operator-branch machinery rather than defending it. (v) If branch selection's gain is not separable from operator coverage under the blind-full-chain control, the selection claim is withdrawn.

**P 槽位**：P1 (evidence-state / knowledge-supply control) primary. Imports P9 (D5.2 E1 admissibility gate / G1-G11) for the abstain threshold's calibration, matching D1.1/D1.3: D1.2 supplies the branch-agreement STATISTIC, P9/D5.2 owns the threshold's calibration【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】. NEW_SLOT_CANDIDATE flag: this is the one D1 surface with no occupant in the branch-over-observations form on either the speech side or the donor side, and it straddles P1 (the branch set is a supply act) and P8 (the arbiter is a reward-as-controller); if the portfolio wants observation-branch arbitration as its own charter rather than as a component of P1, this is the sub-direction to promote.

## D1.3 供给拓扑与粒度控制（supply topology, order, granularity and density as an executed arm set）

**定义**：The form of the supply, not its quantity: modality/channel subset and ordering, input format (parallel / sequential / interleaved), segmentation and chunking granularity, supply density (frames, chunks, context length), resampling, and the prompt position of the audio relative to the instruction — decided per instance by an external score over EXECUTED arms rather than by a text-only classification made before the signal is seen. Distinct from sub-direction 1 (which prices HOW MUCH) and from sub-direction 2 (which arbitrates ACROSS branches): here the object is the arm alphabet itself and the finding that its members are not interchangeable on a frozen core.

**语音域状态**：OCCUPIED as a per-query LLM classification, ABSENT as a scored choice over executed arms (D1 read set, cut 2026-07-26). 2604.14520 Chain-of-Modality (audio-VISUAL, not speech-only) makes supply order and format an explicit per-query decision variable — a text-only planner emits (task type, cognitive depth, ordered minimal-sufficient modality subset, topology in {Parallel, Sequential, Interleaved}) over one frozen backbone reused under three prompt roles, fully training-free on the Plan-Decide path. Its DIAGNOSIS is stronger than its method and is the load-bearing borrow: unimodal baselines beat joint A+V inference on unimodal-sufficient tasks (Qwen2.5-Omni-7B Music-AVQA Loc: Visual 83.02 vs A+V 82.71; Comp: Audio 49.32 vs A+V 45.2); bare permutation of A->V to V->A moves cells by up to 9 points (Exist(7B) 58.0 -> 49.0, the largest shift in Table 3; Loc(7B) 40.9 -> 33.8; Count(7B) 68.4 -> 63.2; Temp(3B) 35.3 -> 38.8); interleave density inflates 'Yes' rates on AV-Matching; and the frame ablation shows CoM at 5 frames (54.97 on DailyOmni) beating the fixed-topology baseline at 15 frames (54.80) with 66% fewer visual tokens, while the fixed baseline degrades monotonically with visual granularity on AVHBench V-Hal and CoM stays flat. THE GAP IS QUANTIFIED BY THE PAPER ITSELF: its router LOSES to the single best fixed topology on 3 of 6 columns (AVHBench A-Hal 78.2 vs Parallel 83.7; Music-AVQA A-V 63.7 vs Parallel 64.8; DailyOmni 55.4 vs Interleaved 55.5), the planner never sees the signal before pruning it (query-type-conditioned, never instance-evidence-conditioned), the plan degenerates silently on the 3B core ('fallback to default sequences') with nothing detecting it, and no per-instance oracle over the three topologies is published anywhere in the D1 read set. The SPEECH-ONLY topology alphabet — segmentation/chunking, resampling, prompt position of audio relative to instruction, repeated-listen ordering — is untested; 2604.14520's own domain note says the A/V/interleave alphabet is audio-video-specific and the visual-dominance prior has no analogue in a speech-text deployment. Task-conditioned negatives are real and per-subset: Ola-7B WorldSense 41.1 -> 40.0 (-1.1), Qwen2.5-Omni-3B AVHBench A-Hal 77.8 -> 73.5 (-4.3) on a model whose V-Hal rises +18.3.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED, and it supplies the best measurement discipline in the donor set. 2605.07134 Region4Web (vision-GUI, TRAINED components — donor for measurements and designs only) treats observation granularity as a design choice separate from action granularity and, crucially, DERIVES the mechanism from measured environment structure before proposing it: consecutive actions have median LCA-depth ratio 0.48 vs a random baseline of 0.22 with 81.7% above the random median (so a region is a natural unit), and 52.9% of within-page steps produce zero DOM change with 74.4% under 5% (so re-observing every step is wasteful). Its load-bearing control arm is an ELEMENT-LEVEL variant achieving the same compaction at the wrong granularity and doing WORSE (46.1% at 4,013 tokens vs backbone 48.5% at 5,410, vs full pipeline 53.9% at 3,814) — the same compaction at the wrong granularity hurts. It reports success rate jointly with observation length (-30% to -54% tokens across six actor configurations) and openly shows domain-level regressions inside an improving average (GPT-5.1 overall 45.2 -> 47.5 but CMS 57.1 -> 54.5 and Reddit 65.1 -> 60.5 drop; SteP overall 39.5 -> 38.7, a net regression). 2606.28864 (vision BASELINE_ANCHOR) contributes the token-budget-cap ablation (300 vs 1024 with reliable extraction preserved so truncation does not confound) as the diagnostic separating 'the chain drives the answer' from 'the model loses focus', and their own regime finding — extra compute helps reasoning benchmarks, hurts perception-only ones — implies a per-instance verbosity/budget router they never build.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED at the context-composition level. 2604.04373 (text) sweeps memory/context size and reports that retrieval RELEVANCE rises monotonically while task performance is NON-MONOTONIC with a sweet spot at intermediate size that beats full memory — a ready-made falsification of 'more relevant supply is better supply' — and formalizes value-of-context as information gain that can be NEGATIVE for misleading context; it also supplies the random-context control arm. 2504.21776 WebThinker (text; the parent framework AudioRAG is built on) contributes the digest-returning acquisition tool: a hierarchical explorer that returns a condensed answer to the main chain so the controller's context stays bounded — a supply-topology pattern rather than a retrieval one. 2605.03231 cotomi Act (vision-GUI, but its measurement is topological) plots the observation/history design trajectory in accuracy-vs-token-cost space and finds current-observation and history axes PARTIALLY SUBSTITUTE (combined +13.8pp < the sum of individual +22.1pp) — a genuinely reusable warning about non-additivity when composing supply mechanisms.

**开放增量**：Scoped increment for speech/omni, in three parts. (a) ENUMERATE THE EXPRESSIBLE ARM SET ON THE DEPLOYED CORE FIRST. Arm availability is a property of the core, not the method — 2604.14520 records that Ola-7B lacks interleaved-token support and that this 'restrict[s] the Planner's optimization space' — so the speech topology alphabet (single-pass vs chunked with k window sizes; audio-before-instruction vs instruction-before-audio; single vs repeated audio; raw-audio view vs ASR-transcript view vs both) must be enumerated against our actual deployment before anything is designed over it. (b) EXECUTE k TOPOLOGIES AND PUBLISH THE PER-INSTANCE ORACLE ROW absent from the D1 read set【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】, so the headroom of the topology decision is measured before any controller is claimed. (c) HARVEST ORDER SENSITIVITY AS A SIGNAL rather than treating it as a pathology to be structurally avoided. Two boundaries stated rather than assumed: Region4Web's motivating measurement INVERTS in audio — '52.9% of steps have zero change' is what justifies caching an observation, but a stream is never zero-change — and its whole method needs persistently addressable node identity plus a withheld remainder that can be revealed on demand, neither of which a waveform provides; and 2604.14520's attention-ratio evidence is white-box and must be replaced by behavioural probes under our contract.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY (order/format arms with published order-sensitivity to replicate and extend): Music-AVQA and AVHBench A-Hal/V-Hal (T2, both metadata_only, public), plus DailyOmni and OmniBench and WorldSense (T2, metadata_only, public) for the density and negative-transfer cells. SECOND (speech-only topology alphabet, where the alphabet is untested): GigaSpeech Test (T2 metadata_only) and CS-Dialogue Test-dagger (T2 needs_download, reconstructible from the stated code-switching filter; sizes unpublished so we re-derive and report our own split) for chunking/window/view arms on long-form and code-switched audio; AISHELL-NER Dev/Test-dagger (T2 needs_download, reconstructible from the AISHELL-NER entity-bearing filter) because entity-bearing items are where granularity should bite and where token metrics are blindest. THIRD: MELD-Hard1k reconstruction (T2 must-be-reconstructed) for the prompt-position and repeated-listen arms under controlled acoustic conditions. ACQUISITION_ITEM（T2 中已有行、需下载/构建，批量下载须在下一步提案单独批准）：GigaSpeech Test（metadata_only）、CS-Dialogue Test-dagger（needs_download）、AISHELL-NER Dev/Test-dagger（needs_download）、MELD-Hard1k（must-be-reconstructed）、MOV-Bench（needs_download, request-or-rebuild; FineVideo source under an academic-use licence, not redistributable）——MOV-Bench 虽在 T2 中已有行，但本次仅点名不设计，留待长音频密度臂需要时再纳入。NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION（T2 中完全不存在）：none for this sub-direction.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and -3B (T3; the 3B core is deliberately retained because 2604.14520 documents silent plan degeneration on it — 'fallback to default sequences when complex orchestration fails' — which is a phenomenon this sub-direction should detect rather than avoid). Ola-7B is cited as the arm-availability cautionary case (T3: 'lacks native support for interleaved modality tokens — action space is core-dependent') and is not a required run. Qwen3-ASR-1.7B (T3) as the front-end for the ASR-view arm. LOCAL LANE: llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B GGUF (T3, -ngl 28 + input_audio) is CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY with the weak-core caveat stated (T2/T3 hazard 4: no lift measured on this lane may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction); it is also the natural place to run the arm-space enumeration itself, since the question 'can this deployment even express multiple input_audio segments / repeated audio / interleaved text-audio?' is a capability check, not a research result.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS. (1) DIRECT READOUT: the deployment-default topology, single pass, standardized prompt identical to the wrapper's final prompt per 2604.14520 Sec 5.1's own hygiene rule. Anchors: Qwen2.5-Omni-7B Music-AVQA A/V/A-V 77.9/82.2/62.9, AVHBench A-Hal/V-Hal 73.2/66.8, OmniBench 46.5, DailyOmni 54.8, WorldSense 39.2; and for the speech carriers, Loop-0 rows 2605.29430 GigaSpeech S2ER 21.47 / WER 11.92, CS-Dialogue S2ER 19.73 / MER 14.44, AISHELL-NER Test S2ER 19.91 / NER 2.45. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: uniform random topology per instance at matched cost. (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: majority/MBR over the executed topologies with no external score. (4) ORACLE: per-instance best topology over the arm set — the row that exists nowhere in the D1 read set and whose absence means the headroom of the topology decision is currently unquantified. BEST-FIXED-TOPOLOGY IS A MANDATORY FIFTH CONTROL, not optional: 2604.14520 Table 6 (Sequential 77.2/82.0/63.4/75.2/69.9/54.8, Parallel 74.5/85.4/64.8/83.7/72.8/43.7, Interleaved 77.9/82.2/62.9/73.2/66.8/55.5, CoM 78.8/85.8/63.7/78.2/79.7/55.4) is the control we inherit and it is unflattering to routers. ELEMENT-LEVEL-EQUIVALENT CONTROL borrowed in shape from 2605.07134: the same supply compaction applied at the WRONG granularity, to prove the gain is granularity and not compaction (their arm: 46.1% at 4,013 tokens vs 53.9% at 3,814). PROMPT-TEMPLATE ROBUSTNESS CONTROL: 2604.14520's own Table 7 shows template choice dominating topology by tens of points (under the multiple-choice template its AVHBench A-Hal is 28.9 vs 22.7 — both catastrophic), so at least two templates are run and reported separately.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL: cross-topology agreement and margin over the estimated equivalence relation; the 2-call permutation probe (run A->B and B->A, or audio-first and instruction-first, and use disagreement as an instance-level reliability signal) — no gold, no logprobs, 2 black-box calls; discrete semantic entropy in counts-only form (T3: legal; probability-mass/predictive/length-normalized variants ILLEGAL); MBR/chrF (T3: 0 model calls); S2ER hypothesis-vs-hypothesis as a margin arbiter layered on consensus. FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION on a self-carved split: the per-instance topology oracle; 2604.14520's A!=V / Align_A / Align_V / Error_A / Error_V conflict-and-blind-trust diagnostic, which needs the gold answer to separate 'followed the wrong channel' from 'followed the right one' (it is a ready-made offline EVALUATION of any arbitration policy we build, and it needs only unimodal probes plus the joint answer, all black-box, but it is not a runtime signal); any correctness-labelled fit of a granularity threshold; MMAR-Rubrics (T2: offline-calibration-only, answer-gated). EXPLICITLY EXCLUDED BY THE BLACK-BOX CONTRACT: 2604.14520's Sec 3.2 attention-ratio analysis, which must be replaced by behavioural probes.
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 (per-instance topology oracle + recovery fraction — this is the sub-direction's headline deliverable), G2 (direct-readout floor), G3 (three nulls), G5 (margin honesty — report how often the controller actually departed from the default topology), G6 (harm, with the per-subset losses shown rather than absorbed: 2604.14520's own Ola/WorldSense -1.1 and 3B/AVHBench-A-Hal -4.3 are the pattern to expect), G7 (heterogeneity — per-benchmark and per-subset before any aggregate; 2605.07134 openly reports domain-level regressions inside an improving average and we adopt that reporting), G9 (boundary), G11 (two metric families on the same output). TOKEN PAIRED WITH SEMANTIC: on the audio-visual carriers, MCQ accuracy paired with an equivalence-estimator-scored open-output variant of the same items (the corpus warns that MCQ inflates omni scores by 13-20pp relative to open-ended, per the T2/T3 digest note on options-stripped protocols, so both protocols are run and kept separate); on the speech carriers, WER/CER/MER/NER paired with S2ER, anchored by 2605.29430's CS-Dialogue S2ER 19.73 -> 4.16 against MER 14.44 -> 13.28. SUPPLY-LENGTH REPORTED JOINTLY WITH ACCURACY in every cell (2605.07134's discipline), plus the density sweep as an accuracy-vs-supply-budget curve (2604.14520's frame ablation shape, ported to chunk count / window size / repeat count). PER-BUCKET STRATIFIED DELTAS by acoustic condition, by long-vs-short duration, by entity density and by code-switch presence.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: required both to compute cross-topology agreement and to define the per-instance oracle on open-output protocols. Same construction as sub-directions 1-2 (S2ER prompt with the gold argument replaced by the competing topology's answer, three-round bidirectional voting at majority-3, judge and prompt pinned, validated on a self-carved 120-item split with 5 repeated runs, std, and an expert-comparison column). Extra requirement here: because the same item is answered under k structurally different supply forms, the estimator must be checked for FORM sensitivity — that it does not systematically judge, say, a chunked-supply answer as inequivalent merely because it is phrased differently — reported as an equivalence-rate-by-topology table. (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: the audio-visual MCQ carriers give a deterministic per-instance best-topology oracle OFFLINE ONLY, which is exactly the missing Table-6 row; on the ASR and open-output carriers, the substitute is the equivalence estimator with a declared noise floor (repeated-scoring Kendall's W) plus paired significance, and the substitute's noise floor is reported next to every topology delta so that the many small published deltas in this area (+0.6, +0.8, +0.9 in 2604.14520, all single-run at T=0) cannot be repeated as findings. (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: topology choice is reported and, if a controller is built, keyed per acoustic condition — the falsifiable proposition being that the best supply form is condition-dependent (a chunked/re-segmented supply should pay under reverb and long duration, an ASR-view arm should pay under clean read speech, a repeated-listen arm under low SNR) rather than task-type-dependent as 2604.14520's text-only planner assumes. This directly attacks that paper's own structural weakness — the planner decides 'minimal sufficient' supply WITHOUT LOOKING AT THE SIGNAL and therefore cannot know this clip is noisy — and it is stated as the hypothesis under test, not as an inherited result.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS: supply length in tokens/frames/chunks reported jointly with accuracy in every cell; prefill (TTFT) and per-token generate latency following 2604.14520 Table 8's protocol (100 samples, single GPU), which is also the honest-overhead template — and which shows a supply wrapper can be SLOWER when nothing is pruned (OmniBench per-token 0.682 -> 0.763 s) while being faster when it is (AVHBench prefill 0.282 -> 0.099 s). Count executed-arm decode passes explicitly, since executing k topologies costs k times the direct-readout call budget and any accuracy claim must be made against that. Report auxiliary overhead the controller itself introduces as its own line item (2605.07134's step-scale vs task-scale accounting, with per-component invocation counts). VARIANCE: >= 3 seeds per sampled arm with bootstrap 95% CI; we explicitly do NOT inherit 2604.14520's T=0 fixed-seed single-run protocol, under which its own +0.6/+0.8/+0.9 deltas are inside plausible noise. Exact McNemar with Holm for the MCQ carriers, Wilcoxon for the continuous metrics.
- 击杀条件：(i) If the per-instance topology oracle on our carriers is not clearly above the best-fixed-topology row with CIs, the topology decision has no headroom on that carrier and it is dropped — this is the first measurement to run and the cheapest kill. (ii) If the arm-space enumeration on our deployed core yields fewer than three expressible, genuinely distinct arms, the sub-direction is not runnable on that core and must be re-scoped rather than simulated. (iii) If the 2-call permutation probe alone captures most of the reliability signal, report that and drop the expensive multi-topology execution. (iv) If deltas do not survive the second prompt template, the finding is template-driven and is withdrawn (2604.14520's Table 7 shows template choice dominating topology by tens of points). (v) If per-subset losses (of the Ola/WorldSense -1.1 and 3B/A-Hal -4.3 shape) are not offset by gains within the same bucket, report the wrapper as task-conditioned and refuse the aggregate claim.

**P 槽位**：P1 (evidence-state / knowledge-supply control for speech tasks) primary. Feeds P6 the arm alphabet for the integrated-system charter; imports G1-G11 from P9.

## D1.4 音频外部知识检索的决策效用（audio-RAG retrieval decision utility: hop control, stopping right, contamination and snapshot discipline）

**定义**：For audio tasks whose answer is NOT recoverable from the waveform, the decisions governing external retrieval: whether to search at all, what to query, how many hops to buy, when to stop, when to abstain, and how to admit or refuse retrieved evidence before it enters the core's context. Distinct from sub-direction 1's general pricing because the supply here is exogenous and unbounded (a corpus/web) rather than a re-representation of an in-hand signal, and it carries its own reproducibility and contamination discipline.

**语音域状态**：OCCUPIED as a mechanism, ABSENT as a decision layer (D1 read set + D0 quintet + T1 corpus, cut 2026-07-26). 2602.10656 AudioRAG demotes the frozen omni to a queryable observation source behind a text controller (Qwen3-8B, WebThinker-derived Think-Call-Answer loop over a 2-tool set) and publishes the numbers to beat: Qwen3-Omni-30B raw 37.0 -> agentic 46.2 (+9.2 abs / +24.9% rel); Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 32.2 -> 39.5; comparison points Audio-Flamingo-3 28.8, Audio-Reasoner 20.2, Baichuan-Omni 24.4, Gemini-2.5-Flash 45.0. Which tool, what query, how many hops are ALL decided by free-form reasoning tokens with no score, no confidence, no cost term and no stop rule beyond chain termination; there is no tool ablation (so the +24.9% cannot be attributed between retrieval and decomposition), no hop/latency/search-cost accounting, and no retrieval-quality measurement whatsoever (whether a retrieved page carried the answer is never checked). THE WRAPPER'S OWN DOCUMENTED HARM IS THE OPENING: it reduces reasoning (A), knowledge (C) and audio-processing (B) errors while INCREASING type-D invalid answers, attributed to infinite logical loops — a wrapper-induced regression on the authors' published taxonomy that a stopping right can attack with an existing metric rather than a new one. Adjacent audio: Omni-DeepSearch (T1 2605.08762) shows over-search drift, where budget (15,5) DEGRADES against (10,3) on VIDEO/AMBIENT; AudioToolAgent 2510.02995 reports that web-search tool ablations 'did not reveal consistent improvements'; Omni-Decision 2607.11433 records audio tools at 330 of 4,038 tool requests (8.2%) against 1,901 web_search, i.e. the retrieval channel dominates the audio channel in an omni evidence loop. INFORMATION-BOUNDARY HAZARD ON THE CARRIER ITSELF: AudioRAG's question-validity filter and its error-type judge both receive the gold audio attribute, and 80% of its items derive from public dataset metadata so a live web search can plausibly reach the source dataset page.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED at the admission gate rather than the hop-count level (donor read set, cut 2026-07-26). 2606.03054 ToolGate (vision-GUI/VLM) is the closest occupant of the pre-call decision: gate g(prefix, pending call) in {0,1} executed BEFORE the tool output enters the context, with the decision-theoretic target written down and declared unobservable from logs. Its diagnostic is the transplantable part: across 15,782 labelled invocations, 11.8% helpful, 9.9% harmful, 78.3% unchanged — acquisitions are not free-and-mostly-good, they are mostly-inert with symmetric upside and downside; and on a 802-call verifiable subset, when the tool output is wrong and the pre-call prediction was already correct, the post-call prediction becomes wrong in 13.9% (43/309) of cases. Its controlled adversarial-stimulus experiment collapses visual belief from 0.993 to 0.142 and shows a skeptical prompt recovers only to 0.461 — prompt-level skepticism is NOT a defense, which is the argument for gating BEFORE admission rather than discounting after. Contract-relevant caveats: the gate is a FITTED logistic regression at a tuned tau=0.5, matched-domain +1.65 collapses to +0.32/+0.08 strictly cross-domain, tool identity alone supplies +1.31 of the +1.65 (most of the signal is a static per-tool prior), and an in-domain gate on the largest model HURT accuracy. 2607.04334 contributes the four-arm mitigation comparison with an explicit queries-per-action cost column so a gate cannot win by refusing everything.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED, including the direct parent of the speech carrier. 2504.21776 WebThinker (text) is the framework AudioRAG is built on, and its own gap is instructive: tool invocation is UNPRICED — its preference rule penalizes tool calls only as a lexicographic tie-break after correctness — its document memory is accumulate-everything with top-k similarity retrieval and no write-gate or poisoning defense despite ingesting arbitrary web pages, and its loop ends when the model emits EOS with no calibrated stop and no abstain. 2605.05701 (text, multi-hop QA search) supplies the operational controller: a utility over the action menu {SEARCH, DECOMPOSE, ANSWER} minus a budget-dependent penalty, normalized to value-per-budget, with deterministic guard operators (premature-answer suppression, minimum-search enforcement), under dual hard budgets, plus an explicit harm constraint and a safe-set-gated finalizer; its component ablation identifies the budget-dependent penalty as the largest single contributor, and it reports that larger budgets can introduce redundant search so MIDDLE budgets sometimes beat HIGH. 2602.16699 supplies the closed-form retrieval rule for the simplest case (retrieve iff p_ret*gamma >= p_da on PopQA) and the warning that 'always acquire' is the default failure mode a controller must beat (their non-thinking prompted baseline retrieves 97.7% of the time, cost-insensitively).

**开放增量**：Scoped increment: the STOPPING right and the ABSTAIN right on an audio-RAG loop, measured at equal retrieval cost on the wrapper's own published error taxonomy, under a frozen retrieval snapshot. Three specifics. (a) The stop target is already instrumented by the carrier: type-D invalid answers rise under the wrapper, so a budget-capped stopping rule is scored on an existing class, not a new metric, and its cost is measured against the A/B/C reductions it may give back. (b) The audio-query channel is unmanaged: T_a is called with an arbitrary natural-language query and its answer is trusted, and type-B (audio-processing) error is the residual the wrapper barely improves — deciding how many audio queries, with which framing, and whether to re-query on disagreement sits exactly on top of that residual (and connects to sub-direction 2's branch set). (c) An admission gate before retrieved evidence enters the context, rather than after. Boundary stated rather than assumed: text-RAG's supply is exogenous and unbounded with a discrete re-issuable priced act, whereas the audio-specific question 'what was actually said/heard' has bounded endogenous supply — so the value-of-information calculus differs in kind, and the two channels must be priced separately rather than on one scalar. Reproducibility boundary: any claim built on a live search API needs a pinned retrieval snapshot or the benchmark is not reproducible, and the carrier's 80%-from-metadata construction means contamination must be measured, not assumed away.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY: the AudioRAG 500-item benchmark (T2 row 'AudioRAG 500-item benchmark', local_status needs_download, released at github.com/jingru-lin/AudioRAG with per-sample source provenance; boundary_class eval-only-gold with the explicit note that the correctness filter and error judge see the gold attribute and must not leak into any deployed scorer; per-source licenses mixed across MMAU/CinePile/MNSC/FMA/Jazznet/MusicNet/iNaturalist/CHEER and to be checked before redistribution). A FROZEN RETRIEVAL SNAPSHOT is a hard precondition: we crawl once, hash it, and serve every arm from the same snapshot, because the paid search API is nondeterministic and time-varying. CONTAMINATION SPLIT IS PART OF THE DESIGN, not an afterthought: the 500 items split 80% LLM-generated from public dataset metadata vs 20% human-curated from online video audio chosen for timeliness and anti-contamination, and every result is reported separately on those two strata, since a snapshot that contains the source dataset page turns the retrieval right into a lookup. SECOND (a no-retrieval control family, to separate the retrieval right from the decomposition right): MMAU Test-mini and MMAR (T2, both metadata_only, public), where the answer IS in the waveform, so a controller that still buys retrieval hops there is demonstrably mispriced. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION (named only, not in T2 as a designed carrier): Omni-DeepSearch's released set is in T2 with local_status needs_download and the explicit note that open-web retrieval drifts over time and a snapshot must be fixed — it is a candidate second carrier once the snapshot discipline is proven on AudioRAG.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3; carrier anchors 32.2 raw / 39.5 agentic) as the LOAD-BEARING audio tool for the primary AudioRAG run【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】. Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B (T3; the carrier's own raw anchor is 37.0), served through our local llama.cpp GGUF lane (T3, -ngl 28 llama-server + input_audio), is kept for the matched-backbone confirmation cell against the paper's 4xA100 audio-tool serving ONLY and is never load-bearing, with the WEAK-CORE CAVEAT stated: per T2/T3 hazard 4 no lift measured on this lane may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction, and the T1/T2 note that two circulating numbers exist for the 30B Thinking model (66.4 vs 69.9 on MMAR) plus a distinct Flash-Thinking variant means every baseline on that lane is re-run locally rather than quoted. Text controller: an open-weight Qwen3-8B-class model to match the carrier's configuration, with a second heterogeneous controller family run as the self-evaluation-contamination control. Judge: the carrier uses GPT-4o (T3, proprietary API with snapshot-drift risk) — we pin a snapshot string and additionally report a second heterogeneous judge, because the carrier's judge is unvalidated against human agreement in the dossiers.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS. (1) DIRECT READOUT: frozen omni answers from the audio alone, standardized prompt. Anchors from 2602.10656 Table 2 (3-run mean, GPT-4o judge): Qwen3-Omni-30B 37.0, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 32.2, Audio-Flamingo-3 28.8, Audio-Reasoner 20.2 (anchor only, no T3 row【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】), Baichuan-Omni 24.4, Gemini-2.5-Flash 45.0. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: random hop-count / random stop within the same expected retrieval budget. (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: majority/MBR over answers produced from k independently retrieved evidence sets, with no pricing and no gate — this row also doubles as the 'multiple search results are never aggregated or cross-checked' gap the carrier leaves open. (4) ORACLE: per-instance best hop-count and best stopping point over the executed budget ladder; T1 records oracle_if_published NONE for this carrier, so we produce it. PUBLISHED WRAPPER ANCHOR TO BEAT AT EQUAL RETRIEVAL COST: agentic Qwen3-Omni + Qwen3-8B 46.2, and agentic Qwen2.5-Omni + Qwen3-8B 39.5. TWO ADDITIONAL CONTROLS THE CARRIER ITSELF LACKS AND WHICH ARE MANDATORY HERE: (a) TOOL ABLATION — search-only, audio-only, both — since without it the +24.9% cannot be attributed between external retrieval and question decomposition; (b) ALWAYS-RETRIEVE and NEVER-RETRIEVE corners, because 'always acquire' is the documented default failure mode of prompted controllers (2602.16699 reports 97.7% retrieval rate for its cost-insensitive prompted baseline) and any pricing claim must beat both corners. PROMPT-LEVEL NULLS from 2606.03054 Table 3 in shape: a self-report arm that simply asks the controller whether a hop is worthwhile, and a sparse-prompt arm.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL: hop-level consensus/margin across independently retrieved evidence sets over the estimated equivalence relation; discrete semantic entropy in counts-only form (T3 legal; probability-mass/predictive/length-normalized variants ILLEGAL); vote-rate P(True) (legal in vote-rate form, with the 2604.25591 caveat that it collapses to chance or below on weaker cores and harder benchmarks); MBR/chrF (0 model calls); S2ER hypothesis-vs-hypothesis as a margin arbiter over competing answers derived from different evidence sets; the type-D detector itself (a format/termination check requiring no gold). FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION on a self-carved split: the carrier's own question-validity filter and answer-correctness filter, and its GPT-4o error-type judge — all three receive the gold audio attribute per its Tables 4-5 and NONE may inform a deployed scorer; the per-instance oracle hop-count; any correctness-labelled fit of a stop threshold; the forced-answer-probe labels if we build a supply-effect instrument on this carrier (see sub-direction 5). RETRIEVAL-SIDE HYGIENE AS A LEGALITY ITEM, not merely reproducibility: a snapshot containing the source dataset page is an information-boundary leak on the 80% metadata-derived stratum, so snapshot composition is audited and the leak rate is reported per stratum. MECHANICAL ISOLATION per G9: gold-bearing files opened only by the offline calibration script behind a logged hook (2607.13347's design, the strictest instance in the corpus).
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 (per-instance oracle over the hop/stop ladder + recovery fraction), G2 (direct-readout floor — 37.0 raw must appear as a first-class policy row, and a wrapper that fails to beat it is a loss not a wash), G3 (three nulls), G5 (margin honesty — report how many hops the stopping rule actually cut and how many items it abstained on), G6 (harm — the up-mass/down-mass and worsen/improve share over the A/B/C/D taxonomy, with exact McNemar; the whole point is that the published wrapper trades A/C reductions for a D increase and we must show our stop does not simply re-trade in the other direction), G7 (heterogeneity — the 80%/20% contamination strata are a mandatory decomposition axis, plus per-audio-type buckets), G9 (boundary, including the retrieval-snapshot audit), G11 (metric honesty). METRICS: judge accuracy paired with the A/B/C/D error taxonomy used as a per-decision-right diagnostic (B tracks the audio-supply right, C the retrieval right, D the stopping right — the taxonomy is reusable exactly because it is decision-right-aligned), plus an open-output arm scored by the equivalence estimator so the claim does not rest on a single closed-form judge. PER-BUCKET STRATIFIED DELTAS: contamination stratum (metadata-derived vs human-curated), audio type (sound / music / speech), hop depth, and estimated acoustic condition of the audio context.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: the carrier is judged by an LLM against a gold short answer, so the DEPLOYED consensus/margin signal cannot use that judge — the estimator is the S2ER-style hypothesis-vs-hypothesis construction (three-round bidirectional voting at majority-3, pinned judge and prompt, validated on a self-carved split with 5 repeated runs, std, and an expert-comparison column) applied to answers produced from different evidence sets. Its output is what makes 'these two hops agree' meaningful; its tie/merge error is reported and propagated. (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: the 500-item answer key gives a deterministic oracle OFFLINE ONLY — used to fill the per-instance hop/stop oracle row, to fit any calibrator, and to measure the estimator's agreement with the key on this carrier (which is itself a useful validity datum).
