For the open-output arm the substitute is the estimator with a declared noise floor from repeated-scoring reproducibility plus paired significance. Gold and the gold-conditioned filters never touch the runtime policy. (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the retrieval decision is keyed on the estimated acoustic condition of the audio context as well as on the question, on the explicit hypothesis that a degraded audio context should shift spend toward the AUDIO channel (re-query, re-listen, operator) and away from the retrieval channel, because a retrieval hop cannot recover an unheard entity. This is the sub-direction's sharpest condition-keying claim and it is falsifiable: if hop-count utility is flat across acoustic-condition buckets, the keying is unnecessary and we say so.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS: retrieval hops, snapshot bytes served, audio-tool decode passes, text-controller tokens, judge calls, wall-clock, and dollars — reported separately, never merged. The carrier reports NONE of these (T1: cost_axis 4xA100 per component, per-call latency NONE_REPORTED), so cost accounting is itself a deliverable. Adopt the hard dual-budget audit (any item exceeding either the hop cap or the token cap counts FAILED) over a 4-level budget ladder, reported as per-cell win counts with an explicit refusal of a dominance claim; the donor's own finding that MIDDLE budgets sometimes beat HIGH, and the audio-domain over-search-drift instance where budget (15,5) degrades against (10,3), are the reasons the ladder is not optional. VARIANCE: >= 3 runs (matching the carrier's 3-run averaging) plus bootstrap 95% CI, which the carrier does not report at n=500; exact McNemar with Holm on the paired A/B/C/D transitions. Snapshot hash and controller/judge model strings recorded per run so a number can be re-derived.
- 击杀条件：(i) If, at equal retrieval cost, a priced stop/abstain policy does not reduce type-D invalid answers without giving back the A/C reductions, with CIs, the stopping claim is dead. (ii) If results on the 80% metadata-derived stratum diverge sharply from the 20% human-curated stratum and the snapshot audit shows source-page reachability, the carrier's headline is a contamination artifact and the carrier is demoted to the 20% stratum only. (iii) If the tool ablation shows the published +24.9% is dominated by decomposition rather than retrieval, the retrieval decision right has little to control and the sub-direction re-scopes to the audio-query channel (sub-direction 2). (iv) If always-retrieve is not beaten at equal or lower cost, the pricing claim is withdrawn. (v) If a live-snapshot dependency cannot be frozen and hashed, the carrier is not reproducible and is dropped rather than reported with caveats.

**P 槽位**：P2 (audio-RAG / retrieval decision utility; AudioRAG and Omni-DeepSearch are the named carriers for that slot). Feeds P1 the exogenous-supply half of the pricing menu and P9 the admission-gate calibration question.

## D1.5 供给效用判别器（read-out vs new-info discrimination — supply evaluated as supply）

**定义**：The instrument, and then the runtime signal, that answers: did this acquisition change the answer, and in which direction? Separating (a) read-out — the core already knew, the supply was inert — from (b) genuine new information, and separating helpful from harmful supply. This is simultaneously the missing EVALUATION in the dimension and the natural place to attach a scalar, which is why it is a sub-direction of its own rather than a metric appendix to the others.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT (D1 read set, 6 papers, cut 2026-07-26). No paper measures whether an acquired observation changed the answer. 2603.02206 VoiceAgentRAG is the cautionary extreme: an entire architecture optimized for hit rate and latency with NO answer-quality metric anywhere — its own precision claims in the threshold sweep (tau 0.30 gives 90% hits with 'low precision') are asserted and never measured against answers, and its 75% headline conflates predictive prefetching with the Fast Talker's cache-on-miss and the Slow Thinker's direct-retrieval caching with no ablation isolating the predictor. 2602.10656 AudioRAG measures only final accuracy with no tool ablation, so its +24.9% cannot be attributed between channels, and retrieval quality is never checked. 2509.21749's operator leave-one-out (full 48.97; w/o denoise 33.17; w/o enhancement 41.77; w/o normalization 45.57; w/o analysis 47.23) is the closest proxy in the read set and is coarse and set-level rather than per-instance. PARTIAL in adjacent audio (T1 corpus, same cut): 2510.11454 Audio-Maestro publishes the nearest speech-side instrument — an Improved/Degraded 2x2 over tool-invoked answer changes showing 41.6-43.5% of changes go the WRONG direction (Gemini 10.75 improved vs 7.65 degraded; DeSTA 43.0%; GPT-4o 43.5%) — but it is an offline post-hoc diagnostic that never feeds a decision, and its failure taxonomy is dominated by Tool Output Error (73.3-90.0%) over Incorrect Tool Selection (6.7-16.7%). 2606.09925 AudioProcessBench shows the compression that makes this measurement necessary: a 51.5-point step-verification spread compresses into only a 9.5-point chain-selection spread, with rank inversions, i.e. how well a signal verifies is NOT how much it buys you.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED, and this is the single most transplantable donor device in the whole D1 mapping — the same 2606.03054 four-way transition classification is also cited by D3.1's counterfactual tool-credit arbitration; the split is stated in 开放增量 below so the instrument is not double-designed.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 2606.03054 ToolGate (vision-GUI/VLM) contributes the FORCED-ANSWER PROBE: query the frozen core for its current best answer immediately before and immediately after an acquisition and classify the transition four ways — helpful (wrong->right), harmful (right->wrong), unchanged-correct, unchanged-wrong — converting 'did this observation help' into an observable transition without a counterfactual rollout. Base rates over 15,782 labelled invocations: 11.8% helpful, 9.9% harmful, 78.3% unchanged. The authors are explicit that probes are used ONLY for offline label construction and diagnostics, never in the deployed loop — a discipline we adopt verbatim. Two further reusable instruments: the gate-vs-confidence DECORRELATION check (Pearson -0.19, Spearman -0.22 between the gate score and the pre-call probability on the gold answer, i.e. the signal is not confidence in disguise), and the feature ablation isolating how much of a controller's value is a static prior (tool identity alone gives +1.31 of +1.65). 2605.01345 Appendix G.1 adds the EVIDENCE-ONLY ABLATION: hold the observation sequence fixed and delete the intermediate reasoning trace (66.0% -> 48.0%), attributing the gap to integration rather than acquisition — a clean way to separate what was observed from what was carried. 2605.18652 Table 4 supplies the three-arm control (Random context / single-stage retrieval / two-stage retrieval) that isolates 'more context' from 'right context', with the Random arm as the honest null.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED. 2604.04373 (text) supplies the paired context-on/context-off improvement statistic delta-avg-m(x) as a per-query attribution measure, the formal construct that value-of-context is reduction in output uncertainty with an explicit sign convention where NEGATIVE gain flags misleading context, the finding that retrieval relevance rises monotonically while task performance is non-monotonic (falsifying 'more relevant is better'), and the random-context control arm as the essential null. Its validation estimator uses token-level logprobs, which our contract does not guarantee — the construct transfers, the estimator does not. 2506.11930 Feedback Friction (text) supplies the ceiling framing: how much externally supplied information a frozen model can actually incorporate is itself bounded and measurable. 2607.03436 (text) decomposes a router-to-oracle gap into what is real and what is measurement, which is the honest-headroom accounting device this instrument needs.

**开放增量**：The scoped increment is a SPEECH-LEGAL supply-effect instrument and its label-free deployable proxy. SPLIT WITH D3.1 (undeclared duplicate otherwise, since both cite the same 2606.03054 four-way classification and the same 2510.11454 Improved/Degraded 2x2): D1.5 owns the probe over SUPPLY ACTS — operator, re-decode, re-listen, retrieval hop; D3.1 owns it over TOOL INVOCATIONS and the direction-of-change arbitration for tool-produced evidence; the shared probe-corpus construction (forced pre/post answer under the estimated equivalence relation) is build-once infrastructure so the cost is not double-counted across the two sub-directions.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 Three parts. (a) THE INSTRUMENT: a forced-answer-probe analogue for a frozen speech/omni core, where 'flip' and 'unchanged' are defined over an ESTIMATED answer-equivalence relation rather than an argmax over a closed option set — the donor's probe presupposes a small option set so that 'unchanged' is well defined by construction, and free-form speech output has no such relation; the estimator's error must therefore be carried into the base rates rather than ignored. (b) THE COST INVERSION: for speech, running the probe means extra decode passes over a waveform, so the supervision pipeline that made the vision gate cheap is expensive here — which means the instrument is an OFFLINE calibration device and the deployed signal must be a cheaper label-free proxy (consensus/margin, cross-branch agreement, transcript stability), with the proxy validated against the instrument on a self-carved split. (c) THE PAYOFF STRUCTURE THIS EXPOSES: the donor base rates say acquisitions are mostly-inert with symmetric upside and downside, and the speech-side Improved/Degraded 2x2 says 41.6-43.5% of tool-induced answer changes go the wrong way. If those shapes hold on our carriers, the read-out/new-info discriminator is worth more than any additional supply channel, because the dominant cost is paying for inert supply and the dominant risk is harmful supply. Stated as the hypothesis under test, not as a transferred conclusion.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY: MELD-Hard1k reconstruction (T2 must-be-reconstructed from 2509.21749 Table 3) with the operator menu as the acquisition set — each operator application is one probed acquisition, giving a dense, cheap, condition-stratified instrument population under controlled perturbations. SECOND: AudioRAG 500-item set (T2 needs_download) with each retrieval hop and each audio query as a probed acquisition, and a frozen snapshot. THIRD: GigaSpeech Test and CS-Dialogue Test-dagger (T2 metadata_only / needs_download) with each evidence turn or re-decode as a probed acquisition — this carrier is where the token-vs-semantic divergence is sharpest and where the instrument's semantic definition is most load-bearing. FOURTH (a closed-option carrier retained deliberately so the estimator can be validated against a construct that does not need it): MMAU Test-mini and Music-AVQA (T2 metadata_only, public), where the forced-answer probe can be run in its ORIGINAL closed-option form and the estimator-based form can be compared against it — that comparison is how we quantify what the estimator costs us. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION: none required.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and -3B (T3; the probe requires only re-prompting for a current best answer, which is contract-legal, but it is a decode pass per probe so core cost dominates the design). Audio-Flamingo-3-7B as a heterogeneous second core, since the donor's own cross-domain collapse (+1.65 matched to +0.32 strictly cross-domain) warns that a gate's value is regime-dependent even within one domain and must be re-measured per core. Qwen3-ASR-1.7B (T3) for the ASR carrier. LOCAL LANE: llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B GGUF (T3) is CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY — here it is genuinely useful as a probe-volume lane because probes are many and individually cheap, but the WEAK-CORE CAVEAT binds (T2/T3 hazard 4): base rates measured on the weak lane may not hold on a strong core, and no base rate measured there may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction. Base rates are therefore reported per core, never pooled.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS, defined here at the level of the SUPPLY POLICY the instrument is used to score. (1) DIRECT READOUT: no acquisition at all — this row IS the read-out hypothesis, and the fraction of items on which it already matches the post-acquisition answer is the headline quantity of the sub-direction. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: acquire a randomly chosen supply act. (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: acquire everything and aggregate with unweighted majority/MBR — the 'more context' arm, whose comparison against a selective arm at matched cost is the 'more context vs right context' separation borrowed in shape from 2605.18652 Table 4. (4) ORACLE: acquire only where the instrument says the acquisition was helpful — the per-instance ceiling of a perfect discriminator, which is the row that tells us whether building the discriminator is worth anything at all. RANDOM-CONTEXT / IRRELEVANT-SUPPLY NULL borrowed from 2604.04373: supply a deliberately unrelated observation and confirm the instrument scores it as inert-or-harmful; if it does not, the instrument is measuring novelty of phrasing, not information. PUBLISHED ANCHORS quoted with domain labels and NOT inherited as effect sizes: donor base rates 11.8/9.9/78.3 (vision-GUI, 2606.03054) and 13.9% over-trust (43/309); speech-side 2510.11454 Improved/Degraded 2x2 with 41.6-43.5% wrong-direction share and the TOE-dominated failure taxonomy; 2509.21749's operator leave-one-out as the coarse set-level comparator our per-instance instrument should subsume.
- 信号与合法性：THE INSTRUMENT ITSELF IS GOLD-CONSUMING AND IS FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION ON A SELF-CARVED SPLIT. Specifically: the four-way transition labels require knowing whether the pre- and post-acquisition answers were correct, which needs the answer key; therefore the probe-labelled corpus is built once, offline, on a carved split, behind a logged file-open hook (G9, following 2607.13347's mechanically-blocked design, recorded in T1 as the strictest instance in the corpus), and it never runs in the deployed loop — exactly as the donor does it. DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL PROXIES that the instrument is used to VALIDATE, and which are what actually ships: pre-vs-post answer CHANGE detected by the equivalence estimator (change is observable without gold; only its direction needs gold), cross-branch/cross-decode consensus and margin, discrete semantic entropy in counts-only form (T3 legal; probability-mass and predictive variants ILLEGAL), MBR/chrF (0 calls), transcript/answer stability across turns, and S2ER hypothesis-vs-hypothesis. MANDATORY DECORRELATION CHECK borrowed from the donor: report Pearson/Spearman between the deployed proxy and the core's own verbalized confidence, to show the proxy is not confidence in disguise (donor reference: -0.19 / -0.22). MANDATORY SELF-BIAS CHECK per G10: if the estimator shares a model family with the core, measure SelfGap (2606.09925 records -24.6 and -25.2 for the strongest critics that are also generators) and run a heterogeneous-family estimator as a control.
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 (the oracle-discriminator row IS the headroom row for this sub-direction, with recovery fraction for the deployable proxy), G3 (nulls, including the random-context arm), G5 (margin honesty — report the actual number of acquisitions skipped and admitted by any gate built on the proxy), G6 (harm — the harmful-transition share is a first-class reported quantity, not a footnote, with exact McNemar on paired transitions), G7 (heterogeneity by acoustic condition and by supply channel), G9 (the strictest instance in the design: the probe corpus is mechanically firewalled), G10 (self-bias), G11 (metric honesty). ADDITIONAL GATE SPECIFIC TO THIS SUB-DIRECTION, inherited from 2606.09925: report the compression from VERIFICATION quality to DECISION utility explicitly — a 51.5-point step-verification spread compressing into a 9.5-point selection spread means a proxy that discriminates well may buy almost nothing, and the two axes must be reported side by side rather than one standing in for the other. TOKEN PAIRED WITH SEMANTIC throughout: on the ASR carrier, WER/CER/MER deltas alongside S2ER deltas for the SAME transitions, which is where the instrument earns its keep — 2605.29430's CS-Dialogue row (S2ER 19.73 -> 4.16 while MER 14.44 -> 13.28) shows a token metric would classify most of those transitions as 'unchanged'. PER-BUCKET: acoustic condition, supply channel (operator vs re-decode vs retrieval vs turn), and transition type.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: here it is not a convenience but the definition of the construct — 'unchanged' cannot be stated without it, and the donor's probe is well defined only because it operates over a closed option set. Same construction (S2ER-style, gold argument replaced by the competing answer, three-round bidirectional voting at majority-3, judge and prompt pinned, validated on a self-carved 120-item split with 5 repeated runs, std, and an expert-comparison column). UNIQUE REQUIREMENT: on the closed-option carrier (MMAU Test-mini, Music-AVQA) run BOTH the exact-match transition classification and the estimator-based one, and report the disagreement matrix — that number is the price the estimator charges, and every free-form base rate is reported with it attached. (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: closed-option carriers supply the deterministic transition oracle offline; on free-form carriers the substitute is the estimator plus a declared noise floor from repeated-scoring reproducibility (Kendall's W) and paired significance, with the explicit rule that any base-rate difference smaller than the noise floor is reported as indistinguishable. The oracle-discriminator policy row is computed offline and labelled a ceiling, never an effect — this is precisely the failure the Interactive/Agentic ASR pair models for us, where a gold-conditioned trajectory is reported as a result once the upper-bound framing is dropped between versions. (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: base rates (helpful / harmful / unchanged) are reported PER ACOUSTIC CONDITION BUCKET and never pooled, because a false-positive rate measured on clean audio does not constrain noisy audio; and the deployable proxy's threshold is keyed on the estimated condition. The falsifiable proposition is that the helpful share rises and the inert share falls as conditions degrade — which, if true, means the discriminator's value is concentrated exactly where a global always-acquire policy wastes the least, and the controller must be evaluated on that skew rather than on a pooled average.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS: the instrument's own cost is a first-class reported quantity, because it inverts the donor's economics — each probe is a decode pass over audio, so building an N-invocation labelled corpus costs N extra core calls, and the design reports (a) probe corpus construction cost, (b) deployed proxy cost per item, and (c) the acquisition cost the proxy saves, so the gate must pay for itself on axis (c) net of (b). Report frozen-core decode passes, estimator/judge calls, wall-clock and dollars. VARIANCE: base rates reported with binomial CIs at the stated corpus size (the donor's 15,782 invocations set the scale we should aim at on the cheap carrier and cannot reach on the expensive ones — so per-carrier corpus size is stated and CIs are computed, never assumed); >= 3 seeds for sampled arms; bootstrap 95% CI on all paired deltas; exact McNemar with Holm on transition contingency tables. Repeated-scoring reproducibility of the estimator (Kendall's W) is measured and reported once per carrier and per core.
- 击杀条件：(i) If the oracle-discriminator row is not meaningfully above the always-acquire row at matched cost, a perfect discriminator buys nothing on that carrier and the carrier is dropped — this is the cheapest and most decisive kill in the whole D1 taxonomy and should be run first. (ii) If the estimator-based transition classification disagrees with the exact-match classification on the closed-option carrier beyond the G4 tie-rate reference band, the construct is not measurable on free-form output yet and the estimator must be fixed before any base rate is published. (iii) If the deployable proxy's correlation with the instrument is at or below the random-context null, there is no label-free proxy and the sub-direction delivers an offline instrument only — which is still a deliverable, but must be reported as such rather than dressed as a controller. (iv) If the proxy is statistically indistinguishable from verbalized confidence (decorrelation check fails), it is confidence in disguise and the novelty-free framing is stated plainly. (v) If probe cost exceeds the acquisition cost it saves on every carrier, the instrument stays offline permanently and the direction re-scopes to evaluation methodology.

**P 槽位**：P1 (evidence-state / knowledge-supply control) primary — this is the instrument that makes every other D1 pricing claim measurable. Secondary: P9 (decision-calibration / admissibility gates), which owns the calibration contract for the deployable proxy, and P10, which needs a supply-effect baseline before selection-pressure degradation can be attributed.

## D1.6 预期式与跨轮供给调度（anticipatory supply scheduling under a hard latency budget）

**定义**：Deciding to pay for supply BEFORE it is needed, and scheduling that payment against a real-time budget: predictive prefetch of evidence for a turn that has not arrived, asynchronous acquisition overlapped with generation, and the accept/fallback decision on pre-acquired evidence. D1 owns the DECISION to anticipate and its pricing; D2/P3 owns what persists across instances and how it is keyed; P7 owns the interactive/full-duplex validation carrier that consumes the latency accounting produced here.

**语音域状态**：PARTIAL and thin — one instance in the D1 read set (cut 2026-07-26), and it is not an acoustic one. 2603.02206 VoiceAgentRAG builds the anticipatory architecture: a Slow Thinker that on each user utterance directly retrieves and caches, then predicts n=3-5 likely follow-up topics with an LLM (prompted to emit document-style descriptions so embeddings land nearer real document content) and prefetches them asynchronously; a Fast Talker that embeds the query, hits an in-memory FAISS semantic cache at cosine >= tau, and on miss falls back to the vector store, caches the result and raises a priority-retrieval event. Numbers: retrieval latency 110.4ms -> 0.35ms (316x), hit rate 75% overall (150/200) and 79% warm, 110.1ms saved per hit, 16,508ms total; per-scenario hit rates range 95% (feature comparison) down to 45% (upgrade comparison) and 55% (mixed rapid-fire); warm-up curve 58% (turns 1-4) -> 86% (turns 5-9); threshold sweep tau 0.55/0.50/0.45/0.40/0.35/0.30 -> 15/30/55/75/82/90% hits. CRITICAL DOMAIN LABEL: the supply mechanism is TEXT-ONLY — STT feeds text into an ordinary text RAG pipeline, with no audio understanding, no acoustic evidence and no re-listening — so it is a speech-SYSTEM instance, not an audio-supply one, and T1 classifies it accordingly. WHAT IS UNEXERCISED: what to prefetch is LLM next-topic likelihood with no utility, no cost term and no reward; how much is constants (n=3-5, top-k, 0.5s interval); whether to trust the cache is one global scalar tau tuned offline; the TopicShift event exists on the bus and NO policy consumes it; historical pre-warming across conversations is proposed in the paper's own Sec 5.2 and not implemented, with the cache dying at TTL 300s. AND THE STANDING WARNING: there is NO answer-quality metric anywhere in the system — T1 records signal_legality as deployment-legal but the system has only latency and hit rate — which makes it the dimension's live example of optimizing a proxy that is never tied back to answers.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED in the gated-retrieval and warm-start forms (donor read set, cut 2026-07-26). 2605.18652 MementoGUI (vision-GUI, TRAINED — donor for design and measurement only) makes retrieval itself gated and priced: episodic context is REFRESHED ONLY when a learned trigger flag fires, otherwise the previous context is carried forward, with a two-stage coarse-retrieve-then-filter design and an explicit mixing weight over visual and goal-text similarity; its Table 4 three-arm control (Random episodic context / single-stage / two-stage) is the honest null structure for any prefetch claim. Its own residual, stated at direction level: the trigger is binary and learned, and the value-of-retrieval question — is refreshing context worth its context cost here — is never priced against a budget. 2606.17929 PreAct (vision-GUI) is the anticipation-on-repeat donor: agents that get faster on repeated tasks, i.e. anticipatory reuse with a write-gate. 2605.03231 cotomi Act supplies the inverse discipline — withhold the observation until requested (lazy observation, 471s -> 184s at comparable accuracy) — and, more usefully, the finding that current-observation and history axes PARTIALLY SUBSTITUTE (combined +13.8pp against a sum of individual +22.1pp), which is a direct warning that prefetched context and in-turn acquisition are not additive.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED at the read-gate level. 2601.03192 MemRL (text) supplies utility-gated RETRIEVAL with a triplet schema and calibration/forgetting instrumentation. 2604.27283 RSCB-MC (text, LLM coding agents) supplies the risk-sensitive read-gate whose shape is directly instantiable: asymmetric rewards making a false-positive injection strictly worse than a missed reuse, abstention as a first-class ACTION, and false-positive-injection rate reported as its own metric — with the ablation that removing the abstention action raises false positives from 0.0% to 17.5% and collapses cumulative reward, proving the gate is functional rather than cosmetic, plus a context-budget table showing no-memory dominates injection once FP influence and token cost are charged. 2605.05701 supplies the dual-hard-budget accounting and the observation that its controller can be latency-NEGATIVE (mean latency 20.91s -> 15.23s, -27.2%, by shortening trajectories), which is the shape of claim anticipatory supply should be making.

**开放增量**：Scoped increment, deliberately narrow because this is the thinnest speech surface. (a) TIE ANTICIPATORY SUPPLY TO AN ANSWER-QUALITY AXIS. The carrier architecture optimizes hit rate and never checks whether cache-served context yields the same answer as fresh retrieval — that check IS the read-out/new-info discriminator of sub-direction 5 applied to the anticipation channel, and it is simultaneously the missing evaluation and the natural scalar. (b) MAKE THE ACCEPT THRESHOLD A DECIDED, CONDITION-KEYED VARIABLE rather than one global constant: the carrier's own sweep shows the entire precision/recall trade living on tau, and its calibration finding — that query-to-document cosine runs systematically LOWER than query-to-query, so thresholds calibrated on query-query matching are far too strict — is a concrete, reusable trap for any similarity gate we build. (c) PRICE THE PREFETCH ITSELF: n and top-k and the rate limit are constants, and the carrier's own worst scenarios (45% and 55% hit rate) are exactly the wasted-spend regime, while the unconsumed TopicShift event is the signal a spend-or-skip policy would consume. BOUNDARY, stated: this sub-direction inherits the carrier's regime conditions — the mechanism is moot when the store is local and fast (their own local FAISS over 76 docs takes 0.1ms), when conversations are topically incoherent, and when inter-turn time is under 2s — so the FIRST deliverable is a regime measurement on our own carrier, in the shape of 2605.07134's design-question-first discipline (measure the environment property that would justify the mechanism before proposing it), not a mechanism.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY (multi-turn speech with real acoustics, which the original carrier lacks): the interactive-simulation harness of 2604.09121/2605.29430 run over GigaSpeech Test (T2 metadata_only, public/standard) and AISHELL-NER Dev/Test-dagger (T2 needs_download, reconstructible from the entity-bearing filter rule, sizes unpublished so we re-derive) — the harness itself (ASR -> judge -> LLM correction generator -> voice-cloned TTS with the ORIGINAL utterance as acoustic prompt to hold speaker timbre constant across turns) is reusable scaffolding PROVIDED the gold-conditioned stopper and the gold-conditioned evidence generator are replaced with deployment-legal ones and both are reported. AISHELL-NER is the right entity-intensive carrier here because the anticipation payoff should concentrate on named entities, which is also where the carrier papers' own gains are largest and where cross-utterance carry-over is currently zero by construction. SECOND (systems substrate only, not a research baseline; NOT IN T2 — "VoiceAgentRAG" and its 200-query/10-scenario/20-turn benchmark with the synthetic 12-doc/76-chunk KB appear in neither T1, T2 nor T3, and no repo URL is verified at source, so this is named only as NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION, matching the D1.2/D5.6 convention, and is not designed on until acquired and its licence verified): a candidate second carrier for latency and budget ACCOUNTING, explicitly not as a baseline, since the 2603.02206 dossier records the underlying architecture as having no answer-quality metric at all.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION (named only, not in T2 as a usable carrier): the Salesforce full-duplex conversation validation set is recorded in T2 with local_status not_released / internal, so it is unavailable and no design depends on it; a real multi-turn speech corpus with per-turn latency traces is the acquisition to propose.
- 冻结核：Qwen3-ASR-1.7B front-end plus an open-weight corrector/controller (T3; the carriers' anchors are Loop-0 GigaSpeech S2ER 21.47 / WER 11.92 and AISHELL-NER Test S2ER 19.91 / NER 2.45, with the backbone sensitivity band Whisper 6.82 / Qwen3-ASR-1.7B 2.02 / FireRedASR2-LLM-8.3B 0.55 telling us how much of any gain is front-end quality). IndexTTS-1.5 (T3, tool-only) for vocalizing simulated turns with reference-audio acoustic prompting. Controller-scale sensitivity is a designed axis, not an afterthought, because T3/T1 record the exact price: 32B -> 8B costs 2.11-4.07 S2ER points at Loop 10 on every dataset, and the weaker corrector actively INCREASES WER/MER on some benchmarks — so an anticipation policy must be tested at both scales. LOCAL LANE: llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B GGUF (T3) is CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY with the weak-core caveat stated (T2/T3 hazard 4); for this sub-direction it is additionally useful because the carrier's own scope condition is that the latency win only materializes with fast local inference, so a local lane is the regime where the mechanism is even testable — but no lift measured there is extrapolated to a strong core.
- 基线阶梯：FOUR FIXED ROWS. (1) DIRECT READOUT: no anticipation — acquire only when the turn arrives, standardized prompt. Anchors: Loop-0 rows above; and, for the accounting substrate, the carrier's own 110.4ms baseline retrieval latency. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: prefetch n randomly chosen items at the same expected prefetch spend — the null that separates 'anticipating the right thing' from 'having anything warm at all' (this is exactly the ablation the original carrier lacks, since its 75% headline conflates the LLM predictor with cache-on-miss and with direct-retrieval caching). (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY / ALWAYS-PREFETCH: prefetch everything within budget, no policy — the 'more supply' arm. (4) ORACLE: prefetch exactly what the next turn will need, computed offline — the ceiling that tells us what a perfect predictor is worth, which no paper in either read set reports. MANDATORY EXTRA CONTROL, borrowed in shape from 2605.18652 Table 4: a RANDOM-PREFETCHED-CONTEXT arm, to show the gain is right-context and not more-context. MANDATORY REGIME MEASUREMENT BEFORE ANY MECHANISM (2605.07134's design-question-first discipline): measure on our own carrier the two properties that would justify anticipation — turn-to-turn topical coherence, and the fraction of turns whose evidence need is predictable from the preceding window — and report them against a random baseline, exactly as the donor reports LCA-depth 0.48 vs random 0.22 and 52.9% zero-change. If those properties are absent on speech carriers, the mechanism is not justified and we say so. PUBLISHED ANCHOR TO BE CITED WITH ITS DOMAIN LABEL AND NOT INHERITED: 316x retrieval speedup and 75% hit rate (text-only supply, no acoustic evidence, no answer-quality axis).
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL: cache-vs-fresh answer equivalence measured by the estimator (this is the answer-quality axis the carrier lacks, and it needs no gold — it compares two hypotheses); similarity margin over the runner-up as a per-query accept gate instead of one global tau; cross-turn transcript/answer stability; discrete semantic entropy in counts-only form (T3 legal; probability-mass and predictive variants ILLEGAL); MBR/chrF; S2ER hypothesis-vs-hypothesis. FENCED TO OFFLINE ONE-TIME CALIBRATION on a self-carved split: the prefetch oracle (what the next turn actually needed); the isotonic or any labelled calibration of the accept threshold; and — this is the sharpest fence in the sub-direction — the harness's GOLD-CONDITIONED stopper and gold-authored correction generator, which T1 classifies as EVAL-ONLY-GOLD for both carrier papers, meaning the entire published trajectory is an oracle ceiling and not a deployable gain. If we reuse that scaffolding we must replace both and report the loss relative to the published ceiling, because reusing it unchanged silently imports a gold-conditioned gain. CARRIED CALIBRATION TRAP: the carrier's own measurement that query-to-document cosine (0.30-0.55) runs systematically lower than query-to-query similarity means any threshold calibrated on query-query matching is far too strict — this applies directly to any similarity gate we build and is recorded as a design constraint, not a finding.
- 度量与门：BINDING GATES: G1 (prefetch oracle + recovery fraction), G2 (no-anticipation floor), G3 (three nulls, with the random-prefetch arm mandatory), G5 (margin honesty — report how many prefetches were actually consumed versus wasted, which is the quantity the carrier never reports), G6 (harm — stale or wrong prefetched context that changes a correct answer to a wrong one is the harm channel here, and it is measured with the sub-direction-5 instrument, not assumed absent; the carrier's own TTL-based eventual consistency is the mechanism that produces it), G7 (heterogeneity — per-scenario decomposition is mandatory, since the carrier's own range is 45% to 95% by scenario and its warm-up curve is 58% -> 86% by turn depth), G9 (boundary — the gold-conditioned stopper and simulator are the exact hazard), G11. ANSWER-QUALITY AXIS IS MANDATORY AND IS THE POINT: every latency or hit-rate number is reported alongside a paired answer-quality number on the same items, because the carrier is the dimension's cautionary case of a proxy optimized to completion with answers never scored. TOKEN PAIRED WITH SEMANTIC: WER/CER/MER/NER alongside S2ER per turn, with round-wise reporting. PER-BUCKET STRATIFIED DELTAS: acoustic condition, turn depth (the warm-up regime), topical-coherence bucket, and entity-density bucket.
- 三件设计件：(1) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: it IS the missing answer-quality axis here — 'did cache-served context yield the same answer as fresh retrieval' is a hypothesis-vs-hypothesis equivalence question requiring no gold, and it converts the carrier's unmeasured precision claim into a measured one. Same construction (S2ER prompt with the gold argument replaced by the competing answer, three-round bidirectional voting at majority-3, pinned judge and prompt, validated on a self-carved 120-item split with 5 repeated runs, std, expert-comparison column). Adopt the Case A / Case B calibration pair as a unit test. Gate on a HETEROGENEOUS judge family: the carrier papers use one Qwen3-32B as corrector, simulator AND judge, and the 8B ablation swaps corrector and simulator while keeping the judge, so judge/actor family correlation is never broken in the published work and we must break it. (2) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: the prefetch oracle is computable offline on a self-carved split (we know, after the fact, what each turn needed) and is labelled a ceiling; the stopping oracle is ALREADY PUBLISHED per dataset per round by the carrier papers and is used as the regret target — the honest deliverable is regret-against-oracle-stop for a deployment-legal stopping rule, which is measurable precisely because the ceiling curve exists. For open-output turns the substitute is the estimator with a declared noise floor and paired significance. Nothing gold-derived reaches the runtime scheduler. (3) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the accept threshold and the prefetch spend are keyed on the estimated acoustic condition of the incoming turn, on the explicit hypothesis that a degraded turn should shift spend AWAY from anticipatory text supply and toward in-turn acoustic acquisition — because a prefetched document cannot recover a misheard entity, and the carrier papers' own residual failure mode is exactly that the ASR repeatedly misrecognizes the corrective utterance so the loop stalls for lack of reliable anchors. The global-constant baseline (one tau, fixed n, fixed interval) is the mandatory comparison arm.
- 成本与方差：COST AXIS: this sub-direction is where cost is the primary metric rather than an accounting duty. Report (a) prefetch spend — embedding/API calls, wasted-prefetch count, cache memory; (b) retrieval latency; (c) end-to-end turn latency; (d) core decode passes; (e) dollars. ADOPT THE CARRIER'S OWN METRIC DISCIPLINE AND ITS INVERSE: it reports retrieval latency as primary BECAUSE total time is swamped by generation variance, and it openly states the 112ms saving is INVISIBLE end-to-end with a 500-8000ms generator — so we pre-register which surface our wrapper touches and report both the isolated surface and the end-to-end number, and we do NOT claim an end-to-end win that the generation variance would swallow. HARD DUAL-BUDGET AUDIT (latency cap and spend cap; any turn exceeding either counts FAILED) over a budget ladder, per-cell win counts, no dominance claim. VARIANCE: the carrier runs ONE run per scenario with no CI; we run >= 3 seeds per scenario with bootstrap 95% CI on paired deltas, report per-scenario and per-turn-depth rather than pooling (its own per-scenario spread is 45%-95%), and use exact McNemar on paired turn-level outcomes.
- 击杀条件：(i) If the regime measurement on our speech carriers shows turn-to-turn evidence needs are not predictable above the random baseline, the mechanism is unjustified on speech and the sub-direction stops there — this is the first and cheapest measurement, and reporting a null here is a legitimate deliverable. (ii) If the random-prefetch arm matches the predictive arm, the published hit-rate story is warm-cache-not-prediction on our carrier too, and the predictive machinery is dropped. (iii) If the answer-quality axis shows cache-served context changing answers harmfully at a rate comparable to the latency it saves, the mechanism is net-negative and is killed regardless of the latency number. (iv) If the latency saving is invisible end-to-end under our actual generation cost, the claim is confined to the isolated surface and no system-level benefit is asserted. (v) If a deployment-legal stopping rule cannot close a meaningful fraction of the published oracle-stop regret, report the regret honestly and do not reframe the oracle curve as an achievable target — the carrier papers' own drift between versions on exactly this point is the failure mode to avoid.

**P 槽位**：P1 (evidence-state / knowledge-supply control) primary, with an explicit hand-off: P7 (interactive/full-duplex validation carrier) consumes the latency/budget accounting and the multi-turn harness produced here; P3/D2 owns anything that persists beyond the session (the carrier's own unimplemented historical pre-warming is a D2 write-gate question, not a D1 scheduling question). Imports P9 (D5.2 E1 admissibility gate / G1-G11) for the per-turn condition-keyed accept threshold's calibration, matching D1.1/D1.3: D1.6 supplies the similarity-margin STATISTIC, P9/D5.2 owns the threshold.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 Flagged as the thinnest speech surface in D1 and therefore the lowest build priority.

## D1 维度综合

HOW THE SIX RELATE. They are not six parallel proposals; they are one supply stack with a measurement instrument at its base. D1-5 (read-out/new-info discrimination) is logically PRIOR to all the others: without an instrument that says whether an acquisition changed the answer and in which direction, every pricing, branching, topology and retrieval claim in D1-1 through D1-4 and D1-6 is unmeasurable, and the dimension's own read set proves the point — 2603.02206 optimized an entire architecture on hit rate with answers never scored, and 2602.10656's +24.9% cannot be split between its two channels because no ablation exists. D1-1 (pricing) is the general controller over a menu; D1-2 (branching) and D1-3 (topology) supply the two most distinctive members of that menu on a speech core, and both exist because the observation is a decided variable; D1-4 (retrieval) is the one channel whose supply is exogenous and unbounded and therefore carries its own contamination and snapshot discipline; D1-6 (anticipation) is the temporal dimension of the same decision and is the thinnest speech surface.

BUILD ORDER, by cost-to-first-kill rather than by importance. (1) D1-5's oracle-discriminator row on the cheapest carrier — if a perfect supply discriminator buys nothing, most of D1 collapses, and this is a few hundred probed acquisitions on MELD-Hard1k. (2) D1-2's budget-matched comparison (k observation branches vs k decodes of one observation) — one comparison, one number, and it decides whether the dimension's most distinctive unoccupied surface is real. (3) D1-3's per-instance topology oracle, which is a pure measurement and produces the headroom row no paper in the read set publishes. (4) D1-1's priced policy, which needs the instrument from (1) and the arm sets from (2)-(3). (5) D1-4, which needs a frozen retrieval snapshot, a paid judge and a contamination audit before it produces anything. (6) D1-6, which needs a multi-turn harness and whose regime precondition may not even hold on speech. Sub-directions 1-3 share one carrier (reconstructed MELD-Hard1k), one estimator and one condition-key implementation, so their marginal cost after the first is small; 4 and 6 each carry their own infrastructure.

CROSS-DIMENSION SEAMS, stated so the charters do not collide. D1/D2: D1 owns the decision to pay for an acquisition and its price; D2 owns whether the result is written, how it is keyed, and eviction. The ACOUSTIC-CONDITION KEY is shared machinery built once — D1 uses it to index thresholds and prices, D2 uses it to index memory entries, and the donor read set flags condition keying as the sharpest unclaimed slot in P3 precisely because every memory donor keys on a symbol (class, symptom pattern, URL, task category) and none on a continuously-drifting estimate. D1-6 sits exactly on this seam and the split is: scheduling and pricing of anticipation = D1; anything surviving the session = D2. D1/D3: D1 owns how-many/which/when-to-stop over an already-given tool or operator surface; D3 owns the surface itself and tool credit. TwS's operator set sits on this seam, and the BLIND-FULL-CHAIN control is the instrument that separates them — an apparent D1 selection gain that vanishes under unconditional application of all operators was a D3 coverage gain all along, which is why that control is mandatory in three of the six designs. The same seam also governs D1-5 vs D3.1: D1-5 owns the forced-answer-probe instrument over SUPPLY ACTS (operator, re-decode, re-listen, retrieval hop), D3.1 owns it over TOOL INVOCATIONS and the direction-of-change arbitration for tool-produced evidence, and the probe-corpus construction is shared build-once infrastructure across the two rather than an undeclared duplicate.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 D1/D5: the scalar that prices supply is D1's consumer, not its object; its calibration and admissibility contract (G1-G11) belongs to P9 and is imported, and the selection-pressure risk of pricing supply on a proxy belongs to P10 — for which D1-5's base rates are the prerequisite baseline. D1/D4: the integrated-system charter consumes D1's arm sets and never the reverse; D1 makes no system-level claim.

WHAT IS UNIFORM ACROSS ALL SIX, and is the dimension's actual finding at direction level. Every supply decision in the D1 read set is an LLM classification, never an argmax over scored outcomes; every quantity is a constant (K_max=5, n=3-5, tau=0.40, max-iter 3, tool-cap 20); stop is model-internal or gold-owned; abstain is absent in all six papers; supply is never evaluated as supply; and the two papers that emit a usable signal never consume it (CoM's <final_confidence>, AGR's S_c). That uniformity is why the six designs share one baseline ladder shape (direct readout / random-among-N / consensus-only / oracle), one estimator, one condition-key, and one refusal — the comparison target is the published WRAPPER at equal supply cost, never the raw model, because raw-model deltas are already claimed and re-claiming them is not a contribution.

STANDING CAUTIONS THAT APPLY TO ALL SIX. (a) The three design pieces are not boilerplate: the answer-equivalence estimator is the load-bearing construct in D1-2 and D1-5 (agreement and 'unchanged' are undefined without it) and merely useful in D1-3 and D1-6; its clustering error must be reported in both directions and propagated, never assumed away. (b) Wrappers lose on subsets — the read set shows a topology controller at -1.1 on one core-benchmark pair and -4.3 on another while the same model gains +18.3 on a sibling subset, and a tool wrapper sending 41.6-43.5% of its answer changes in the wrong direction — so per-bucket reporting precedes every aggregate and losses are shown, not absorbed. (c) The local llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B lane is cheap verification only in all six designs, never load-bearing, and carries the stated weak-core caveat that no lift measured on it may be extrapolated as a strong-core prediction; where a design depends on innate tool-call emission alongside input_audio, that capability is verified first and externalization of the decision function is treated as an arm rather than a failure. (d) 1C semantics hold throughout: these are DESIGNS, nothing is executed, no novelty verdict is issued, donors carry domain labels, H5 stays withheld, and every occupancy or absence claim above is scoped to the stated read sets at cut 2026-07-26. (e) SHARED CALIBRATION CARVE (portfolio-level clause, matching D2/D5): each carrier shared across sub-directions — MELD/MELD-Hard1k (1/2/3), AudioRAG-500 (1/2/4/5), GigaSpeech/CS-Dialogue (1/3/5/6) — takes exactly ONE named, versioned, blob-registered calibration split shared by every sub-direction that touches that carrier, not six independent self-carved splits; D1 maintains a single cumulative gold-exposure ledger across all six designs so an item used as calibration in one sub-direction cannot silently become a test item in another, mechanically enforced by one file-open hook per carrier (2607.13347's design).【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

Gates G1-G11 are quoted from the D5 dossier's 决策效用证据 group synthesis (2026-07-26-d5-tfrl-dossier-draft.md line 173), which is their canonical statement.

DISCIPLINE CHECKS I RAN ON MY OWN OUTPUT. (1) No novelty verdict appears anywhere; occupancy words are always paired with a read-set qualifier and a cut date. (2) Every dataset named in an experiment-design cell exists in T2 with its local_status quoted; the only datasets named outside T2 are MUGEN's 1,750-instance set and a real multi-turn speech corpus with latency traces, both explicitly tagged NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION and not designed upon. (3) Every number carries its paper id; donor numbers carry a domain label and are never used as a cross-modality effectiveness claim (H5 withheld) — where a donor result could be misread that way (2606.28864's up-to-30-point TTS gains, 2606.03054's base rates, 2602.16699's oracle-match figures) I state explicitly that it is the shape or the protocol that transfers, not the effect. (4) All four fixed baseline rows appear in all six designs; the oracle row is flagged as absent from the D1 read set (T1 records oracle_if_published NONE for all six D1 rows) so producing it is part of each deliverable. (5) Gold-consuming instruments are fenced in every design: AudioRAG's correctness filter and error judge, the Agentic-ASR gold stopper and simulator, MMAR-Rubrics, the forced-answer-probe labels, the isotonic calibrator, and every oracle row. (6) The llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B lane is marked cheap-verification-only with the weak-core caveat in all six. (7) The three design pieces are instantiated concretely and differently per sub-direction rather than repeated.

TWO JUDGEMENT CALLS THE PARENT MAY WANT TO OVERRIDE. (a) I folded the Interactive/Agentic-ASR pair (2604.09121 / 2605.29430) into sub-directions 1, 5 and 6 as a CARRIER with a published oracle-stop ceiling, rather than giving the core-task repair loop its own sub-direction — because its repair right operates on ANSWERS, which my boundary assigns to D5/P8, and its D1-relevant content is exactly the singleton evidence-action menu and the noisy evidence channel. If the portfolio wants the speech core-task loop visible as its own charter, that is the seventh sub-direction and it should be carved from D5's side of the seam, not mine. (b) I marked sub-direction 2 (observation branching + cross-observation consensus) with a NEW_SLOT_CANDIDATE flag inside P1 rather than proposing a new slot outright: it is the only D1 surface with no occupant on either the speech or the donor side in the branch-over-observations form, and it straddles P1 and P8, so whether it is a component of P1 or a charter of its own is a portfolio-shaping decision above my level.

ONE CORRECTION I APPLIED TO THE SEED LIST. The owner's seeds listed 'read-out/new-info discrimination' and 'supply evaluation as supply' as two seeds; they are one construct measured by one instrument, so I merged them into sub-direction 5 and used the freed slot for anticipatory supply scheduling, which the dossier carries (2603.02206) but the seed list did not name.

# D2 多模态记忆（外部状态/笔记/经验库）

## 维度纲领

D2 管辖「跨实例持久物」：在单条 utterance / 单个 episode 结束后仍然存在、并在下一条输入上被读回以改变控制面决策的外部状态——笔记、经验条目、条件先验、可靠性画像、abstain 词条——以及它的写入通道（写门、准入统计量、生命周期）、读取通道（检索准入、注入定价、冲突仲裁）、防护面（溯源、隔离、投毒威胁模型）、键空间（声学条件 × 任务类型）与跨核/跨任务迁移资格。边界排除三类相邻物：(a) 单 episode 内的证据状态与工作记忆（AOP-Agent 的 M_E/M_W、Omni-Decision 的 query-scoped evidence state——归 D1/D4，D2 只在「把 per-episode 记忆提升为持久条目」这一升格步骤上与之接壤）；(b) 技能/程序工件（可执行 program、工具组合、技能退役——归 D3/P5，D2 只管「这个技能在什么条件下可信」这类元条目）；(c) 判官本身的质量研究（归 P9，D2 只把判官统计量当作写/读门的输入信号消费）。本维在语音/audio-omni 已读面上的净位置：外部记忆有两种形态被占（可编辑抽象笔记库带效用写门——但域标签是 VLM；逐 episode 分层 omni 证据记忆带检索工具与停止权——但随视频消亡），跨实例持久化、声学条件键控、黑盒合法写门、投毒威胁模型四项在该读面上未见占据。下列七个子方向按「键→条目→写→读→防→迁→计量」的控制回路顺序排列，全部为方向级，不含创新点判定，不含跨模态有效性结论（H5 withheld）。本次仅交付前三步 D2.1（键）/D2.2（条目）/D2.3（写门）；D2-4..D2-7（读/防/迁/计量）为 FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED，已同日委托补齐（见总集成「规模」条），正文中对它们的引用是设计意图声明而非已交付设计。【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

## D2.1 声学条件键控与键有效性验证

**定义**：记忆条目的检索键从「查询语义嵌入」扩展为二部键（声学 regime 描述子 × 任务类型），其中声学分量由确定性 DSP / 开源前端在部署时测得（SNR 估计、近讲/远场、说话人或 diarization 标签、重叠标志、带宽与编解码伪迹、语音/音乐/噪声类别、语速），而非由被冻结的核自行「说出口」；并同时交付一套键有效性工具，回答「这个键是否真的把失败机理分开了」而不是假定它有效。本子方向只管键空间本身（构造、分桶、可判别性、估计误差传播），不管条目内容（D2-2）与门（D2-3/D2-4）。

**语音域状态**：ABSENT（读面＝D2 卷宗 3 篇 + D0 五件 + T1 37 行，cut 2026-07-26）。三篇的键全部是文本或文本嵌入：2607.00685 键为 Qwen3-VL-Embedding 的 query 嵌入（域标签 VLM）；2605.28192 的 audio_keypoints 是核自行发出的自然语言句子（'A low, sustained, electronic musical tone plays throughout the segment'），经 BGE-M3 稠密与 BM25 稀疏索引，切分本身由 ASR 时间戳驱动（FunASR），因此非语音音频无独立时间索引、任何核未言语化的声学属性（SNR、信道、说话人身份/轮次、重叠、韵律轮廓、编解码伪迹）下游不可恢复；2604.25591 完全不按任何声学变量分层，最接近的切分只是 perception-vs-reasoning 子任务（Table III：MMAR-Perception 上 P(True) 双模型领先 0.73/0.73，MMAR-Reasoning 上语义熵族领先 0.74/0.70，最优信号随任务结构翻转）。2607.00685 自己的 §6 方向（'enrich notes beyond text'）只举参考图像、工具轨迹、结构化清单，全部仍是符号内容，未触及非言语声学条件。D0 五件同样无声学键：2607.11433 Omni-Decision 的 state 是 query-scoped evidence atoms，2606.15141 EChO 的 dispatch 是 question-type-conditioned static。

**视觉域对照**：PARTIAL（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条，cut 2026-07-26）。存在「非语义键」的成熟形态但键变量都是离散且可观测的：2602.20323 PhysMem（vision-VLM/具身机器人域）用手工设计的 SYMBOLIC STATE s（动作类型 + 物体属性离散特征）做聚类、检索硬过滤与 resonance 的 consistent(e,p) 判定，是本读面上最接近「条件键」的形态——但 s 是逐任务手工设计的离散量；2605.18652 MementoGUI（vision-GUI 域）的粗检索显式混权 s_{t,i}=λ_v·cos(q_t^v,m_i^v)+λ_g·cos(q^g,m_i^g)，即模态原生键与目标文本键的加权，证明「模态原生分量承重」这一形态在视觉侧已被建；2606.31612 ATMem（vision-GUI 域）按 item identity 键控而非环境条件；2605.07134 Region4Web 按 page URL 键控。D6 综合明确记录：'every memory donor here keys on class (RGSE), symptom-pattern (RSCB-MC), page URL (Region4Web) or task category (cotomi Act); none keys on a continuously-drifting, estimated deployment condition'。迁移边界：PhysMem 的符号态是离散、稳定、免费可得的；声学 regime 是连续、会话内漂移、且只能带误差估计——键估计误差会传播进下游每一道门，PhysMem 无此误差通道。

**文本域对照**：PARTIAL（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条 text/code 40 条，cut 2026-07-26）。键工程被做透但键变量是清洁离散标签：2603.23234 MemCollab（text/LLM agents 域）用 (task category, subcategory) 做硬过滤再叠 provenance 硬过滤再软相似排序（Eq.9-11），并在 §3.5 用「按类别的错误类型经验分布 + 两两 Jensen-Shannon 散度」验证该键确实把失败机理分开（代数族聚成低散度簇，Number Theory 与 Counting&Probability 分离）——这是本读面上唯一一套「论证而非假定键有效」的方法，且模态无关；2604.14004 Memory Transfer Learning（text/code 域）用 t-SNE + Davies-Bouldin Index + Local Inverse Simpson's Index 度量记忆嵌入的簇结构随抽象度上升而溶解（Figs 4-5），是键空间几何的现成诊断；2601.03192 MemRL（text 域）键为 intent 嵌入，其 §改进空间明确记录「no environment/condition key, so 'this worked under these conditions' cannot be expressed」；2604.27003（text-agent/embodied-text 域）给出反面警告——RETRIEVAL DIVERSITY COLLAPSE：BabyAI B→A 上把同质的 'find [object]' 洞见细分成近乎相同的条目后检索器无法区分，FWT 从 +15.0 掉到 −1.0，即「更细的键只在细分产生真正可区分且可被检索器分开的条目时才有用」。迁移边界：全部键是外部给定的干净标签（benchmark 名、语言、仓库、题类），语音侧的 domain 是会话内连续漂移且无标签的声学条件，held-out-domain 构造与 abstraction-vs-specificity 的对照都没有干净对应物。

**开放增量**：在本维已读面上未见有人做过的方向级台阶：把「部署时可测的声学 regime」当作一等检索键并先验证其有效性再使用——具体为 (a) 定义一组不依赖核言语化的声学描述子与分桶规则，(b) 把 MemCollab 的 JSD-by-error-type 键验证协议与 2604.14004 的嵌入几何诊断搬到声学桶上，回答「按声学桶切分是否真的把这个核的失败机理分开」，(c) 明确度量键估计误差如何传播（误键率 → 下游门的静默腐蚀率），因为这是 PhysMem/MemCollab 的离散键完全没有的误差通道。范围限定：本步只交付键与键有效性，不交付任何门；若键验证不通过（声学桶间 JSD 不显著高于随机分桶），后续六项的条件键控设计应整体降级为任务类型单键。

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：主载体（全部按 T2 行号引用，local status 逐条注明）：MELD / MELD-Hard1k（T2 行 26，local_status = needs_download（MELD）/ must-be-reconstructed（Hard1k，非字节一致））——本表中唯一自带完整声学扰动配方的载体（1,000 条测试 utterance、p=0.3 合成扰动、Table 3 配方，用 librosa/pyroomacoustics/pyrubberband 廉价重建），因此是声学桶的天然锚，且扰动类型已知＝桶标签有 ground truth 可供一次性键验证；LibriSpeech test-other × MS-SNSD（8 噪声类型 @10dB）/ L2-Arctic（6 种 L1 口音）（T2 行 34，local_status = metadata_only）——提供 SNR 受控与口音受控两条正交轴；SpeakerSleuth（T2 行 46，local_status = needs_download（released），1,818 条人工核验实例、跨 4 个语料、'controlled acoustic difficulty'）——提供说话人/轮次轴；MMAU test-mini 1k（T2 行 1，metadata_only）与 MMAR 1000 项（T2 行 2，metadata_only）作为任务类型轴（sound/music/speech/mixed × perception/reasoning）。次要：ParaPairAudioBench（T2 行 47，needs_download，5,175 对，Style/Rate/Emphasis/Age/Gender 五维）用于韵律轴。批量下载须在下一步提案单独批准的：MELD 全集、MELD-Hard1k 重建、SpeakerSleuth、ParaPairAudioBench。仅命名不承诺的 NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION（不在 T2 内）：DAPS（近讲/远场配对）、CHiME 系列（真实远场）、VoxConverse（重叠/diarization）、Common Voice 口音分片、Libri2Mix/WHAM!（重叠与噪声混合）。
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-3B 与 7B（T3 行 2，开放权重）+ Audio Flamingo 3（T3 行 3，开放权重）——刻意与 2604.25591 完全同核，使其 Table I-III 的 AUROC/AURAC 数字成为已发表参照带（published reference band）——在本设计报出任何 delta 之前须在我方技术栈上重测直读层，不得未经重测直接当作本设计的地板，理由是本读面（T3#11 等）反复记录同核跨复现不可移植；【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】三核并行是 R4（信号跨核翻转：同基准 P(True) 0.79/0.52，AF3 在 hallucination 上 0.44 低于随机）在键分层下的必要重测。本地验证通道：llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B（T3 行 1，-ngl 28 常驻 llama-server + input_audio）——仅用于验证声学描述子提取管线连通、桶标签 schema 可解析、prompt 合规；永不承重；弱核警告：该 30B 通道在 GGUF 下 tool-call 能力未验证（T3 行 1 deployment_constraints），且 D5 综合记录的 ATLAS 跨基准不对称是方向不稳的（2/3 favor weak-core gain、HLE-Verified 反转），故该通道上的任何键有效性数字不得外推至强核。声学描述子提取器一律是确定性 DSP 或开源前端（非被测核）：SNR 用 WADA-SNR 或波形能量-噪声底估计、语音/音乐/噪声用轻量分类器、说话人/重叠用开源 diarization、带宽与编解码用频谱截止检测、语速由 ASR 时间戳导出——每个提取器自身的误差率必须单独测并进入误差传播账。
- 基线阶梯：四行固定基线（本子方向的「被解释对象」是检索命中质量与桶可判别性，故四行按检索任务实例化）：(1) 直读＝无记忆单遍解答，同骨干同预算，作为一切 delta 的地板（published anchors：2604.25591 MMAU 7B acc 0.71 / 3B 0.73 / AF3 0.74，MMAR acc 0.55-0.58；2605.28192 MOV-Bench Qwen3-Omni-Instruct 52.79、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 46.63、MiniCPM-o 4.5 53.18；T1 digest §A 2602.10656 AudioRAG Qwen3-Omni-30B 直读 37.0）；(2) random-among-N＝在 K=10 重采样候选中随机取一（published floor：2606.09925 AudioProcessBench 随机聚合器 BoN 47.5 / MV 40.6 / Avg 44.1，是本语料中唯一发表的随机-聚合地板）；(3) consensus-only＝纯 counts 多数投票 / discrete semantic entropy 取众数，不用任何记忆与任何键（published anchor：2604.25591 MMAU 7B discrete semantic 0.85 AUROC / 0.90 AURAC；2606.09925 MV 对每一个真实 critic 都胜过 BoN）；(4) oracle＝逐项对候选池取最优（离线、消费 gold、只在自切校准分片上算一次并登记；published oracle anchors：2602.13685 AuTAgent single-tool-succeeds 66.1 vs No-Tool 50.0（MMAU test-mini, Qwen2-Audio-7B），2606.04730 KIT 17 候选 oracle 均值 +14.12 而最佳实现仅 +3.71＝26% 回收率、8/10 策略净差于 greedy）。本子方向另加两行专属对照：随机分桶键（桶数相同、成员随机——键的空对照，直接对应 2604.13552 的 random-rule 控制臂设计）与纯语义键（现状：BGE-M3 文本嵌入 top-K，即 2605.28192 的键）。
- 信号与合法性：键分量（全部 deployment-legal，因为不消费 gold 也不消费核内部量）：SNR 估计、近讲/远场判别、说话人/重叠标签、带宽与编解码伪迹检测、语音/音乐/噪声类别、语速——由外部确定性 DSP 或开源前端产生，legality 与 T1 legality 普查中 21 行 deployment-legal 同类。键有效性统计量：按桶的错误类型经验分布 + 两两 JSD（借自 2603.23234 §3.5，text 域方法）——此统计量在验证阶段需要 per-item 正误标签，因此是 GOLD-CONSUMING，一律围栏到自切校准分片上的一次性离线校准（分片＝MELD 训练侧 + LibriSpeech dev-other × MS-SNSD，绝不含任何将来报告 delta 的测试项）；嵌入几何诊断 DBI / LISI（借自 2604.14004，text/code 域方法）＝无标签，deployment-legal。K 次重采样的共识浓度（counts-only，deployment-legal，依赖答案等价估计器且其 FP/FN 须随表报出）——三件设计件 (b) 推荐的在线可用替代之一，此处补齐其合法性标签。【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】ILLEGAL 明列并给出替代：predictive entropy、length-normalized entropy、probability-mass semantic entropy、logit-normalized P(True)（均需 token/sequence logprob，2604.25591 五估计量中的三个）→ 替代为 discrete semantic entropy（counts-only，2604.25591 Eq.8-9，本就 legal）、NLI 聚类但以 cluster COUNTS 代替概率质量（我们自己的合法化，论文本身的 discrete 变体是直接丢掉聚类，两者不同，须分别测）、P(True) 以 K 次自验证的 vote rate 代替（legal）。G9 机械隔离：gold 文件在非校准阶段由文件打开钩子封锁并记录违规。
- 度量与门：绑定门：G9 边界（本子方向最吃重——键验证需要标签，必须机械隔离到校准分片）、G3 空白对照（随机分桶键 + 纯语义键两条空臂，2604.13552 的 random-rule 臂证明数学类基准根本测不出检索质量，故必须在 MELD/MMAR 这类非结构化输出上测）、G4 fidelity/分辨率（检索命中的 tie 率、跨大差距 tie 率、重复检索的 Kendall's W、位置偏置）、G7 异质性（按桶分解后再聚合，禁止只报总均值）、G11 双指标族。指标：键可判别性＝桶间 JSD 与桶内一致性、DBI/LISI；检索质量＝命中@K、误键率（估计桶 ≠ 真桶的比例，MELD-Hard1k 上有 ground-truth 桶）、误键的下游静默腐蚀率（误键条目被注入后的答案改变率）；token 指标与语义/rubric 指标成对——ASR 侧 WER/CER 必须配 S2ER（T3 行 24，LLM 二元语义等价判官，3 轮双向投票，Pearson r 0.8914/0.8280/0.9031，注意跨论文不可比：同组 Loop-0 GigaSpeech S2ER 14.12 vs 21.47），MCQ 侧 accuracy 必须配 MMAR-Rubrics（T3 行 25，offline-calibration-only，answer-gated，须 gold CoT，绝不入环）。逐桶分层 delta 为强制项：每个声学桶 × 每个任务类型（perception/reasoning，沿用 2604.25591 Table III 的切分）各报一格，永不只报均值。
- 三件设计件：(a) 答案等价估计器：本子方向的键验证需要「这两次回答算不算同一个答案」才能算错误类型分布。设计＝双层——MCQ 载体（MMAU/MMAR/SpeakerSleuth/ParaPair）用选项恒等（免费）；自由形式载体（MELD 标签串、ASR 转写、开放式）用 counts-based 双向 NLI 聚类（2604.25591 Eq.6-7 的合法化版：聚类保留、簇质量改用成员计数）叠加 S2ER 式二元语义等价判官（T3 行 24 协议，3 轮双向投票，多数-3 最优、多数-5/7 更差）；估计器自身的 FP/FN 率在校准分片上一次性测定并随每张表报出，绝不假定它是 0。(b) 确定性 oracle 替代：键验证的「正误」在部署没有 oracle，故 oracle 行只在校准分片上用 gold 算一次作参照；在线可用的替代＝K=10 重采样的共识浓度 + counts-based 语义熵 + 自验证 vote rate，三者先用 2601.21484 ETS 的密度分层分离协议（App B.4/Fig.9：只有 self-consistency 能把正误分开，mean logprob / negative entropy / self-certainty 全部重叠；text 域方法）验证其可分性，再用 2601.03192 MemRL 的 Q-校准可靠性图（分箱估计值 vs 下游成功率，原文 Pearson r=0.861，text 域）做校准检查。(c) 声学条件键控：本子方向即是该设计件的本体——二部键（声学 regime 桶 × 任务类型），桶由外部提取器给出而非核言语化，桶定义与阈值在校准分片上冻结后写入版本号，测试期不再调。
- 成本与方差：成本轴三项并列报告：(i) 键提取的边际成本——每条 utterance 的 DSP/前端秒数与显存，与核解码一遍的成本之比（预期 <5%，须实测而非声称）；(ii) 键验证的一次性离线成本——JSD 分析所需的标注调用与判官调用数，明确标为一次性、不进部署账；(iii) 按 R5 的不对称记账：K=10 重采样池若已被 D2-3/D2-4/D2-7 共用，则本子方向对它的边际成本为零，须在表中显式写出这一共享关系而不是重复计价（2604.25591 的 24-64% token 节省未含其自身 K=10 采样成本，是本读面上的反面样板）。方差：每格 ≥5 独立种子（沿用 2510.02995 AudioToolAgent 的 5 seeds × 12 agent 协议，其自述理由是 vendor 默认非零温度导致的非确定性）；比例类指标报 Wilson 置信区间（沿用 2607.11433 Omni-Decision 对两条主行的做法）；配对显著性用精确 McNemar / Wilcoxon（G6），逐桶报 sign test 并按 2606.17929 PreAct §5.3 的纪律把合并 p 值明确读作方向性下界（因为桶内重复是相关而非独立的），主张压在逐桶检验与方向一致性上。
- 击杀条件：预注册的四条停止线：(K1) 键无效——在校准分片上，声学桶间的错误类型 JSD 不显著高于随机分桶对照（同桶数、成员随机），则声学键控在本载体族上不成立，D2.2 与 D2-4..D2-7（后四项 FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED）的条件键控设计整体降级为任务类型单键，并如实记录该负结果；【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】(K2) 键估计不可用——误键率（估计桶 ≠ 真桶）在校准分片上测（分片＝MELD 训练侧按 Table 3 配方重建 + LibriSpeech dev-other × MS-SNSD，与「信号与合法性」条款的围栏口径一致，不得用 MELD-Hard1k 测试格的 ground-truth 桶做本设计决策，避免测试标签驱动键轴移除）> 30%，且误键条目的下游静默腐蚀率显著为正，则该桶轴（如重叠、编解码）从键中移除而非降低阈值；【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】(K3) 分辨率不足——纯语义键对照与声学键在所有桶上的检索命中差异都落在 5-seed 置信区间内，则本载体族分辨率不足，须换载体（优先补 NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION 的真实远场/重叠语料）而非加大 K；(K4) 反向证据——若纯语义键在多数桶上胜过二部键，如实报告并把「声学键在此核此任务族上无增益」写入方向图，不做补救性重调阈值（这条直接对应 R3 的基线倒挂纪律：wrapper 主张必须能输）。

**P 槽位**：P3

## D2.2 记忆条目 schema：类型化规范条目、溯源字段与 abstain 词条

**定义**：持久条目「长什么样」：字段集合（键、内容、类型、溯源、置信、生命周期计数器）、内容的抽象层级（原始轨迹 / 工作流 / 摘要 / 洞见）、条目类型体系（AVOID / PREFER / SEQUENCE / 期望可靠性 / abstain 旗标），以及一条条目必须携带多少「它是在什么条件下由谁用什么证据学到的」才能被审计。本子方向只管条目的数据模型与内容类型，不管准入门（D2-3）、检索准入（D2-4）与迁移资格（D2-6）——但数据模型的表达力是那三者的前置约束：字段里没有溯源，D2-5 的按写者回滚就无法表达；字段里没有条件作用域，D2-6 的条件作用域迁移就无法表达。

**语音域状态**：PARTIAL（读面＝D2 卷宗 3 篇 + D0 五件，cut 2026-07-26）。语音/omni 侧存在一套 episode 级结构化 schema：2605.28192 AOP-Agent 的 mid-level record ＝ {visual_keypoints[], audio_keypoints[], keywords[], description} + 全局摘要，层级为 fine-clip / mid-segment（G_max=30s、上限 120s、2.5s 重叠）/ global，audio keypoint 的字段规格明确要求 'speech intention, emotion, sound events, environment sounts, and audio-only evidence that may not be visually obvious'（Fig.9）——这是本读面上唯一带独立音频字段的记忆 schema。但四项缺失是明确的：无 confidence 字段、无 provenance 字段、无条件字段、无生命周期计数器；且该 schema 随视频消亡（严格 episodic），不落在 D2 的跨实例边界内。abstain 词条在本读面上零占据：2605.28192 由 prompt 明令禁止弃权（'You must select one of the provided options. Do not reject all options without selection'，Fig.14）；2604.25591 只度量弃权价值（AURAC）从未落为策略、无阈值选择程序、无 coverage/risk 工作点；2607.11433 Omni-Decision 的 Answer Critic 更是把弃权判为 BLOCK（'BLOCK when: the answer is a refusal or abstention'）并要求 'Commit now'（D0 卷宗记为 CONTRADICTED）。2607.00685（域标签 VLM）的三元组 (s,g,e) ＋ guidance 的四段式（Abstracted Error Pattern ≤60w / Root Cause ≤50w / Generalizable Strategy 用 If-Then ≤120w / Anti-Patterns & Boundary ≤80w，合计 ≤350w）是本读面上最完整的可复用条目模板，但同样无溯源、无条件、无 abstain 词条，且 K=1 意味着无多条目聚合与矛盾仲裁。

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条，cut 2026-07-26）。类型化规范条目在视觉/具身侧已被建：2602.20323 PhysMem（vision-VLM/具身域）的条目为 e=(o,ω,r,c,s)（观测、所选选项、二元结果、上下文、符号态），三层记忆（episodic 原始且容量受限 N_max / working 待检假设带置信 / long-term 已验证原则带重要性、按 γ=0.995 衰减），条目类型为 AVOID / PREFER / SEQUENCE 并在注入 prompt 时显示置信值（Fig.3）；其最有设计价值的观察是难任务上 68% 的已学原则是 AVOIDANCE 约束（中等难度为 41%），即系统主要靠「排除动作」变好。2606.31612 ATMem（vision-GUI 域）的 m_t=(φ_t, E_t)，每个条目带稳定 item 标识、结构化任务关键字段与执行状态 s_i ∈ {remaining, finished, skipped}。2606.17929 PreAct（vision-GUI 域）的条目是状态机程序 P=(S,T,M,V)，状态带验证谓词（可访问性树模式 + 超时）、转移带动作、轨迹中键入的具体值被提升为命名参数、metadata 带 DEDUP 签名。迁移边界：PhysMem 的置信来自与物理世界的交互（免费、可重复的二元结果），s 是手工设计的离散特征；PreAct 的谓词是对可访问性树的近确定性结构匹配（免费真假），波形观测无此免费谓词，最近的类比（对部分 ASR 转写做字符串匹配）本身是误差率随声学条件变化的噪声估计量。

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条 text/code 40 条，cut 2026-07-26）。2601.03192 MemRL（text 域）给出 Intent-Experience-Utility 三元组 M={(z_i, e_i, Q_i)}，Q 由环境奖励以 Monte-Carlo EMA 更新（Eq.4）且只更新真正被注入上下文的条目，并证明无偏性与方差界 lim sup Var(Q_t) ≤ α/(2−α)·Var(r|s,m)——这条方差界是设置 α 对抗奖励噪声的可用规则，对我们更重要因为我们的奖励更噪；其记忆构成分析显示最高 Q 分箱中仍保留约 12% 的「失败」记忆（高价值近失手），即效用门不等于成功过滤器，这是对 success-replay 写门的直接告诫。2603.23234 MemCollab（text 域）给出 m=(η, c, u, ℓ+, ℓ−) ——条目形如「enforce 不变量；avoid 违规模式」的类型化规范项，并带 MODEL-IDENTITY 溯源标签 (ℓ+, ℓ−) 存于单一共享库、读时按溯源过滤而非分库；库中只存抽象规范约束，绝不存原始轨迹、演示或解答。2604.14004（text/code 域）给出四级抽象阶梯 Trajectory / Workflow / Summary / Insight 作为现成的表示设计空间，并给出反演探针（让 LLM 从条目反推源任务、与真任务比相似度）作为可操作的抽象度量。2604.27283 RSCB-MC（text 域）给出 pattern-variant-episode 三层 schema，使控制器能区分「只有症状匹配」与「操作签名也匹配」。迁移边界：MemRL 的 r 是程序化成功位；MemCollab 的构建需要 N≥2 个可比模型解同一题并需带 gold 或可执行验证器的训练集（1000 标注实例/数据集），单核部署只能退化为其 self-contrast 变体，该论文自己测了退化幅度（7B 平均 67.5 vs MemCollab 71.6 vs vanilla 57.1）。

**开放增量**：在本维已读面上未见有人做过的方向级台阶：一个同时携带（i）条件作用域字段（本条目在哪个声学 regime × 任务类型下学到、宣称在哪些桶内有效）、（ii）溯源字段（哪个核、哪次采样、什么证据、什么提取 prompt 版本、什么时间）、（iii）类型（AVOID / PREFER / SEQUENCE / 期望可靠性 / ABSTAIN）、（iv）生命周期计数器（使用次数、被采信次数、被反驳次数、衰减重要性）的条目模型，且其中 ABSTAIN 类条目——形如「在此 regime 下此核对数值/说话人属性的自报置信不可信，弃权或升级」——在语音侧读面上完全无先例。同时交付一次表达力审计：把 D2.3（已交付）与 D2-4..D2-7（FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED，已同日委托补齐）每一项要表达的判定写成对该 schema 的查询，凡查不出来的即为字段缺口（这一步直接对应我记录在案的失败谱系：失败形态逐层上移到数据模型表达力/完备性）；对 D2-4..D2-7 的审计在其设计交付前只能是占位查询清单，不能算作已验证的表达力覆盖。【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：schema 是数据模型，验收靠「能否表达并支撑下游判定」，故载体选择以覆盖多种失败结构为准：MMAR 1000 项（T2 行 2，metadata_only；signal/perception/semantic/cultural 四层，是失败类型最异质的 MCQ 载体，且 2604.25591 记录它是最难、最推理绑定的一片：acc 0.55-0.58、所有方法最佳 AUROC 仅 0.67-0.71，且推理模式回退在三个模型上全部变差 0.59→0.54 / 0.58→0.53 / 0.56→0.47）；MMAU test-mini 1k（T2 行 1，metadata_only）作对照较易片；MELD / MELD-Hard1k（T2 行 26，needs_download / must-be-reconstructed）提供条件字段的真值桶；AQUA-Bench（T2 行 6，metadata_only，不可回答的音频问题）与 Audio-Hallucination（T2 行 7，metadata_only）——这两个是 ABSTAIN 类条目唯一可验收的载体，因为它们自带「本题应当弃权」的标签结构（AQUA 的不可回答标签、Audio-Hallucination 的幻觉标签），没有它们 abstain 词条无法测。SpeakerSleuth（T2 行 46，needs_download）提供说话人一致性这类「核系统性不可靠」的条目素材（其记录：给出文本形式的对话轮次时性能崩塌，模型优先文本连贯性而忽略声学证据，连明显的性别切换都检不出）。需下一步提案批准的批量下载：MELD + Hard1k 重建、SpeakerSleuth。NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION（不在 T2）：无——本子方向刻意只用 T2 内载体，以免 schema 设计被未知数据结构牵着走。
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-3B / 7B（T3 行 2）+ Audio Flamingo 3（T3 行 3）三核，理由同 D2-1（与 2604.25591 同核，直读与不确定性锚现成）；条目的「提炼器」（把一次失败轨迹变成一条规范条目）必须与「解答器」分离并单列，因为 2603.23234 已记录提炼器偏置风险（其 b-cancellation 论证 τ=f(s,b) 是非形式的、从未被度量，而提炼器恰是最强 agent，条目可能携带提炼器自身的偏置）。本地验证通道：llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B（T3 行 1）仅用于验证条目 JSON schema 能被稳定生成与解析——这一点非空话：2606.31612 的迁移边界明确指出「解析器假定可靠的结构化输出合规性，在冻结语音核上是真实风险」；该通道永不承重，弱核警告同 D2-1。
- 基线阶梯：四行固定基线（本子方向被解释的对象是「条目内容形态」，故四行都在同一注入预算下实例化，遵循 2604.27003 的信息预算匹配规则：所有条件每次检索呈现的条目数大致相同）：(1) 直读＝零条目注入（published anchors：2604.25591 MMAU 7B 0.71 / 3B 0.73 / AF3 0.74、MMSU 7B 0.62、SAKURA 7B 0.70、AQUA AF3 acc 0.18、MMAR 0.55-0.58）；(2) random-among-N＝随机注入 N 条库内条目（published floor：2606.09925 随机聚合器 BoN 47.5 / MV 40.6 / Avg 44.1；另可对齐 2604.13552 的 random-rule 臂——其在 GSM8k 上与真方法持平 87.41 vs 87.49 但在 Finance 上崩塌 0.2665 vs 0.2863，说明结构化基准根本测不出条目质量，故 MMAR/MELD 这类载体是必需的）；(3) consensus-only＝K=10 counts 多数投票、不注入任何条目（published anchor：2604.25591 discrete semantic MMAU 7B 0.85/0.90）；(4) oracle＝逐项在所有条目形态臂中取最优（离线、消费 gold、只在自切校准分片算一次并登记；published oracle anchors 同 D2-1：2602.13685 AuTAgent 66.1、2606.04730 KIT +14.12 vs 实现 +3.71）。本子方向另加抽象阶梯四臂（借 2604.14004 的 Trajectory / Workflow / Summary / Insight，text/code 域设计）与类型消融（有/无 AVOID 类、有/无 ABSTAIN 类），后者对齐 2604.13552 的正负规则不对称消融（其结果：去掉负规则的代价大于去掉正规则，87.49→86.88 vs →87.19，解释为正规则复述模型已知、负规则提供独有的禁令信号）。
- 信号与合法性：条目字段本身的 legality 分类是本子方向的核心交付之一：条件字段（来自 D2-1 的外部提取器）＝deployment-legal；溯源字段（核标识、采样序号、提取 prompt 版本、时间戳）＝deployment-legal（纯簿记）；置信字段——若来自核自报的 confidence 数字则是 deployment-legal 但未校准（2605.28192 的 confidence 是自报数而非 logprob，2509.16971 AudioGenie-Reasoner 的 S_c 更是发出后从未被任何阈值消费、全文从未评估），若来自 K 次重采样的共识浓度/vote rate 则 deployment-legal 且有 2604.25591 的 AUROC/AURAC 锚；效用/Q 字段——MemRL 式 EMA 需要奖励 r，部署无 oracle，故 r 必须是代理，且必须显式声明 MemRL 的无偏性证明此时只收敛到 E[proxy|s,m] 而非真效用（该论文的迁移边界原文即如此警告）。ILLEGAL 明列：任何以 test-item gold 填充的正误字段（G9）；任何 logprob 派生的置信字段（predictive/length-normalized/probability-mass 语义熵、logit-normalized P(True)、2604.13552 的 min-PPL 选择——后者尤须注意，该论文自称黑盒 API 定位却在 Eq.5 依赖 sequence logprob，是本读面上最隐蔽的一处合法性泄漏）。gold-consuming 仪表围栏：抽象度反演探针（2604.14004 §4.3.3）与条目正确性标注一律只在自切校准分片上跑一次。
- 度量与门：绑定门：G9 边界（条目字段不得含 test-item gold 派生量，文件钩子机械封锁）、G3 空白对照（random-rule 臂强制，且必须在非结构化输出载体上跑）、G5 margin 诚实（ABSTAIN 类条目必须报「实际触发了多少次弃权、覆盖率降了多少」，一个只在 12/476 上触发的弃权规则是伪装的 no-op）、G7 异质性（按声学桶 × 任务类型分解）、G11 双指标族。指标：schema 表达力审计＝D2.3（已交付）与 D2-4..D2-7（FORWARD_REFERENCE_NOT_YET_DESIGNED）的判定清单中可由该 schema 直接查询回答的比例（缺口逐条列出，不聚合为单一完成度——遵循「完成度永不聚合上标」；D2-4..D2-7 部分在其设计交付前不可执行，标为待运行）；【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】条目经济性＝条目数（Mem）与条目 token 长度（Len）并列于准确率旁（借 2607.00685 的记账约定，其规模为 28-50 条、261-462 tokens/guidance，最多 61-67 条）；ABSTAIN 类条目的验收＝risk-coverage 曲线与 AURAC（借 2604.25591 的度量对，且必须同时报原始准确率，因为该论文的 AF3/AQUA 行——AUROC 0.82-0.89 但 AURAC 仅 0.34-0.40、基准准确率 0.18——正是「排序好但覆盖无用」的样板）；token 指标配语义指标：ASR 侧 WER/CER 配 S2ER（T3 行 24），MCQ 侧 accuracy 配 MMAR-Rubrics（T3 行 25，offline-only）——理由是本读面已记录 token 级指标系统性低报 wrapper 增益（T1 digest 事实 6：S²ER 19.73→4.16 而 MER 仅 14.44→13.28；Audio-Mind MMAR-Rubrics +6.9 vs accuracy +1.5，约 4.6 倍差）。逐桶分层 delta 强制。
- 三件设计件：(a) 答案等价估计器：条目的「这条经验适用于同一类失败吗」需要判断两次输出是否等价。设计同 D2-1（MCQ 用选项恒等；自由形式用 counts-based 双向 NLI 聚类 + S2ER 式二元等价判官 3 轮双向投票），并额外要求：估计器的 FP/FN 在校准分片上测定后，作为条目「失败类型」标注的噪声上界写入 schema 的元数据字段——即 schema 自身承认其内容是带噪估计的产物。(b) 确定性 oracle 替代：条目的置信/效用字段不得由 gold 填充；替代＝K=10 共识浓度 + counts 语义熵 + 自验证 vote rate + 参考无关 critic 边际（T3 行 31 AudioProcessBench critic prompt，reference-free，deployment-legal；注意其 SelfGap 自盲：既是生成器又是评审的两个最强 critic 自盲最重 −24.6/−25.2，故条目的置信绝不能由产生该条目的同一核自评——这是 G10 在 schema 层的具体化）。(c) 声学条件键控：条件作用域字段直接消费 D2-1 的桶标签，且必须记录「本条目宣称有效的桶集合」与「本条目实际被观测到有效的桶集合」两个独立字段，二者的差就是 D2-6 迁移资格门的输入。
- 成本与方差：成本轴：条目提炼的调用成本（每条失败轨迹一次提炼调用，按 2607.00685 的量级参照 ~$3-7 全流程 vs RL/DPE 自演化基线 >$500——域标签 VLM，只作量级参照不作跨模态结论）；注入成本按 Len × 检索条数计入每次解答的 prompt token；异步提炼的延迟记账采用 2604.13552 Table 7 的模板（顺序 10.25s / 并行 4.11s / 单次调用 2.05s，并证明异步记忆更新在下一次查询到来前完成，用户可感知延迟封顶 2.01×）。方差：≥5 种子；条目库随机重建 3 次（提炼是随机过程，库本身是随机变量——2605.28192 的记忆构建温度为 1.0 正是此风险的来源）；比例指标报 Wilson CI；配对 McNemar/Wilcoxon；逐桶 sign test 并按 PreAct §5.3 纪律把合并 p 读作方向性下界。
- 击杀条件：(K1) 表达力不足但不可修——若表达力审计显示 D2.3（已交付部分可立即测）与 D2-4..D2-7（补齐交付后测）的判定中有 >1/3 无法由任何合理字段集表达（而非仅仅当前字段集缺失），则 schema 路线本身有问题，回到 D2-1 重议键与条目的粒度；【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】(K2) 条目形态无分辨率——四级抽象阶梯与类型消融在所有桶上的差异都落在 5-seed CI 内，且 random-rule 臂与真条目臂无差异，则该载体族测不出条目质量，换载体（优先 MELD/MMAR/开放式）而非加大注入预算；(K3) ABSTAIN 词条不可验收——若 AQUA-Bench 与 Audio-Hallucination 上 ABSTAIN 类条目的 risk-coverage 曲线不优于「按共识浓度全局阈值弃权」（即条目化没有超出单一统计量），则 ABSTAIN 类条目降级为 P9 的一个阈值而不再作为 D2 的持久条目类型；(K4) 提炼器偏置不可分离——若把提炼器换核后条目内容的下游效果显著改变而条件字段不变，说明条目编码的是提炼器特异性而非条件结构，如实报告并将该发现前置到 D2-6 的迁移资格门（对齐 2603.23234 未度量的 b-cancellation 缺口）。

**P 槽位**：P3

## D2.3 黑盒合法写门与条目生命周期（准入、逐条归因、反驳、衰减、驱逐）

**定义**：决定「什么可以被写进持久库」以及「写进去之后如何被降级或删除」的机制：准入统计量（在无 test-item gold 的部署条件下由共识浓度、判官边际、自验证投票率等合法信号构成）、准入的风险目标（写门的假阳率对应一个显式风险预算而非一个调出来的常数）、逐条目反事实归因（而非批级聚合接受/回滚）、以及生命周期（隔离窗口、置信更新、反驳、重要性衰减、驱逐、折叠压缩）。本子方向不管条目内容（D2-2）也不管读时准入（D2-4），只管「入库与留存」这一侧。

**语音域状态**：ABSENT（读面＝D2 卷宗 3 篇 + D0 五件，cut 2026-07-26）。三篇均无黑盒合法写门：2607.00685（域标签 VLM）的 commit gate 是批级 accept-if-improves 带回滚（Eq.6-7、Alg.1 line 16：在同一批上以 M'_i 重跑，Acc(B_i|M'_i) ≥ Acc(B_i|M_i) 才接受），其奖励是 gold 匹配或 LLM 判官二元裁决——两种实例化都用了但从未消融判官奖励 vs gold 奖励，因此该门在噪声奖励下的行为未被测量；T1 legality 普查把该行归入 train-time-only / gold-label-bound 并注明「NOT deployment-legal as-is against test-item gold」。该门确实承重且已被测：去掉后 MMMU_val 65.7，低于 vanilla 67.1，带门 68.4；MathVista 76.2 vs 77.3——但 Table 2 的完整六格显示这只在 MMMU 上对两个骨干都决定性，在 Qwen3-VL-Plus 的 MathVista/MMStar 上未带门变体持平或略胜（80.4 vs 80.1；69.6 vs 69.4），在 8B/MMStar 上未带门 62.7 也高于 vanilla 62.1，即该门是防最坏回归而非一致改善。2605.28192 完全无门：每个 keypoint/description 由单次 Omni-LLM 调用在温度 1.0 下提交，无验证、无重采样、无共识、无置信字段、无溯源、无回滚，唯一防线是 prompt 级 'Do NOT hallucinate events outside this specific timeframe'——其 App E.2 是教科书级自投毒：记忆库含幻觉线索（'someone shouts for everyone to stop'），agent 过度解读并推出错误因果关系答错。2604.25591 产出了写门所需的全部信号却从未接到记忆上（自列为 future work）。生命周期在语音侧同样零占据：无逐条归因、无使用计数、无衰减、无驱逐、无隔离。

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条，cut 2026-07-26），但每一种门都靠我们没有的 oracle 站立。2606.17929 PreAct（vision-GUI 域）的 verify-before-store 是本读面上最干净的写门规格：双谓词——把环境重置到任务的干净初始态、在那里重放 P′、并咨询基准自带的评估器，仅当 r′.success（重放到达终态无错）AND score′ ≥ 1.0 才存；两半各自必要（只用评估器通过会放进「动作静默 no-op 但环境仍持有先前步骤的通过态」的程序，只用重放成功会放进 cov=100%/score=0 这类重要失败，Table 3 记了五个可复现实例）；存储按 dedup 签名 UPSERT 而非追加。其 2×2 门消融（cold/warm × gate-on/gate-off，报每格 cold→warm delta 与 diff-of-deltas 作为「门的增益」：ΔON +1.2/+0.2/−4.0 vs ΔOFF −1.4/−2.4/−5.75，增益 2.6/2.6/1.75）是可直接照搬的设计。其 L6 自陈正是我们的问题：门靠重执行验证，预设可重置或幂等环境与可得的任务评估器。2602.20323 PhysMem（vision-VLM/具身域）的 verify-then-promote 是证据门而非相似度门：conf(h) ≥ τ_p≈0.8 AND |E_support| ≥ 3 才提升为原则，提升时源经验被折叠并删除（Eq.6）；conf ≤ τ_r 且 ≥2 条矛盾经验则反驳并移除；长期记忆重要性按 γ=0.995 衰减；并有 surprise 触发写入（resonance ρ<1 触发巩固，ρ=1 只强化）与 ACTION-LEVEL 归因（Eq.5，只用「该假设所指具体动作被尝试过」的经验更新置信，以隔离 episode 级混杂）。其消融（1.0× token 归一化）：w/o Resonance 81/58/21、w/o Verification 85/64/27、w/o Working Memory 84/69/28，验证与共振各自承重且重要性随难度上升。2605.18652 MementoGUI（vision-GUI 域）的写门形状为「学得的显著性标量 o_t > τ 则追加」（Eq.5），是确定性阈值化的干净形状，但 o_t 来自 LoRA 训练的算子，机制不在免训练合同内。2606.31612 ATMem（vision-GUI 域）把选择性学进权重（STR-GRPO），其 §改进空间明确记下「一个在写时执行同一边际效用准则的外部写门，正是本文留下的免训练槽位」。

**文本域对照**：PARTIAL（供体读面＝D6 卷宗 73 条 text/code 40 条，cut 2026-07-26）。唯一真正无标签无外部反馈的写门是 2604.13552 TF-TTCL（text 域）的写弃权规则：闭式任务按答案多数投票分正负，若所有 agent 互不相同则整条样本丢弃、不写任何规则；开放式任务按与 teacher anchor 的嵌入相似度取前 50% 为正。但它有两处硬伤：负例挑选用最小困惑度（Eq.5）需 sequence logprob，与其自称的黑盒 API 定位（Table 1/Table 4）矛盾；且写弃权的假丢弃率从未测量、无分级准入。其可借鉴的三件：正负规则不对称消融（去负规则 87.49→86.88，去正规则 →87.19，即负规则提供独有禁令信号）、随机规则对照臂（GSM8k 上持平 87.41 而 Finance 上崩塌 0.2665 vs 0.2863）、以及记忆剪枝作正则（相似度 FIFO 上限 1000 条略微提升 87.49→87.72 同时封顶开销）。2601.03192 MemRL（text 域）无写入门（每条摘要轨迹都写，过滤靠事后 Q 与相似度阈值），但给出留存侧仪表：Forgetting Rate FR = N_lost/N_fail（MemRL 0.041 vs MemP 0.051，去掉 z-score 归一化与相似度门后升至 0.073）与 Q-校准可靠性图（Pearson r=0.861，从 0.2-0.3 分箱的 21.5% 升到 0.9-1.0 分箱的 88.1%）。2510.08191 Training-Free GRPO（text 域）给出写门的动作词汇 Add/Delete/Modify/Keep 与「只有同时含赢家与输家的组才产生信号」的组比较规则。2603.23234 MemCollab（text 域）的门是离线对照标注 oracle（需 gold 或可执行验证器 + 1000 标注实例/数据集）。

**开放增量**：在本维已读面上未见有人做过的方向级台阶：把 PhysMem 的 verify-then-promote 三态生命周期（经验 → 待检假设带置信 → 已验证原则）在无确定性 oracle 的条件下重建——用部署时真有的统计量代替物理交互与基准评估器：以 K 次重采样的共识浓度、判官边际、自验证投票率组成的准入统计量驱动置信更新，以「跨条件重现」代替「重执行」（同一条目在同一声学桶的多条独立样本上是否重复被证实），并把 τ_p 从调出来的常数改为对应一个显式假原则率目标的校准阈值（逐核逐桶在自切校准分片上拟合，因为 R4 的信号跨核翻转使全局单门不安全）。同时补上语音侧完全缺失的逐条目反事实归因（hold-out-one-note 重跑）与生命周期（使用计数、衰减、驱逐、隔离窗口），使「修好两题毒害一题」的条目不再被批级聚合门欣然接受。

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：写门的验收需要「可重复遇到同类失败」的载体，故按失败结构复现性挑选：MMAR 1000 项（T2 行 2，metadata_only）——最异质、最难、且 2604.25591 已记录推理模式回退在其上对三个模型全部有害（0.59→0.54 / 0.58→0.53 / 0.56→0.47），因此它是检验「门开对了但修复无用」这一 R2 风险的最锋利载体；MMAU test-mini 1k（T2 行 1，metadata_only）作较易对照；MELD / MELD-Hard1k（T2 行 26，needs_download / must-be-reconstructed）——扰动类型已知使「同类失败」有客观定义，是写门准入正确性唯一有真值可核的格；LibriSpeech test-other × MS-SNSD 8 噪声 @10dB（T2 行 34，metadata_only）提供同一 utterance 在多个 SNR 下的重复观测，用以实例化「跨条件重现」这一重执行替代；AQUA-Bench（T2 行 6）与 Audio-Hallucination（T2 行 7）用于测门在信任度条件下的稳定性（R4 的实测面：P(True) 在 3B/AQUA 上 0.52＝随机、AF3/Hallucination 上 0.44＝低于随机）。需下一步提案批准的批量下载：MELD + Hard1k 重建。NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION（不在 T2，仅命名）：CHiME 系列真实远场、Libri2Mix/WHAM! 重叠混合——用于将来把「跨条件重现」推到真实而非合成退化上。
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-3B / 7B（T3 行 2）+ Audio Flamingo 3（T3 行 3）三核并行，且写门阈值必须逐核拟合——理由是 R4 的实测：同一基准上 P(True) AUROC 在 7B 为 0.79 而在 3B 为 0.52，AF3 在 hallucination 上 0.44，最优信号还随 perception-vs-reasoning 条目类型翻转（2604.25591 Table III）。判官/critic 角色必须与解答核分离并单列，遵循 G10：AudioProcessBench 记录既是生成器又是评审的两个最强 critic 自盲最重（SelfGap −24.6 / −25.2，而总体最强 critic Gemini-3-Flash 因不在该生成器池中 SelfGap 未定义）。本地验证通道：llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B（T3 行 1）仅验证写门管线连通（统计量能算出、阈值能触发、隔离队列能落盘），永不承重；弱核警告：该通道对应 2605.28192 Table 4 的 Qwen3-Omni-30B planner/reflector 行，但我们只借其存在性不借其数字，且 D5 综合记录的弱核不对称是方向不稳的。
- 基线阶梯：四行固定基线（被解释对象＝写门是否让库变好而不是变坏，故四行都在「同库容量、同注入预算」下实例化）：(1) 直读＝空库（published anchors：2604.25591 MMAU 7B 0.71 / 3B 0.73 / AF3 0.74、MMAR 0.55-0.58、MMSU 7B 0.62、SAKURA 7B 0.70；2605.28192 MOV-Bench Qwen3-Omni-Instruct 52.79 / Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 46.63）；(2) random-among-N＝无门写入后随机取一条注入（published floor：2606.09925 随机聚合器 BoN 47.5 / MV 40.6 / Avg 44.1）；(3) consensus-only＝K=10 counts 多数投票、完全不写不读记忆（published anchor：2604.25591 discrete semantic MMAU 7B 0.85 AUROC / 0.90 AURAC）——这一行同时是「门的价值必须超出单纯共识」的最硬对照；(4) oracle＝逐项在全部写门变体中取最优（离线、消费 gold、仅在自切校准分片上算一次并登记；published oracle anchors：2602.13685 AuTAgent single-tool-succeeds 66.1 vs No-Tool 50.0，2606.04730 KIT 17 候选 oracle 均值 +14.12 而最佳实现 +3.71＝26% 回收率、8/10 策略净差于 greedy）。本子方向另加三条必须行：无门写入臂（直接对齐 2607.00685 Table 2 的 65.7 低于 vanilla 67.1 这一负结果锚，域标签 VLM）、批级 accept-if-improves 臂（把 M2Note 的门用判官奖励而非 gold 重建，即它自己从未单独评估的那个变体）、以及 PreAct 式 2×2 门消融（cold/warm × gate-on/gate-off，报 diff-of-deltas 作为门的增益，vision-GUI 域设计）。
- 信号与合法性：准入统计量（全部 deployment-legal）：K 次重采样的共识浓度与 counts-based discrete semantic entropy（2604.25591 Eq.8-9，纯计数，本就合法；T3 行 30 记其在 MMAU 7B 为 0.85 AUROC / 0.90 AURAC）；counts-based NLI 聚类熵（我们自己的合法化，与论文的 discrete 变体不同——后者是直接丢掉聚类，两者须分别测）；自验证 vote rate（P(True) 的合法形式）；参考无关 critic 的 top-2 边际（T3 行 31 AudioProcessBench critic prompt，reference-free，deployment-legal，最佳 critic Gemini-3-Flash step Avg 69.2 / chain Avg 60.5，最差 Qwen2-Audio-7B step Avg 17.7 低于随机 23.4 却仍在 chain 上 51.0，比随机聚合器高 6.9——这正是 G3 空白对照不可省的证据）；MBR（T3 行 32，0 模型调用、基于 chrF，是本读面上最便宜的合法信号，且 KIT 记录其选中虚假 SHAS 候选的比例在每个任务上都是 0.0%）。ILLEGAL 明列：test-item gold 驱动的批级 accept-if-improves（M2Note 原形，T1 归入 train-time-only）、任何 logprob 派生量（predictive/length-normalized/probability-mass 语义熵、logit-normalized P(True)、TF-TTCL 的 min-PPL）、以及 PreAct 门的第二半（基准自带确定性评估器）。gold-consuming 仪表围栏：写门的准入正确性（真阳/假阳率）、τ_p 的风险目标标定、Q-校准可靠性图，全部只在自切校准分片（MELD 训练侧 + LibriSpeech dev-other × MS-SNSD）上跑一次并登记 blob；G9 机械隔离由文件打开钩子执行并记录违规。
- 度量与门：绑定门：G1 headroom（无 oracle-over-arm-set 行与 recovery 分数则不得作决策效用主张——KIT 的 26% 回收率是本读面的参照量级）、G2 直读地板（同骨干同预算直读必须作为一等策略行，跑不赢即为输，R3 已实测 −14.5/−17.5 与 +0.38 的塌缩）、G3 空白对照（random-among-N、无权重多数投票、组内分数置换三条）、G5 margin 诚实（门实际接受了多少条、拒了多少条——一个只接受 12/476 的门是伪装的 no-op）、G6 harm 尾部（严重损失阈值、变差/变好占比、配对 McNemar/Wilcoxon，绝不只报均值——文档域研究里统计中性的均值掩盖了 25.7% 变差实例）、G9 边界、G10 自偏置（SelfGap 必测）、G11 双指标族。指标：写门质量与修复质量分开测（R2：2604.25591 的 MMAR 结果证明门开对了但回退无用则一文不值，adaptive 0.58 vs direct 0.59，任何阈值救不了；因此动作集必须含 no-op 修复）；逐条目反事实归因＝hold-out-one-note 重跑的 delta 分布；留存侧仪表＝Forgetting Rate FR=N_lost/N_fail（借 2601.03192，text 域）与 Q-校准可靠性图；库经济性＝Mem（条目数）与 Len（token）并列于准确率旁（借 2607.00685 记账约定）。token 配语义：ASR 侧 WER/CER 配 S2ER（T3 行 24），MCQ 侧 accuracy 配 MMAR-Rubrics（T3 行 25，offline-only，answer-gated）。逐桶分层 delta 强制（声学桶 × perception/reasoning）。
- 三件设计件：(a) 答案等价估计器：写门的准入统计量（共识浓度）本身依赖「这 K 个采样里哪些算同一个答案」。设计＝MCQ 用选项恒等；自由形式用 counts-based 双向 NLI 聚类（2604.25591 Eq.6-7 合法化：聚类保留、簇质量改用成员计数）＋ S2ER 式二元语义等价判官（T3 行 24，3 轮双向投票，多数-3 最优）；估计器 FP/FN 在校准分片测定并随每张表报出——这一点对写门尤其致命，因为等价估计器的误差会直接伪造共识浓度，进而伪造准入。(b) 确定性 oracle 替代：这是本子方向的核心设计件。以「跨条件重现」代替 PreAct 的重执行与 PhysMem 的物理交互——一条候选条目只有在同一声学桶的 ≥3 条独立样本上都使准入统计量改善（共识浓度上升或 critic 边际上升）时才从隔离区提升为正式条目（结构上照搬 PhysMem 的 conf ≥ τ_p AND |E_support| ≥ 3，但证据来源换成合法统计量）；反驳规则同构（≥2 条矛盾样本且置信低于 τ_r 则移除）；τ_p/τ_r 逐核逐桶在校准分片上按显式假原则率目标标定，而非取 0.8/0.995 这类常数。替代统计量的可分性先用 2601.21484 ETS 的密度分层分离协议（App B.4/Fig.9，text 域）验证，校准性用 MemRL 的 Q-可靠性图（r=0.861，text 域）核。(c) 声学条件键控：写门是分桶的——准入阈值逐桶拟合，且条目入库时强制记录「在哪个桶被证实」；LibriSpeech × MS-SNSD 的同一 utterance 多 SNR 结构使「同桶重现」与「跨桶重现」可被分开测，这是本设计件在写门上的具体咬合点。
- 成本与方差：成本轴：按 R5 的不对称记账显式写出——若控制面本就为 D2-4/D2-7 生成 K=10 候选，则共识浓度作为写门统计量的边际成本为零，这一条必须在成本表里写成共享关系而非重复计价（2604.25591 的 24-64% token 节省未含其自身 K=10 采样成本，是本读面的反面样板）；隔离区重现验证的成本按「每条候选条目 ×(≥3 条同桶样本 × K 次采样)」计，并与其带来的准确率增益一起报为「每点成本」；提炼与门判定的调用数单列；PreAct 的 L3 记录其 verify-replay 在 OSWorld 上中位 +217% wall overhead、Android +162%（vision-GUI 域），作为「重执行式门很贵」的量级参照——我们的替代必须报出自己的对应数字而非声称更便宜。方差：≥5 种子；库随机重建 3 次（写门是随机过程的下游，库本身是随机变量）；比例指标 Wilson CI；配对 McNemar/Wilcoxon（G6）；逐桶 sign test，合并 p 按 PreAct §5.3 纪律读作方向性下界；门增益用 diff-of-deltas（2×2 设计）而非单格 delta。
- 击杀条件：(K1) 门无价值——带门臂与无门臂在所有桶上的 diff-of-deltas 都落在 5-seed CI 内，且带门臂不优于 consensus-only 行，则黑盒合法写门在本载体族与本核族上无增益，如实报告并把 D2 的重心移向 D2-4 读门（对齐 2607.00685 自身的六格证据：其门只在 MMMU 上决定性，在另外几格上未带门持平或略胜）；(K2) 门开对但修复无用——门的准入精度良好（校准分片上真阳率高）而端到端 delta 仍 ≤0，则命中 R2，须把动作集扩到含 no-op 与多种修复并重跑，若仍 ≤0 则记为「本任务族上记忆中介的修复不如提示中介」并停止加装门（MMAR 是最可能触发这条的格）；(K3) 统计量不可分——ETS 密度分层协议显示共识浓度/critic 边际在正误上重叠不可分，则该统计量不得作准入依据，退回只用 MBR 或直接判本子方向在该核上不成立；(K4) 隔离期成本失控——重现验证的成本使每点成本高于「直接多采样再多数投票」的每点成本，则写门在经济上被 consensus-only 支配，如实报告并把结论写成「在此预算带内记忆不划算」；(K5) 等价估计器污染——若等价估计器的 FP/FN 使共识浓度的有效分辨率低于门阈值的区分需求（即门实际上在噪声上开合），则先修估计器，禁止在未修的估计器上报任何门增益。

**P 槽位**：P3

<!-- 补齐交付：workflow wf_cafdaeba-493，2026-07-27 -->
## D2.4 检索准入与读门（一条记忆何时被允许掌舵）
