R3 CONSENSUS-ONLY: take the post-evidence answer when the plane's internal sources agree, else direct — the cheapest hand rule and the incumbent-shaped opponent. R4 ORACLE: per-instance best of {direct, post-evidence}, gold-consuming, ceiling only, with recovery = realized/oracle as the headline statistic (G1) and 2606.04730's 26% recovery as the field's reference point for how little of an oracle gap gets realized. PUBLISHED ANCHORS: 2605.28480's bucket table (−0.6 / −0.8 / +1.2 / +6.3 / +9.4 / +4.0 / −33.3) and subcategory deltas (+17.65 Anomaly, +15.87 Music Theory, +14.29 Temporal against −25.00 Audio Difference, −13.33 Spatial, −12.50 Aesthetic, −11.11 Acoustic Quality); 2511.02834 OmniInstruct −3.6; 2512.23646 68.7 vs 72.08; 2607.11433's critic-gated strata (First-pass accepted 652 / 60.4%, Recovered 53 / 41.5%, Stuck 152 / 21.7%) as the ready-made stratification format.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL selector inputs, all label-free: (i) frontend self-consistency across resampled re-listens, counts only; (ii) agreement between the direct answer and the post-evidence answer; (iii) evidence-answer support count and contradiction count; (iv) claim-boundary violations flagged by the tool-boundary contract; (v) the format checker's structural verdict. OFFLINE-ONLY, CARVED SPLIT: the admissibility band's thresholds are fit ONCE on a carved 200-item calibration split against MMAR-Rubrics/gold and never re-touched; MMAR-Rubrics is gold-CoT and gold-answer dependent and answer-gated, hence deployment-illegal in loop (T2 and T3 both state this). ILLEGAL: any use of test-item gold to decide which of the two answers to keep — precisely the recorded information-boundary failure mode, mechanically fenced under G9 with a logged file-open hook.
- 度量与门：BINDING: G1 (oracle + recovery fraction), G2 (the guarantee IS G2 made operational), G3 (random-among-two null + always-post-evidence null + within-instance permutation), G5 (margin honesty — report how many items the selector actually switched; a band that fires on 1% is a no-op), G6 (harm tail: worsen/improve share, severe-loss threshold, up-mass vs down-mass, exact McNemar / Wilcoxon, never means alone — the reference cautionary datum is a statistically neutral mean concealing 25.7% worsened instances in the document study), G7 (per-bucket before aggregate), G8 (robust-tiebreak: the advantage must hold under all tie-breaking rules), G9 (boundary), G11 (two metric families on the same output). PRIMARY ARTIFACT: the per-bucket delta table plus a CATTS-shaped net-advantage-by-band table with the negative band shown. Token/answer accuracy paired with the MMAR-Rubrics process score; on the options-stripped arm, rubric-style scoring paired with exact match so the protocol effect is visible.
- 三件设计件：ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: the guarantee's unit of account is 'did the plane change the answer?', which requires an equivalence relation — exact match on MCQ (stated as a scope limit); on the options-stripped WorldSense arm and any free-form arm, the bidirectional semantic-equivalence judge, whose agreement band is established on the carved split first and whose disagreement rate is reported as the estimator noise floor beneath which no non-regression claim is made. DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: R4 uses MCQ gold for the ceiling row only, fenced from the loop; on the free-form arm the oracle row is replaced by a human-free upper reference (best-of-the-two under the equivalence judge, scored by the offline rubric) explicitly labelled a substitute with its own error band, following the donor discipline of demoting a proxy row to a named diagnostic rather than reading it as a live gain. ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the guarantee is stated and verified PER CONDITION BUCKET, because a band calibrated on clean audio does not constrain noisy audio; the report gives, per bucket, coverage (how often the selector fires) and net advantage, and the guarantee is claimed only for buckets where the calibration split had adequate support — buckets with unseen-condition mass are reported as UNCERTIFIED rather than passed.
- 成本与方差：The selector's own cost is the price of the guarantee and must be a column: extra LALM calls for resampling, extra tokens, wall-clock and dollars, reported as accuracy-per-call and as a Pareto point (CATTS's format — 47.9% at 405K tokens vs 43.2% at 920K, a 56% token reduction WITH a 4.7-point gain — is the shape to aim for; a guarantee that costs 2× calls for +0 mean and no regression is still a legitimate but differently-stated result). ≥3 seeds, 5 on judge-bearing arms, bootstrap CIs, judge noise floor ~1.1 pt stated up front. Because the expected effect is a REMOVAL of negative buckets rather than a mean gain, the pre-registered primary endpoint is 'no bucket covering ≥10% of items is negative beyond CI', not the aggregate delta.
- 击杀条件：KILL if the selector cannot beat the random-among-two null on the carved-out 800 — a live outcome, since CATTS's own arbiter is worth ~+0.4 unconditionally and is actively harmful on high-consensus steps. KILL if enforcing non-regression costs more mean accuracy than the regression it removes (the FinTabNet shape, where do-nothing dominates every judge policy), reported honestly as a negative result and a named structural blind spot, following 2604.18860's discipline of publishing its ~0% cell. KILL if the equivalence estimator's disagreement rate exceeds the size of the regression being repaired, since the guarantee is then unmeasurable on our carriers.

**P 槽位**：P6 (integrated system) primary; P9 (decision calibration / admissibility) secondary. Split with D5.3/P8 (A1, binding): D4.3 owns the non-regression ARCHITECTURE — the explicit selection right over {direct readout, post-evidence answer} as a system property, plus the per-bucket harm-reporting artifact; D5.3/P8 narrows to reward-diagnostic-driven REPAIR policy and explicitly DEFERS its abstain/no-regression gate to D4.3 (architecture seat) and P9 (margin calibration).【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

## D4.4 D4-4 证据状态契约（evidence-state design for speech）

**定义**：The static data contract of the shared state: what an evidence atom is, what fields it carries (source tier, claim class, provenance id, age, verification status, acoustic-condition key), which component may read or write which field, who commits, and how contradictions are preserved rather than adjudicated early. Within-query state only — cross-query promotion of verified claims is D2/P3.

**语音域状态**：OCCUPIED in fragments, ABSENT as a contract (read-set-scoped, cut 2026-07-26). 2605.28480 Audio-Mind is the richest instance: an explicit shared evidence state (audio artifact list with stable ids audio_0/derived-with-provenance, evidence log, tool-call history, planner trace), a PERCEPTION-vs-TRANSFORMATION tool dichotomy where transformation output is NOT evidence but a better input for later perception, a per-tool 'boundary' contract naming which claim class each tool may support (a transcript verifies lexical content but not speaker identity; tempo/chord estimates are measurements, not music-theoretic judgments), a neutral evidence summary that preserves contradictions and does not adjudicate sources, and a separated format-only checker — 38 planner-visible tools in 6 categories. 2607.11433 Omni-Decision supplies the commit discipline: 'Planning, verification, repair, finalization, and insufficient stopping read the same state view, while only the reducer commits normalized events to the state', with an enumerable action vocabulary {ground, verify, compute, reground, answer, stop_insufficient, revise_answer} — but its core is NOT a frozen speech model (perception backend gemini-3.1-pro, planner gpt-5.2) and its genuinely frozen-omni cell is the weakest in the paper (11.39). 2603.17822 contributes the corroborated (0.80-0.95) / source-specific (0.50-0.70) / disagreement trichotomy and the confidence×relevance product. 2606.15141 contributes the [UNAVAILABLE] placeholder-over-hallucination convention for failed tool calls. ABSENT: any state contract carrying evidence AGE, an acoustic-condition key, or a verification-status field with defined transitions; and no paper reports what happens when the contract is violated.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED, and it holds the two fields speech lacks. 2604.18860 (TOCTOU / PUSV) makes AGE a first-class inadmissibility criterion: it measures and reports the observation-to-action gap distribution (mean 6.51 s, sd 3.59, range 3.18-13.23 over 10 real desktop tasks) BEFORE claiming a staleness gate is needed, formalizes Visual Atomicity Violation (Def.1 — name the state, the intended target, and the substitution that constitutes a violation), and argues for deterministic cheap checks over a second model call with an explicit latency budget (0.1 s vs ~5 s). 2607.08147 Prismata contributes structural provenance labels with monotone degradation. 2606.11078 contributes the MACRO-ACTION HISTORY as a compressed state write (progress summary plus last-action-result, recursively merged) kept separate from the raw trace. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: all three rest on a re-samplable static observation with structural ancestry; a speech state can only re-verify a DERIVED artifact (transcript, diarization, VAD segmentation), which reintroduces the stochastic model whose non-determinism the GUI defenses were designed to avoid and confounds staleness with model variance. What DOES transfer is the framing — evidence age is measurable and a signal can be declared inadmissible on age alone, independent of content.

**文本域对照**：PARTIAL, mostly as negative space. 2504.21776 WebThinker's document memory M is accumulate-everything with top-k similarity retrieval, no write-gate, no utility score and no poisoning defense despite ingesting arbitrary web pages — the donor's own improvement space names this. 2607.11433's reducer discipline (text-planner-driven) is the closest positive text-side occupant and is counted above. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: WebThinker's memory is keyed by text similarity over crawled pages, whereas a speech evidence state must be keyed by acoustic condition, which is not recoverable from query text — exactly the field the donor cannot supply.

**开放增量**：Specify and instrument a speech evidence-state contract in which every atom carries (source tier, claim class permitted by the producing tool, provenance id to a specific audio span, age since the span was last observed, verification status, acoustic-condition key), with a single-writer reducer, contradiction preservation, and MECHANICAL fencing of gold-consuming instruments from the state. Then measure whether the contract is load-bearing by ablating fields rather than asserting it — no paper in the read set ablates any evidence-state field.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY: MMAR 1000 (T2, metadata_only) because 2605.28480 and 2603.17822 both instantiate evidence states there, giving field-level comparability. SECONDARY, chosen to exercise the age and provenance fields that MCQ does not: MSU-Bench (T2, metadata_only; multi-talker conversational — speaker-attribution claims are exactly where a claim-class boundary bites) and MCIF long-form (T2, metadata_only; up to 15-minute segments, where 'when was this span last observed' becomes non-trivial). CONTROL carrier for the claim-class contract: SpeakerSleuth (T2, needs_download, released, 1,818 human-verified instances across Bazinga 636 / AMI 138 / Behavior-SD 477 / DailyTalk 567) — a contract that forbids a transcript tool from supporting a speaker-identity claim should measurably change behaviour there, and T3 records that LALM judges collapse toward text when interlocutor turns are given as text, which is the failure the contract targets. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION: the LongShOTBench index build (T2, metadata_only; 188 h at 5 FPS plus Whisper plus AF3 is a real GPU bill — stage-gated, duration-stratified subset only).
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3) as the LOAD-BEARING perception/answer component【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 and a local open text LLM as the planner/reducer. Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B (llama.cpp lane) is kept for the matched-backbone verification/replication cell only and is never load-bearing. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT on that lane: cheap verification only — the contract's value must not be inferred from a weak core's behaviour alone, so the field-ablation result is reported as 'on this core, under this contract', with 2512.16978's orchestrator-delta span (+12.95 to +38.52) and 2512.23646's brain-swap span (82.7 / 75.9 / 68.7) cited as the reason a single-core conclusion is not portable.
- 基线阶梯：R1 DIRECT READOUT: no state at all, single call. R2 RANDOM-AMONG-N over the atoms the state would have surfaced (a random evidence subset of the same size fed to the same answerer) — the state-specific null separating 'the right evidence' from 'more evidence', modelled on the RANDOM-EPISODIC-CONTEXT null used in the memory donor lane. R3 CONSENSUS-ONLY: a flat unstructured evidence dump (no tiers, no claim classes, no provenance, no age) into the same answerer at matched token budget — the honest 'the contract does nothing' opponent. R4 ORACLE: gold-selected evidence subset, ceiling only, gold-consuming, with recovery reported. PUBLISHED ANCHORS: 2605.28480's conditionality profile (31.2% zero-tool exits, mean 1.68 tool calls, targeted re-listening on only 50/999 questions with 64 re-listens total, 6 hallucinated tool-name calls across 4 questions rejected by the executor) as the behaviour a contract must reproduce or beat; 2603.17822's Table 5 waste statistics (energy dynamics 59.3% irrelevant over 784 invocations, spectral 67.8% over 736, speaker count 90.3% over 394, tempo 95.5%, rhythm 100%, 4 of 25 tools never fired) as the measured cost of an unconstrained state; 2607.11433's per-stratum accuracies (60.4% / 41.5% / 21.7%) as the outcome-stratification format.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL state fields: tool identity and its declared claim class, provenance id with span offsets, wall-clock/turn age, corroboration count across independent producers, contradiction flags, the producing model's self-reported uncertainty, and an acoustic-condition key derived from a deployment-available front end (SNR estimate, overlap detector, VAD statistics) — none consumes gold. OFFLINE-ONLY: any field whose value derives from the gold CoT or gold answer, including any MMAR-Rubrics-derived quality score; such fields may parameterize the contract ONCE on the carved calibration split and are then mechanically excluded. ILLEGAL: 2512.16978's rubric verifier as an in-loop field — gold-grounded by construction, and the T1 legality census records it as EVAL-ONLY-GOLD for scoring with the in-loop verify_claim tool as a separate gold-free sub-clause.
- 度量与门：BINDING: G3 (the random-evidence-subset null and the flat-dump null are both mandatory), G5 (report how often each field actually changed a decision — a claim-class boundary that never blocks anything is decoration), G6 (harm tail: a contract that suppresses a correct minority claim is a specific harm class and must be counted, not averaged away), G7 (per-bucket by tool-call depth and by condition), G9 (mechanical gold fencing is the contract's own load-bearing property, so it is both a gate and an object of test), G11. FIELD-ABLATION GRID as the primary artifact: leave-one-field-out over {tier, claim class, provenance id, age, verification status, condition key}, each measured on answer accuracy AND on behaviour (tool-call distribution, re-listen rate, contradiction-preservation rate), following the discipline that a claimed contract must be shown to change WHERE effort goes and not only the score. Accuracy paired with the MMAR-Rubrics process score; on MSU-Bench and MCIF, task metric paired with a semantic-equivalence judge.
- 三件设计件：ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: the field-ablation grid compares answers across arms, so equivalence is the unit — exact match on MCQ (scope limit stated), bidirectional semantic-equivalence judge elsewhere with its agreement band published; additionally, ATOM-LEVEL equivalence (do two tools assert the same claim?) is itself an estimator, and its false-merge rate is measured on a hand-audited sample of the carved split before any corroboration count is trusted. DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: there is no per-atom ground truth; substitutes are the gold-free rationality-audit style entailment check (is this claim supported by the atom it cites?) applied offline as the auditor of provenance integrity, plus 2606.15141's [UNAVAILABLE] convention so a missing atom is distinguishable from a hallucinated one. ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the condition key is a first-class contract field, not decoration, and the falsifiable prediction is that keying tool trust to condition (an ASR atom's tier degrades under low SNR / high overlap) changes decisions measurably in the hard buckets and not in the easy ones; a uniform delta across buckets means the key is inert and it is dropped.
- 成本与方差：State construction has a real cost — 2603.17822's 8-10 min/sample is the incumbent's, driven largely by unconstrained tool invocation. Report tool calls, LALM calls, tokens, wall-clock and dollars per arm, with the contract's own overhead separated from the answering cost. A cheap hypothesis worth testing is the incumbent's own: 'a non-reasoning LLM could likely handle most pipeline stages with minor accuracy loss' (2603.17822 §4.2), an untried cost-reduction lane with a stated hypothesis and no experiment. ≥3 seeds; 5 on any judge-bearing field; bootstrap CIs; field-ablation cells will be small and small-N cells (n=8-13 in MMAR subcategories) are flagged as under-powered rather than interpreted.
- 击杀条件：KILL a field if leave-one-out changes neither accuracy (beyond CI) nor behaviour (tool-call distribution, contradiction-preservation rate) — an inert field is removed and the removal is reported. KILL the whole contract if the flat-dump null (R3) matches the contract at matched token budget, which would say the structure is not load-bearing on our core. KILL the condition-key field specifically if its effect is uniform across condition buckets. Any of these is reported as a named structural blind spot rather than omitted.

**P 槽位**：P6 (integrated system) primary; explicit seam to P3 — the same atom schema is what cross-instance promotion would later consume, with D2 owning the promotion and D4 owning the schema. Imports P9 (D5.2 E1 admissibility gate / G1-G11) for the condition-key tool-trust degradation calibration, matching D1.1/D1.3: D4.4 supplies the degradation STATISTIC, P9/D5.2 owns the threshold's calibration.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

## D4.5 D4-5 溯源守卫：音频证据的锚定、注入与通道必要性防护

**定义**：Static provenance guarding inside one query: which component is allowed to see what (anti-anchoring), how an untrusted or model-authored evidence source is confined so it cannot silently drive the decision, and how an answer derived through a channel the item forbids is refused. Covers the guard's construction and its paired security/utility accounting; excludes cross-query poisoning of a persistent store (D2/P3).

**语音域状态**：PARTIAL, with the load-bearing ablation missing, and ABSENT as a threat model (read-set-scoped, cut 2026-07-26). 2603.17822 TalTech makes provenance guarding first-class: the reasoning agent NEVER sees the LALMs' answer predictions (explicit anti-anchoring / anti-sycophancy, citing Turpin and Sharma); a non-dismissal policy keeps single-source LALM claims 'speculative' rather than 'hallucinated' unless actively contradicted by reproducible tool evidence; four named logical pitfalls are checked (absence-of-detection as proof of absence, dismissing single-source claims, diarization over-segmentation, non-overlapping transcription disagreement); and selection is separated from elaboration so the model cannot commit to a narrative before weighing evidence. BUT the answers-visible ablation row does not exist anywhere in the paper (Table 6 contains only the two single-source rows), so the load-bearing provenance-guard decision is UNVERIFIED by its own authors' evidence. 2605.28480 contributes provenance ids and the treat-the-caption-as-evidence-not-ground-truth rule. 2605.08762 documents the channel-necessity failure directly: image-text items fail by SILENTLY FALLING BACK to text search when image search fails, and the benchmark labels which items require visual evidence — so a refusal right is constructible and is not built. 2606.15141's [UNAVAILABLE] convention prevents a failed tool from becoming a hallucinated claim. ABSENT across all 14: any adversarial threat model for audio evidence, any confinement mechanism, any paired security/utility accounting.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED and formally sharp. 2607.08147 Prismata: CONTEXTUAL LEAST PRIVILEGE as a transparent layer operating on the CAPTURED observation; Definition 2.1 (Injection-Free Label) states the design principle we need verbatim — the DECISIVE labeling step must be computed from an input the adversary cannot reach (a restricted view / critical path) rather than trusting a model to resist injection; Biba-style monotone degradation (labels can only DECREASE in privilege, locked once resolved, no-read-down / no-write-up) with capability = min of two mechanisms so it survives an imperfect labeler; allowed-element precision/recall/F1 reported together with the explicit reading that low precision means over-permissiveness and low recall means over-restrictiveness; ASR falls 85.5%→0.7% while benign task success moves 29.9%→26.6% (a ~3.3-point utility tax); and defense_harness() as a VARIANCE-CONTROLLED replay evaluation holding base-agent nondeterminism fixed. 2607.04334 independently converges on the same reporting discipline (a defense cannot win by refusing everything) with a four-arm mitigation comparison on identical probes and an explicit queries-per-action cost column. 2606.10742 MemVenom defines the adversary. TRANSFER BOUNDARY, stated by the donors themselves: Prismata's confinement rests on the DOM giving free near-deterministic structural provenance; a waveform has no structural ancestry, so 'who authored this segment' is at best a diarization/speaker-verification ESTIMATE with its own error rate, and mechanical confinement (remove the untrusted node) has no audio analogue because audio cannot be partially masked with the same guarantee. FAVOURABLE: the architecture is deterministic code interposed on every action, needing no weights, gradients, hidden states or logprobs — contract-compatible even where the substrate is not.

**文本域对照**：PARTIAL. 2504.21776 WebThinker ingests arbitrary web pages into an ungated document memory — negative space that names the gap. 2606.07834 (Cherry-pick Override) supplies the separation principle (verdict generation vs commitment authorization as distinct components, with NO-COMMIT as a routing state rather than an output label) and the finding that three-judge majority voting AMPLIFIES unsafe directional commitment, with 48% (13/27) of unsafe commits being 2-vs-1 cases where a single judge produced the safe verdict and was outvoted. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: text-domain aggregation results; the amplification mechanism is a hypothesis for speech, not a finding.

**开放增量**：Two concrete scoped guards for audio evidence: (1) RESTRICTED-VIEW LABELING — the decisive admissibility label for an evidence atom is computed from a view that excludes the component whose anchoring we fear, starting with the missing answers-visible ablation TalTech never ran, executed as an explicit two-arm experiment; (2) CHANNEL-NECESSITY REFUSAL — for items whose answer requires a specific evidence channel (acoustic vs lexical), the plane refuses an answer derived only through the other channel, instrumented against 2605.08762's documented silent-fallback failure. Both reported with Prismata-shaped paired accounting: guard effectiveness AND benign utility tax in the same table.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY for the anti-anchoring arm: MMAR 1000 (T2, metadata_only) — the incumbent's own arena, where the missing ablation is defined. PRIMARY for the channel-necessity arm: a self-constructed probe set carved from MMAR plus SpeakerSleuth (T2, needs_download, released) and ParaPairAudioBench (T2, needs_download, released; 5,175 pairs with an explicit Tie/abstain label, per-criterion Style 1,440 / Rate 375 / Emphasis 860 / Age 1,500 / Gender 1,000) — the carriers where an answer reachable through the lexical channel alone is demonstrably wrong, and where T3 records the exact failure they expose (LALM judges collapse toward text when interlocutor turns are given as text; ParaPair judges lag humans by ~32 pp with Tie-accuracy 18-19% on Style/Emphasis against human 85.3/76.0). METHODOLOGY DONOR, not measurement surface: 2605.08762's 4-stage leakage filter (joint audio+question, question-only, first-hop entity leakage, modality necessity) is adopted verbatim as the construction discipline for our probe set — a rigorous reusable answer to our own information-boundary discipline. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION: none required for the core arms; Omni-DeepSearch 640 (needs_download, open-web drift) only as a contract donor.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3) as the LOAD-BEARING evidence producer AND as the anchored component whose predictions are hidden or shown【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】; a separate local text model as the arbiter, so 'hidden' is architecturally enforceable rather than prompt-enforced. Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B (llama.cpp lane) is kept for the two-point capability contrast / feasibility cell only and is never load-bearing. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT on that lane: cheap verification only — anchoring susceptibility is plausibly core-dependent (2607.04334 found scaling 7B→30B repaired one failure and WORSENED another), so a single-core anchoring result is charted, not established.
- 基线阶梯：R1 DIRECT READOUT: frozen core answers alone, hence no anchoring surface — the reference for what the guard protects. R2 RANDOM-AMONG-N: random choice among the atoms/answers the guard would have admitted, i.e. the guard's decisions replaced by coin flips at matched admission rate — the fair null, since a null that cannot mechanically move the metric is not a control. R3 CONSENSUS-ONLY: admit everything and decide by unweighted vote (the un-guarded plane). R4 ORACLE: gold-selected admission, ceiling only, gold-consuming. PUBLISHED ANCHORS: 2603.17822's 8.5% figure (tool evidence changed 85/1000 answers, overriding both LALMs' individual predictions) is the ceiling estimate on how far an external-evidence channel can move a frozen core on this benchmark, hence the ceiling on what a provenance guard can be worth there; 2603.17822's reliability numbers (agreement 94.5/83.2/58.0; confidence bins 91.1/74.4/39.4) are the guard's feature basis; 2605.08762's oracle decomposition (entity-only 33.76 vs end-to-end 43.44 vs gold-entity 50.00) is the format for reporting how much of a lift is grounding versus downstream.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL: restricted-view admissibility labels computed from (producing tool identity, declared claim class, provenance span, corroboration by an independent producer) with the anchored component's ANSWER deliberately excluded from the label's input — the exclusion is the mechanism and it consumes no gold. Channel-necessity signals: whether an atom's provenance span carries acoustic evidence versus transcribed lexical content only. OFFLINE-ONLY: the modality-necessity LABEL of each probe item (which channel is required) is constructed once with the leakage-filter pipeline and is EVAL-ONLY — it may not be read by the guard at deployment, which must infer channel provenance from atom metadata alone. ILLEGAL: any guard consulting the item's gold answer or its necessity label in loop (G9, mechanically fenced with a logged file-open hook).
- 度量与门：BINDING: G3 (random-admission null mandatory), G4 (the guard is an evaluator of atoms, so its tie rate, rank reproducibility and positional bias are measured), G5 (report the ACTUAL admission/refusal counts — a guard that refuses 3 of 1000 is a no-op, and conversely a guard refusing 60-77% must be reported as such, following the four-arm mitigation table's certificate arm), G6 (harm accounting: refusals that suppressed a correct answer counted separately from refusals that prevented an error), G9, G10 (self-bias: the arbiter must not be the same model instance as the evidence producer, and if it is, SelfGap is measured). PAIRED REPORTING, non-negotiable: guard effectiveness AND benign utility tax in the same table with a cost column in extra calls per item — two independent donors converge on this discipline and a defense that wins by refusing everything is scored as a failure. Answer accuracy paired with the MMAR-Rubrics process score; on ParaPair/SpeakerSleuth, accuracy paired with an explicit abstain/Tie-accuracy column, since T1's ladder-level fact is that abstain/Tie calibration is the near-universal LALM-judge failure mode.
- 三件设计件：ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: anchoring is measured as 'did hiding the core's prediction change the final answer?', which needs equivalence — exact match on MCQ arms, semantic-equivalence judge on free-form arms with its agreement band published, and a pre-registered minimum detectable change (given 2603.17822's 8.5% answer-change ceiling, the anchoring arm is powered for effects at or above a few percent and is declared under-powered below that). DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: there is no ground truth for 'was this atom trustworthy'; substitutes are (a) PLAUSIBLE-ERROR SYNTHESIS adapted from the GUI critic donor as a MUTATION-TESTING protocol — perturb a known-good atom into a taxonomy-guided plausible error and check the guard catches it — and (b) the INTENT-MASKING construct reused as a test rather than as training data: a guard that can read the producer's own account of what it did will stop checking the evidence, so the guard is evaluated both with and without the producer's stated rationale visible. ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the guard's admission thresholds and its measured false-refusal rate are reported per condition bucket, because the donor lane's shared boundary states an FP rate measured on clean audio does not constrain noisy audio; a guard certified only on clean buckets is reported as UNCERTIFIED elsewhere.
- 成本与方差：Guard cost is measured in extra model calls per item and in queries-per-action terms (the four-arm mitigation table's cost-column format), plus tokens, wall-clock and dollars. The deterministic-check argument from the TOCTOU donor applies as design pressure: prefer a cheap deterministic admissibility check to a second model call where one exists, and state the latency comparison explicitly. ≥3 seeds, 5 on any judge-bearing arm; paired McNemar on the 1000 items for the anchoring arm since it is strictly paired (same items, same evidence, one field hidden); variance-controlled replay (fix the upstream trajectory, vary only what the guard sees) is adopted as the cheapest ablation instrument, matching the argumentation-level REPLAY methodology the incumbent already used.
- 击杀条件：KILL the anti-anchoring arm if hiding the core's predictions changes fewer answers than the equivalence estimator's own noise floor — which would retroactively support the incumbent's unverified 'the effect is small in this setting' and is a legitimate publishable negative. KILL the channel-necessity guard if its false-refusal rate on correct answers exceeds the error rate it prevents, or if atom-level channel provenance cannot be inferred from deployment-available metadata (the guard would then need the necessity label, making it illegal). KILL the security framing entirely if no realizable audio-side adversary can be specified within our contract — the surviving scope is then anti-anchoring and channel necessity only, restated as an internal-integrity guard rather than a security guard, with the security gap reported as a named structural blind spot.

**P 槽位**：P6 (integrated system) primary; P3 seam for the adversary model. Imports P9 (D5.2 E1 admissibility gate / G1-G11) for the per-bucket admission thresholds / false-refusal certification calibration, matching D1.1/D1.3: D4.5 supplies the admission/refusal STATISTIC, P9/D5.2 owns the threshold's calibration.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 NEW_SLOT_CANDIDATE flag: if the portfolio wants provenance/injection guarding for non-text evidence as a first-class slot rather than a P6 sub-item, this is the natural nucleus — strong vision donors (2607.08147, 2607.04334, 2606.10742) and zero speech occupancy in the read set

## D4.6 D4-6 预算归一化的系统比较与 headroom 分解（compute-normalized ARC restatement）

**定义**：The static comparison protocol itself: making cost a first-class column, restating published system comparisons at matched call count / matched token budget, and decomposing the reported gap into what a selection layer could ever recover versus what is single-draw noise or core-capped. This is how a composition claim is stated, not a mechanism.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT. The absence is self-stated by 2603.17822 (concedes 8-10 minutes per sample and that much of it is avoidable) — that is the right self-stated citation for this clause. The ARC-leaderboard gap itself is recorded as an OBSERVED (not author-stated) limitation of 2602.14224 in the D4 dossier: the ARC leaderboard carries no cost/latency/call-count axis, i.e. a 1-call model and an 8-10-minute 40-tool pipeline are ranked on the same line; 2602.14224's fulltext contains no lettered limitations list and no occurrence of "cost", "latency" or "ranked on the same line", so this is not quoted as the paper's own words.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 2606.07264 reports no cost, latency or call count anywhere despite running 2 LALMs × 3 samples plus a captioner plus FlexSED plus a 235B VLM over five visualizations. 2512.16978 reports no cost, latency, token or index-build-time accounting for its agent, so its +2.59 has an unknown gain-per-compute. 2511.02834 reports latency only on a mixed API/local deployment, not comparable across rows. The single positive instance is 2512.23646, which publishes tokens (8.3k input), latency (71 s) and dollars ($0.05-0.11/question) plus a comparison against DVD (18.6k, 104 s, ~10× cost on long videos). HEADROOM DECOMPOSITION is likewise near-absent: only 2605.08762 publishes an oracle decomposition (entity-only 33.76 / end-to-end 43.44 / gold-entity 50.00), only 2602.13685 publishes a single-tool oracle (66.1 vs vanilla 50.0), and 2606.09925 has no oracle/headroom row at all (flagged gap in T2/T3). Core-capped slices are visible but unaccounted: Imagination sits at exactly 60.00 for Step-Audio-R1, Qwen3-Omni-Thinking, VISA AND VISA-without-routing — four systems identical, unremarked by the paper.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED as protocol. 2602.12276 CATTS supplies the accuracy-vs-token Pareto framing with token count as the priced resource (47.9% at 405K tokens vs majority vote's 43.2% at 920K — a 56% token reduction WITH a 4.7-point gain) and the saturation baseline as an explicit BASELINE_ANCHOR (uniform per-step scaling buys +0.2% for 2× tokens, so 'spend more uniformly' is a weak opponent and the gated version is the real one to beat). 2606.28864 charts the regime boundaries of test-time scaling for VLMs. 2607.09438 is the strongest negative result in the donor set for reward-guided selection — 'more tokens, fewer trees': a trained multimodal PRM and a training-free generative critic both fail to beat majority voting, with the PRM regressing −2.16 pp in the high-confidence stratum (48 corrections against 119 regressions). 2605.24785 PANDO supplies a four-term per-task cost identity with an overhead ratio and the discipline of marking a term 'unreported' rather than omitting it. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: all magnitudes are vision/GUI numbers; what transfers is the denominator discipline and the Pareto reporting format, not the crossover points.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED and formally strongest. 2607.03436 decomposes the router-to-oracle gap as G = G_rec + G_noise (assumption-free algebra, Jensen on the convex max), proves every single-commit selector is capped at the reproducible oracle O^repro while the same per-query budget spent as best-of-K on the committed model provably dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle, gives the winner's-curse bound 0 ≤ E[max p̂] − max p ≤ sqrt(ln K / 2k), and imposes PRE-REGISTERED ASSUMPTION GATES that block magnitude reporting on failure. Its critical negative is directly relevant: on MATH-500 verifier-free majority vote reaches 0.824, BELOW the reproducible ceiling 0.837 — self-consistency does not beat committing to the best model there. 2604.14853 supplies the dual-variable-as-price framing. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: p̂ requires a binary correctness label per (query, model, draw); with a proxy judge in that seat the judge's variance is absorbed into G_noise and silently inflates the irrecoverable floor, and the k≥20 draws requirement is PER STRATUM, so an acoustic-condition-keyed decomposition costs k × strata draws.

**开放增量**：A compute-normalized restatement of the incumbent comparison on a shared benchmark — what does one frozen core at N samples reach for the same call count as a multi-model plane? — paired with a headroom decomposition that separates control-liftable from core-capped slices before any wrapper gain is claimed. Both are cheap, both are named as missing by the field's own papers, and both are reporting contributions rather than mechanisms.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：PRIMARY: MMAR 1000 (T2, metadata_only) — the benchmark where the unpriced comparison lives, and where the full 16-row and 14-row leaderboards give a distribution rather than a top line, so our restatement can be positioned as a percentile rather than a point. SECONDARY: MMAU test-mini 1k (T2, metadata_only) for a second, cheaper cost curve. THIRD, for core-capped accounting: the 16 MMAR subcategories with their N values, flagging n=8-13 cells as under-powered. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION: none — this sub-direction is deliberately acquisition-free, which is part of its appeal as an early deliverable. Explicitly NOT measurement surfaces: Omni-DeepSearch (needs_download, open-web drift; our lane scores 6.56/3.44 ≈ 42 of 640 items, so a cost curve there measures noise) and LongShOTBench (index build is a stage-gated GPU bill).
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3, open weight) as the LOAD-BEARING single frozen core whose N-sample curve is the compute-matched opponent to the multi-model plane【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】. Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B (llama.cpp lane) is kept as a second, quantized verification point only and is never load-bearing — our lane is quantized and slow, so absolute wall-clock on it is not comparable to published numbers and only CALL COUNT and TOKEN COUNT are used as normalization axes there, with wall-clock reported for completeness and flagged non-comparable. ACCURACY IS ALSO FLAGGED NON-COMPARABLE ACROSS REGIMES, stated in the same place as wall-clock: our single-core curve is measured on our own served weights in our own environment and is not directly comparable to the multi-model plane's PUBLISHED hosted-core accuracy numbers, which are cited and never re-derived; any crossing claim (击杀条件 below) is qualified by this regime gap unless we also reproduce at least one published plane on our own stack.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】
- 基线阶梯：R1 DIRECT READOUT: 1 call, the origin of every cost curve. R2 RANDOM-AMONG-N: random pick among N samples at each budget point — the null that isolates how much of a scaling curve is selection versus sampling, exactly the separation the routing-gap decomposition demands. R3 CONSENSUS-ONLY: unweighted majority at each N (the verifier-free aggregation ceiling; note the donor's critical negative that majority vote can sit BELOW the reproducible ceiling). R4 ORACLE: two rows, not one — single-draw oracle and reproducible oracle over k≥20 repeated draws, with the noise share reported and never a single-draw union. PUBLISHED ANCHORS to restate at matched budget: 2606.07264 VISA 77.40 Acc / 66.23 Rubrics at (2 LALMs × K=3 + captioner + FlexSED + a 235B VLM over five visualizations); 2603.17822 76.90 / 69.83 at (2 LALMs × 4 queries + a large reasoning LLM + up to 5 tool rounds, 8-10 min/sample); direct-readout anchors 58.41/69.90 and 58.76/71.50 at 1 call; white-box single-model 62.55/71.00; random guess 29.32. The deliverable is the restated table, not a new system.
- 信号与合法性：This sub-direction consumes no in-loop signal — it is a reporting protocol. Legality still binds on the oracle rows: R4 is gold-consuming, reported as a ceiling only and never as an achievable number, with the recovery fraction (realized/oracle) as the headline. MMAR-Rubrics is used as a REPORTING metric on the untouched split and, once, offline on the carved calibration split to check whether a deployment-legal proxy tracks it — that one-time calibration is the only legal use, since T2 and T3 both record its answer-gated gold-CoT dependence. Judge-run variance (~1.1 pt) is stated as the resolution floor of the Rubrics axis before any cost-curve crossing is claimed.
- 度量与门：BINDING: G1 (headroom row plus recovery fraction is the whole point), G2 (the direct-readout floor is the origin of the cost curve), G3 (random-among-N and consensus-only are the two curves every claim is plotted against), G7 (per-subcategory decomposition into control-liftable versus core-capped BEFORE any aggregate claim — the four-way tie at 60.00 on Imagination is the template), G11 (report headroom under at least two metric families on the SAME output: accuracy and Rubrics on MMAR; the standing caveat is that token-level metrics under-report wrapper gains on speech, e.g. Rubrics +6.9 against accuracy +1.5, with the inverse risk on transcription tasks). PRIMARY ARTIFACTS: (a) accuracy-vs-calls and accuracy-vs-tokens Pareto plots with all four ladder rows; (b) a headroom decomposition table separating single-draw noise from reproducible advantage, with pre-registered assumption gates that BLOCK magnitude reporting on failure; (c) a per-subcategory control-liftable / core-capped classification.
- 三件设计件：ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: the reproducible-oracle estimate needs repeated draws to be scored, so on MCQ arms equivalence is exact match; the design deliberately confines the k≥20 resampling arm to MCQ carriers for this reason and states that extending it to free-form outputs would put the semantic-equivalence judge in the correctness seat, whose variance would be absorbed into the noise floor and inflate it — declared a scope limit rather than papered over. DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: MCQ gold provides R4 on the MCQ arms; where no gold exists the oracle row is replaced by a named diagnostic ceiling (best-of-arms under the equivalence judge) explicitly demoted from a live gain, following the donor discipline of demoting an offline benchmark to a named consistency diagnostic with a warning against reading it as a live gain. ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the k≥20 requirement is PER STRATUM, so the decomposition is budgeted as k × condition buckets and the design states up front which buckets it can afford (a stratified 300-item sub-split at k=20 rather than the full 1000), with under-supported buckets reported as UNCERTIFIED rather than pooled.
- 成本与方差：Cost IS the object here: mandatory columns are LALM calls, tool calls, total tokens, wall-clock and dollars, with any term marked 'unreported' rather than omitted. Variance: k≥20 draws for the reproducible-oracle estimate with fixed per-draw seeds; ≥3 seeds for every curve point; bootstrap CIs on all crossings; the winner's-curse bias bound is quoted alongside every max-over-noisy-estimates figure so the oracle row is not overstated; the judge-noise floor (~1.1 pt) is drawn on the Rubrics axis as a band.
- 击杀条件：MEASUREMENT-REGIME REQUIREMENT (precondition on the headline crossing claim): either restate BOTH sides from published numbers only (a pure re-tabulation, no local run), or run the single-core curve AND at least one reproduction of one published plane on the SAME lane, so any crossing is measured within one regime rather than comparing our locally-served accuracy against published hosted-core accuracy.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 KILL the restatement's headline if the compute-matched single-core curve MEETS or EXCEEDS the multi-model plane under that regime-matched measurement — that is a legitimate and arguably more valuable finding and is pre-registered as a reportable outcome rather than a failure (the donor lane already contains the shape: uniform scaling saturating, and a PRM losing to majority vote). KILL the reproducible-oracle arm if the pre-registered assumption gates fail (overdispersion, independence band), in which case the floor is reported but the SHARE is not. KILL any per-subcategory claim resting on n<20 cells.

**P 槽位**：P6 (integrated system) primary; P7 (validation carriers / reporting protocol) secondary; P9 (D5.1) OWNS the G_rec/G_noise decomposition machinery itself — D4.6 IMPORTS it rather than building a parallel instance (same machinery is also imported by D3-SD6; ownership designated once to avoid three independent claims on one instrument).【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

## D4.7 D4-7 验证载体设计（任务 × 时延 × 打断 × 恢复 的并集）

**定义**：What carrier can actually validate a static composition claim. Covers the arena's own construction: dual-protocol pre-registration (closed-option and options-stripped/open-ended), a risk-coverage arm so abstention is scoreable, and the union of task family × latency budget × barge-in/interruption × recovery-from-failure conditions that a deployed speech system faces but no current carrier scores. C3 rewritten.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT as a union carrier; each axis exists in isolation and none co-occurs (read-set-scoped, cut 2026-07-26). TASK axis: MMAR/MMAU are single-turn 4-way MCQA only — recorded as an OBSERVED (not author-stated) limitation of 2602.14224 in the D4 dossier: no multi-step, no open-generation, no interactive speech tasks; 2602.14224's fulltext carries no lettered limitations list of its own, so this is not quoted as its words. 2603.17822 names scaling beyond multiple choice as future work.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 ABSTENTION: structurally unscoreable under MMAR-Rubrics by construction (S = E(Y_CoT) if the answer is correct else 0 discards all correct-reasoning/wrong-answer cases and makes abstention and partial credit unmeasurable); 2605.28480's GIVE UP fired once in 999 and only from an API safety rejection; 2605.08762's UNKNOWN token exists with no coverage analysis anywhere; 2607.11433's Answer Critic actively BLOCKS abstention. LATENCY: reported by 2603.17822 (8-10 min/sample) and 2512.23646 ($0.05-0.11, 71 s) as system properties, never as a CONDITION the carrier imposes. BARGE-IN and RECOVERY: absent from every D4 carrier; the only full-duplex material in the portfolio is a judge-reliability study on an internal, not-publicly-acquirable set (T2: 209 stereo sessions, 152 full-duplex across 13 accent-condition strata plus 57 adversarial defect-injected clips, local_status = not_released / internal), plus S2S-Arena and MTalk-Bench (T2, metadata_only) which rank END SYSTEMS rather than validating a control plane. PROTOCOL CONDITIONALITY is proven: 2512.16978's options-stripped protocol moves Qwen3.5-35B 78.9→58.4 (−20.5) and Qwen3-Omni-Thinking −13.6 while the agent drops only 1.3, and on WorldSense the ordering reverses.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED and methodologically the richest donor lane for this sub-direction. 2606.25760 ARGUS defines the REGIME construct — regime = (dataset, model class, OBSERVABLE MODEL INTERFACE) — with transfer measured as Spearman ρ between per-cell method RANKINGS rather than score values; it splits an open-weight panel (27 methods × 4 agents × 4 datasets) from an API-ONLY panel restricted to the 8-method intersection surviving loss of logits/hidden states/attention, and reports cross-tier transfer honestly as non-detection (mean ρ = +0.08, 95% CI [−0.219, +0.373] includes zero, therefore 'reranked on the target calibration split rather than extrapolated'). It also separates three objectives that select different methods (discrimination / severity ranking / calibration — the same top method on only 2 of 16 cells) and insists a ranking metric is always paired with a coverage metric ('a score can rank errors well by AUROC yet produce regions that are too large, under-covered, or depend on the output interface'). 2607.04334 supplies SEVERITY CALIBRATION of synthetic interventions against a mined NATURAL divergence distribution (coverage of the natural 5th-95th band reported per measure) — the answer to 'is your perturbation representative of deployment?' — plus deterministic forced-choice scoring with no model judge and a four-arm mitigation comparison on identical probes with a cost column. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: both rest on a coordinate-valued or closed-vocabulary output with a deterministic oracle; speech has neither, and their gold-label agreement (κ 0.86 on pixel reads) must be re-established for listening annotation whose severity distribution (SNR, overlap, accent) is continuous rather than a string-distance band. Every ARGUS number is a GUI-grounding number with no cross-modality implication.

**文本域对照**：PARTIAL. 2607.03436 contributes pre-registered assumption gates that block magnitude reporting on failure. 2606.28733 (Agentic Abstention) and 2607.17136 ('Teach it to stop, not just to click') make stopping/abstention the OBJECT of study with variance decomposition rather than a side effect; 2606.12935 MARS supplies risk-controlled stopping. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: all presume an executable task oracle — the abstention carriers score a decision whose correctness is checkable, which is exactly what a free-form speech carrier must substitute for.

**开放增量**：Specify a speech validation carrier as the UNION of four axes — task family (closed-option, open-ended, long-form, interactive) × latency budget (imposed as a condition, not observed as a property) × barge-in/interruption × recovery-from-a-failed-step — with dual-protocol pre-registration and a risk-coverage arm that makes abstention scoreable. The 1C deliverable is the CARRIER SPEC plus a construction plan grounded in acquirable assets; construction itself is a stage-gated build subject to the three-question rule and is proposed, not assumed.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：ACQUIRABLE TODAY (T2 status quoted): MMAR 1000 and MMAU test-mini (metadata_only, no acquisition) for the closed-option cell; WorldSense and Daily-Omni (metadata_only) for the dual-protocol cell, run under BOTH MCQ and options-stripped scoring; MCIF test (metadata_only; long-form up to 15-minute segments, de/en/it/zh) for the open-ended and long-form cells; MSU-Bench (metadata_only; multi-talker conversational) for the interaction-adjacent cell; ParaPairAudioBench (needs_download, released; 5,175 pairs with an explicit Tie/abstain label) and SpeakerSleuth (needs_download, released; 1,818 human-verified instances across Bazinga 636 / AMI 138 / Behavior-SD 477 / DailyTalk 567) for the abstention-scoreable cell. NOT ACQUIRABLE: the full-duplex production set (T2: not_released / internal), so the barge-in axis CANNOT be inherited and is declared a NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION requiring either construction or a licensed source; S2S-Arena and MTalk-Bench (metadata_only) supply protocol design for the interactive axis but rank end systems, not control planes. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION (bulk, proposed not assumed): MOV-Bench (needs_download, request-or-rebuild) for multi-hop; LongShOTBench (metadata_only; the 188 h index is a stage-gated GPU build, duration-stratified subset only); and a barge-in/recovery probe set constructed under 2605.08762's 4-stage leakage-filter discipline.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3) is the LOAD-BEARING open core the carrier's validity is primarily argued against【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】; Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B (llama.cpp lane) is the SECOND open core, included as a cheap-verification/feasibility point and never load-bearing on its own — the carrier's validity requirement is that it separates the two AND separates each from its own wrapped version. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT on the llama.cpp lane specifically: a carrier validated only against that weak quantized core may have compressed dynamic range, so the spec requires at least one additional published-anchor core position (cited, not re-run) to argue range. ARGUS-derived requirement: the carrier's regime key must include the OBSERVABLE INTERFACE, because our contract removes logits/hidden states/attention and the surviving signal families are exactly the sampling/consistency, hybrid, verbalized and model-native ones.
- 基线阶梯：The four fixed rows are properties the CARRIER must be able to express, and each is a validity test of the carrier itself. R1 DIRECT READOUT must be expressible and must appear as a first-class row on every axis — recorded as an OBSERVED (not author-stated) limitation of 2602.14224 in the D4 dossier: no direct-readout baseline row appears in the official leaderboard tables at all, and the carrier must not repeat that. R2 RANDOM-AMONG-N must be scoreable, which requires a defined candidate set per item. R3 CONSENSUS-ONLY must be scoreable, which requires an equivalence relation — on the open-ended cells this is the carrier's hardest requirement and is where MMAR-style carriers silently fail. R4 ORACLE must be constructible offline and reportable with a recovery fraction. PUBLISHED ANCHORS the carrier must reproduce as sanity checks, EACH LABELLED BY BENCHMARK so the two delta sets are not conflated (they are different benchmarks with reversed per-model magnitudes)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】: the protocol flip ON WORLDSENSE (MCQ 54.0/52.65 vs agent 46.56, reversing options-stripped) and, SEPARATELY, the options-stripped drops ON LONGSHOTBENCH (Qwen3.5-35B −20.5 [78.9→58.4], Qwen3-Omni-Instruct −18.7, Qwen3-Omni-Thinking −13.6, agent −1.3); and the ARC leaderboard spread (agent-track floor 23.91 vs single-model floor 19.03) as the distribution a valid carrier should also produce.
- 信号与合法性：The carrier's SCORING instruments are where legality is decided. DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL as in-loop signals, hence usable by systems under test: agreement/vote counts, counts-only semantic entropy, self-reported uncertainty, MBR. OFFLINE-ONLY / EVAL-ONLY-GOLD scoring instruments, fenced from every system under test: MMAR-Rubrics (gold CoT + gold answer, answer-gated), the LongShOTBench-style rubric verifier (gold-grounded by construction — 'comparing the candidate answer against the human-validated reference answer rather than relying on the verifier's own world knowledge'), and any human-annotated reference. The carrier spec REQUIRES a mechanical firewall between the scoring stack and the system-under-test process (a logged file-open hook on the reference files), because the carrier's purpose is to make information-boundary violations detectable rather than trusted.
- 度量与门：BINDING: G2 (direct-readout row mandatory on every axis), G3 (nulls must be expressible), G4 (the carrier's own judges are measured: tie rate on distinct-output pairs, tie rate at gaps ≥ε, Kendall's W rank reproducibility, positional bias — reference failure values to beat are documented in the donor lane, e.g. W = 0.36 and 68.6% self-agreement at temperature 0), G5 (a risk-coverage arm reporting coverage AND selective accuracy, never accuracy alone — this is what MMAR-Rubrics structurally cannot do), G6 (harm tail per condition), G7 (per-axis before aggregate), G9 (mechanical fencing), G11 (two metric families on the same output). CARRIER-VALIDITY REQUIREMENTS adopted from the donor lanes: a human-alignment study for any rubric we introduce (the reliability harness is reusable — inter-rater and intra-rater Krippendorff α plus a 50-pair human preference study, with the published reference values α 0.7921 inter / 0.9216 intra), and SEVERITY CALIBRATION of any synthetic condition (latency pressure, injected barge-in, injected step failure) against a mined natural distribution with per-measure coverage of the natural 5th-95th band, so a perturbation cannot be dismissed as unrepresentative.
- 三件设计件：ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: this is the carrier's central engineering object, not an afterthought — the spec requires a documented equivalence relation per axis (exact match on closed-option; bidirectional semantic-equivalence judge on open-ended with its own agreement study; a criterion-level atomic-match rubric on long-form), each shipped with a measured agreement band and a stated minimum detectable effect, and every downstream statistic (vote counts, consensus, non-regression, oracle) defined ON TOP of that relation rather than assuming exact match. DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: the carrier names, per axis, what stands in for a deterministic oracle — gold labels on closed-option; a multi-verifier rubric ensemble on open-ended with a human-alignment argument (reference: ensemble κ 0.753 / F1 0.847 / r 0.923 against an inter-human leave-one-out reference of κ 0.672 / F1 0.799 / r 0.832); and for the barge-in and recovery axes a CONSTRUCTED oracle where the correct recovery is determined by construction, following the four-principle construction contract (audio dependence, hard-to-find/easy-to-verify, retrieval necessity, answer uniqueness) plus the news-event binding trick against parametric shortcuts. ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: the condition axis is a FIRST-CLASS stratification variable with per-stratum item counts published so under-supported strata are visibly UNCERTIFIED; the stated reason is that the carrier exists to detect condition-dependent regressions that aggregate scores hide, and that the calibration-to-deployment exchangeability assumption every donor gate relies on breaks specifically along this axis.
- 成本与方差：The carrier must publish its own cost of use — judge API spend is real (reproducing MMAR-Rubrics-style scoring needs paid frontier judge calls; the long-form rubric ensemble uses one proprietary verifier) — and the spec includes a single-verifier ablation lane so a cheaper reproduction path exists. Every reported cell carries ≥3 seeds (5 on judge-bearing cells), bootstrap 95% CIs, and the judge-run noise floor (~1.1 pt on the Rubrics axis) drawn as a band; the carrier requires that no system be quoted as a mean-with-error unless repeated runs exist, since the entire D4 read set is single-run without seeds or intervals.
- 击杀条件：KILL the barge-in axis at 1C if no acquirable or constructible source exists within the stage gate (the only known full-duplex reference set is internal and not acquirable) — the carrier then ships as task × latency × recovery and the barge-in gap is reported as a named structural blind spot rather than quietly dropped. KILL the risk-coverage arm if no scoreable abstention target can be constructed on acquirable data. KILL any axis whose equivalence estimator's agreement band is wider than the effect sizes the axis is meant to detect — given that the D4 field's wrapper effects are +1.5 to +2.6 on strong cores, this is a real threshold and it is pre-registered. KILL the whole carrier build if the stage-gate three-question test (which stage uses it / are the parameters chosen / is a subset enough) is not answered; carrier construction is a large artifact and is not exempted by being cheap.

**P 槽位**：P7 (validation carriers) primary; P6 seam (the carrier is what every D4-1..D4-6 claim is stated on); P9/P10 seam (evaluator calibration and selection-pressure measurement both need this carrier before they can be run on speech)

## D4 维度综合

RELATION AMONG THE SEVEN. D4-1 (arbitration), D4-2 (seat assignment), D4-3 (non-regression), D4-4 (evidence-state contract) and D4-5 (provenance guard) are the METHOD SURFACE of static composition; D4-6 (budget-normalized comparison) and D4-7 (validation carrier) are the REPORTING and MEASUREMENT infrastructure every one of the first five must be stated on. The five are not independent: D4-4 is the substrate — D4-1's arbiter reads evidence atoms, D4-3's guarantee selects between two answers derived from them, and D4-5's guard is a set of admissibility fields on the same atoms; D4-2 decides which component holds the pen at the end of that pipeline. Formally: D4-4 defines the state, D4-2 defines the seats, D4-1 defines the deciding function over the state, D4-3 defines the guarantee that function must satisfy, D4-5 defines what the function is forbidden to see.

ORDERING LOGIC (dependency and risk, not preference). (1) D4-2's feasibility cell runs FIRST because it is a gate, not an ablation: T3 flags tool-calling under our llama.cpp GGUF lane as UNVERIFIED, and both D4-1's matched-tool-bank premise and D4-4's tool-boundary fields collapse without it; the field's core-swap fragility evidence (brain swap 82.7 / 75.9 / 68.7; orchestrator deltas +12.95 to +38.52) is why this is a gate rather than a nuisance. (2) D4-3 runs SECOND because it is the cheapest defensible contribution and the precondition for honest reporting of everything else: the read set contains regression in the two largest buckets of the closest incumbent (566 of 999 items at −0.6 and −0.8), an unflagged −3.6, a matched-backbone −8.4, and a wrapper losing outright when the brain is weakened; reporting an aggregate delta without the per-bucket table would hide exactly the regime the program exists to fix. (3) D4-6 runs ALONGSIDE D4-3, not after, because the cost column and the oracle/recovery decomposition must be designed into the first measurement rather than retrofitted; it is also acquisition-free and therefore the lowest-friction early deliverable. (4) D4-1 runs THIRD, once the floor and the denominator exist, because "reward-decided vs hand-decided at matched tool bank and matched frozen cores" is only interpretable against a verified floor and a matched budget. (5) D4-4 and D4-5 run FOURTH and FIFTH in that order, because a provenance guard is an admissibility layer over a state contract and cannot be specified before the contract's fields exist. (6) D4-7 is specified early (its spec constrains the carriers all the others use) but BUILT late and only through the stage gate — the carrier is a large artifact and "cheap" does not exempt it.

CROSS-DIMENSION SEAMS. To D5 (improvement over time): D4 owns the STATIC allocation of a decision right and the guarantee it must satisfy; D5 owns whether the deciding function improves across items and the reward mechanism's own optimization behaviour. The seam is sharp at D4-1, where we specify the arbiter's inputs and fallback right but not its adaptation, and at D4-3, where we specify the admissibility band but not its re-fitting. To D1/P1 (supply pricing): D4-6 owns the comparison DENOMINATOR (matched call count, matched tokens) while D1 owns the per-acquisition price and value-of-information — D4 never decides whether an acquisition is worth issuing, only how the resulting system comparison is normalized. To D2/P3 (memory): D4-4 owns the within-query atom schema including the verification-status field; D2 owns promotion of verified claims across items and the write-gate/poisoning defense on a persistent store. The schema is deliberately designed so the promotion D2 may later want is expressible, but D4 makes no cross-instance claim (cross-instance memory is zero-occupancy across the ARC 1000 and the audio-agent trio, and that gap belongs to D2). To D3/P5 (skills): TalTech's Table 5 tool-waste statistics (energy dynamics 59.3% irrelevant over 784 invocations, rhythm 100%, 4 of 25 tools never fired) are a ready-made offline allocation-fitting signal belonging to D3; D4-4 uses the same tool inventory only to define claim-class boundaries. INCUMBENT-ARBITRATION SPLIT (A2, binding — D3 and D4 both name the same two incumbents as what their open increment replaces, so this must be stated once): D3 owns arbitration over TOOL OUTPUTS / evidence atoms (pre-fusion admission); D4 owns arbitration over CANDIDATE ANSWERS (post-fusion decision). TalTech's tier-scalar fusion weights and VISA's 27-class router are D4-1's incumbents (P6 replaces them); TalTech's per-tool usefulness/waste statistics above are D3's allocation evidence, not a second D4-1 incumbent. D3 does not re-replace the two systems D4-1 already targets.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 To P9/P10: D4-7 is the prerequisite carrier — evaluator calibration (P9) and selection-pressure/hacking measurement (P10) both need a speech arena with a scoreable abstention arm and a documented equivalence relation, neither of which exists in the read set today.

LEVEL AND SCOPE DISCIPLINE. Every claim above is direction-level: no novelty verdict is offered, no innovation point is locked, and every occupancy or absence statement is scoped to the stated read set (D4 dossier 9 entries + D0 quintet 5 entries + D6 donor set 73 entries, cut 2026-07-26). Donor citations retain their domain labels and none of their effect sizes is carried across modalities (H5 withheld) — the vision-GUI and text-agent material supplies protocols, constructs and cautionary nulls only. All seven designs are 1C DESIGN-ONLY: nothing here authorizes execution, model calls, dataset downloads, or the LongShOTBench / MOV-Bench / barge-in builds, all of which are marked NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION and remain subject to the stage-gate three-question rule. SHARED CALIBRATION CARVE (portfolio-level clause, matching D2/D5): MMAR's carved 200-item calibration split (D4-1, D4-3) is the SAME named, versioned, blob-registered split reused by every D4 sub-direction that touches MMAR — D4-2, D4-4, D4-5, D4-6 do not carve independent MMAR splits — and D4 maintains a single cumulative gold-exposure ledger across all seven designs, mechanically enforced by one file-open hook per carrier (2607.13347's design), so an item used as calibration in one sub-direction cannot silently become a test item in another.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

# D5 Multimodal agentic system evolution — improvement dynamics of a training-free control plane over a frozen speech/omni core (system gets stronger across instances/time without weight updates; TFRL is our chosen route to that improvement, gradient-based evolution is the zero-gradient-parity comparator)

## 维度纲领

D5's OBJECT is EVOLUTION ITSELF — the phenomenon of a multimodal agentic system getting stronger across instances/time without weight updates. Training-free RL (TFRL) is the research route we have chosen to realize that phenomenon (owner ruling, 总集成 line 12): the reward/advantage machinery described below is the CHOSEN INSTRUMENT, not the object. Gradient-based evolution (agentic RL/SFT, e.g. OmniAgent-native/TAURA) is the mirror comparator to be matched at zero gradient — "零梯度打平" is a measurable claim, carried by a TRAINED_MIRROR_COMPARATOR row in D5.5's baseline ladder, not a slogan.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 Under this instrument, D5 owns: how external reward/advantage decides the next action over a frozen black-box speech/omni core (P8), under what conditions a signal is licensed to steer at all (P9/E1), how it fails under selection pressure (P10), and how these statistics accumulate across instances and time (TFRL-style advantage from experience). Boundary (for de-duplication): as an INSTRUMENT, D5 owns only the RULE — the advantage estimator, the admissibility gate, budget/stop calibration and its re-calibration, and the measurement/detection of reward hacking. D5 does NOT own the storage side (memory schema, retrieval index, write-gate mechanics, poisoning defence = D2), observation supply and its pricing (P1 = D1), tool/skill induction and tool-level credit artifacts (P5 = D3), or system topology and single-shot orchestration (P6 = D4). Level discipline: directions, not innovation points; no novelty verdicts. Every occupancy/absence claim is scoped to the read set = D5 nine-paper dossier + d0 audit quintet + D6 donor lane (73 entries), cut date 2026-07-26; donors carry domain labels and H5 stays withheld (no cross-modality effectiveness conclusions). C1 -> E1 is the dimension's hinge: the C1 measurement contract (oracle headroom / recoverable mass / coverage / candidate-level fidelity / within-instance ranking / tie-abstain / realized recovery / harm / cost) is an OFFLINE reporting obligation; E1 promotes each accounting axis into a RUNTIME precondition thresholded per acoustic stratum — headroom axis -> D5.1's label-free estimate must exceed h*【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号：D5.1→D5.1】; fidelity axis -> in-stratum tie-rate@epsilon and Kendall's W must clear floors (G4); margin axis -> decision margin must exceed an adversarial-erosion bound AND the realized accept count must be non-trivial (G5); harm axis -> the stratum's severe-loss tail must be bounded under paired significance (G6); coverage and cost become the gate's own reporting pair (what fraction of instances were licensed, at what price). If any precondition fails, the plane must return the direct-readout incumbent (G2, default-deny).

## D5.1 部署期无标签 headroom / 可判性估计器

**定义**：Estimate, from black-box observables only (semantic-cluster entropy, MBR dispersion, judge-score dispersion, structural-check disagreement, observed saturation-N), how much attainable improvement a candidate pool contains and how much of it is reachable by a LABEL-FREE policy — before any budget is spent. This is the first gate on whether the control plane acts at all.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT (read set: D5 nine papers + d0 quintet, cut 2026-07-26). Four pieces of evidence show the quantity is evaded, not built. KIT 2606.04730 resolves it with labels (S4.7 language-level kill switch chosen post hoc from dev/test scores: reranking ON for en/zh, OFF for de/it); its oracle map is the only speech-native headroom map available (ASR -32.10 WER / SQA +14.42 / SSUM +3.85 / ST +6.11, mean +14.12; best realized Lik.+MBR +3.71 = 26% recovery; 8 of 10 configurations net worse than greedy). AudioProcessBench 2606.09925 publishes NO oracle row anywhere, so recovery fraction is not computable (generator mean 46.64%; random-aggregator floor BoN 47.5 / MV 40.6 / Avg 44.1; best critic Gemini-3-Flash chain-Avg 60.5). Omni-RRM 2602.00846 never reports the frozen generator's own pass@N ceiling, so what fraction of headroom its +2.6 (AVQA 77.7->80.3 @N=10) captures is unknown. TTS 2606.18323 names saturation-N 'a per-input difficulty signal' and 'a proxy for distillation headroom' and then runs a uniform N sweep. Closest deployment-legal precursor: 2604.25591 discrete semantic entropy (MMAU / Qwen2.5-Omni-7B AUROC 0.85 / AURAC 0.90, counts-only and legal) — but item-level correctness, not pool-level headroom, and it collapses on trustworthiness sets (P(True) 0.52 on AQUA-Bench = chance; 0.44 on AF3/Audio-Hallucination = below chance).

**视觉域对照**：PARTIAL. 2606.13156 (vision-GUI, VLM grounding) supplies the Honest-Trajectory Protocol: report the triple (m_single-shot, max_pi m_pi over a PRE-REGISTERED label-free policy set that must include do-nothing, m_oracle), foreground honest gain g and oracle gap Delta = m_or - max_pi m_pi; it then reports that none of the observables it tested recovers the oracle (expression length r~-0.14, box geometry, self-verification P(yes) r~0.22 with mean P(yes)~0.40 even on correct boxes), scoping this explicitly as 'a binding constraint, not an impossibility proof'. 2606.25760 (ARGUS, vision-GUI) supplies a UQ benchmark across VLMs and grounding datasets. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: 2606.13156's m_or rests on a crisp deterministic per-step oracle (IoU vs a GT box); speech has none, so Delta becomes a difference of two noisy quantities whose sign is undecidable at the +2.4pp scale that paper works at; its self-verification signal is P(yes) read from logits, excluded by our contract; the counterfactual-overlay probe plants a known-correct answer into the rendering channel and has no modality-matched speech analogue.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED-adjacent and the strongest donor lane. 2607.03436 (text, routing) decomposes G = G_rec + G_noise with G_noise >= 0 by Jensen; Theorem 2 gives the recoverability asymmetry (every single-commit selector is capped at O^repro while best-of-K on the committed model provably dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle: MATH-500 best-of-K 0.946 vs matched-budget pool oracle 0.874); Corollary 3 splits verifier-free aggregation ceiling O^agg from the verifier-gated residual Delta_guess; Theorem 4(b) bounds winner's-curse bias by sqrt(ln K / 2k); pre-registered assumption gates BLOCK magnitude reporting on failure; the k-sweep shows the estimate is 0 at k=1 and settles by k~20-30; critical negative — verifier-free majority vote reaches only 0.824 on MATH-500, BELOW the reproducible ceiling 0.837. 2506.18203 Weaver gives the generation-verification gap (Pass@K - success rate) as the headroom statistic plus label-free verifier-accuracy estimation from pairwise agreement (gold needed only for a scalar class prior on ~1% of items). 2512.02304 gives verifier gain = precision - solver accuracy as a cheap single-round go/no-go (r=0.892 at 10 attempts) and disqualifies verifier accuracy as a predictor. 2607.13347 (document/vision construct donor) names the terminal blocker verbatim: headroom predicts net loop utility (Spearman 0.771, n=6 generators x 100 tables) but 'cannot be measured without ground truth, so whether the loop will help a particular generator or table cannot be predicted at deployment time', and enumerates the untested observables (candidate diversity, inter-candidate agreement, self-consistency entropy, judge score dispersion). TRANSFER BOUNDARY: all are label-using or exact-match-anchored; 2607.03436 needs repeated i.i.d. draws at fixed seeds/temperature (a black-box speech API may expose neither) and its k>=20 requirement is PER STRATUM, multiplying the sampling budget by the number of acoustic strata; a proxy judge's error would be silently absorbed into G_noise, inflating the 'irrecoverable' floor.

**开放增量**：Build and validate a POOL-LEVEL, label-free headroom/decidability estimator for free-form speech outputs from deployment-legal observables only; fit and validate it offline against a labelled headroom on a self-carved split, then deploy it as the E1 headroom precondition. Two scoping decisions are load-bearing and unoccupied in the read set: (i) decompose every speech oracle we report into G_rec + G_noise before any recovery claim — KIT's +14.12 is a single-draw union over 17 candidates, exactly the inflated construction 2607.03436 warns about. D5.1/P9 is the designated OWNER of this decomposition machinery (both D4.6 and D3-SD6 import it rather than building parallel instances, so the portfolio carries one G_rec/G_noise implementation, not three).【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 (ii) report headroom under two metric families ON THE SAME OUTPUT (G11), because KIT's task x metric confound (ASR only in WER, SSUM only in BERTScore) means 'SSUM/ST have limited headroom' is not separable from metric compression.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：MCIF test set (T2, metadata_only; the 1M augmented training corpus IS released as HF YapayNet/iwslt2026-if-augmented) — the only speech-native 4-task x 4-language oracle map, primary open-output carrier. MMAU Test-mini + MMSU (T2, metadata_only, public) — closed-answer carrier where repeated draws for O^repro are affordable and answer identity is exact. LibriSpeech test-other x MS-SNSD (8 noise types @10dB SNR) + L2-Arctic (6 L1s) (T2, metadata_only) — the acoustic-stratum axis. AQUA-Bench + Audio-Hallucination (T2, metadata_only) — zero-headroom control (unanswerable items: the oracle should be flat, and this is where 2604.25591's P(True) is at/below chance). ACQUISITION FLAG for the next-step proposal: every speech carrier in T2 is metadata_only or needs_download — nothing is local — so a single bulk-acquisition item covering {MMAU-mini, MMSU, MCIF test, LibriSpeech test-clean/test-other, MS-SNSD, L2-Arctic, AQUA-Bench, Audio-Hallucination} is required. Note a discrepancy to reconcile before planning: the C1 closeout card states 'MMAU-mini/MMAR 已本地 pin' while T2 (2026-07-26) records metadata_only for both. AudioProcessBench (T2, metadata_only, release promised but URL not confirmed, CC BY-NC 4.0) flagged separately as release-pending. MUGEN is NOT in T2 and may only be named as NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (T3 A_frozen_core, open weight) as the weak-core floor generator. Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B Thinking/Instruct via the local llama.cpp GGUF lane (T3: -ngl 28 resident llama-server + input_audio) = CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY, never load-bearing; tool-call capability under GGUF is unverified per the T3 note and must be checked before any reliance. Gemini-3-Flash (T3, proprietary API, no guaranteed logprobs, snapshot drift) as a cross-family reference where budget permits. To VARY headroom by design the way 2607.13347's 6-generator sweep does, pair the omni cores against a Whisper-large-v3 + text-LLM cascade as a deliberately different-strength generator. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT (mandatory in every claim sourced from the local lane): the ATLAS 2606.01667 cross-family asymmetry is DIRECTION-UNSTABLE — two of three benchmarks favour the weak core (LiveCodeBench +12.0 vs +1.15; BabyVision +6.2 vs +2.32) but HLE-Verified reverses (strong +8.00 vs weak +5.0) — therefore no lift measured on the llama.cpp lane transfers as a prediction about a strong frozen omni core.
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows in every cell. (1) DIRECT READOUT (greedy single pass): anchors KIT 2606.04730 greedy ASR 40.77 WER / SQA 37.24 / SSUM 29.38 / ST 75.28; AudioProcessBench 2606.09925 generator mean 46.64%. (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N: anchor 2606.09925 random aggregator BoN 47.5 / MV 40.6 / Avg 44.1. (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY: anchor Self-consistency@N on AVQA 77.7 (2602.00846); 2606.09925 finding that MV beats BoN for every real critic. (4) ORACLE over the SAME pool, reported in BOTH forms per 2607.03436: O^single (single-draw union, the KIT-shaped inflated one) and O^repro (from k>=20 graded draws) with the noise share S(K); never a single-draw union alone. Published recovery anchors carried with domain labels: KIT 26% recovery (speech); 2607.13347 OmniDocBench best judge policy 27.7% recovery with >72% unrecovered, FinTabNet all judge policies NEGATIVE and always-iter0 beating every one (document/vision); 2607.03436 MATH-500 gap 10.1 pts with 36% noise share (text).
- 信号与合法性：Estimator INPUTS are all deployment-legal: MBR/chrF consensus dispersion (0 model calls, 2606.04730); discrete semantic entropy and vote-rate P(True), counts-only variants (2604.25591 — the probability-mass, predictive and length-normalized entropy variants are ILLEGAL, they need guaranteed logprobs); AudioProcessBench reference-free step-critic score dispersion (2606.09925, deployment-legal critic path); Omni-RRM released weights as an external judge (2602.00846, deployment-legal at inference only — its training is gradient-based and out of contract); structural-check disagreement (see three_design_pieces). FENCED to offline one-time calibration on a self-carved split: the LABELLED headroom used to fit and validate the estimator (gold transcripts/answers), MMAR-Rubrics (offline-calibration-only, requires gold CoT + gold answer + paid Gemini-2.5-Pro), the k>=20 repeated-draw grading, and Weaver's ~1% gold class prior if the weak-supervision estimator is used. Machine enforcement mandatory: the 2607.13347 GT-access guard (hook file opening so any access to a GT directory fails immediately, violations logged; that paper reports 0/0). This is the sub-direction where my recorded information-boundary failure mode is most likely to recur, so the guard is a first-class artifact, not a note.
