- 度量与门：Binding: G1 (this sub-direction IS G1's instrument: headroom row + recovery fraction), G2 (direct-readout floor as a first-class policy row), G3 (three nulls: random-among-N, unweighted majority vote, within-instance score permutation), G7 (task x language x acoustic-stratum decomposition before aggregation), G9 (mechanical information-boundary isolation), G11 (headroom under TWO metric families on the SAME output). Estimator validation metrics: Spearman / AUROC of predicted vs realized headroom per stratum; a calibration reliability diagram in the MemRL 2601.03192 shape (predicted-bin vs realized outcome, their Pearson r=0.861 as the reference standard); pre-registered assumption gates that BLOCK magnitude reporting on failure (2607.03436); winner's-curse correction sqrt(ln K / 2k) applied to every max-over-noisy-estimates figure. Token metrics (WER, chrF) paired with semantic metrics (S2ER binary semantic equivalence; BERTScore) on the same output, per G11 and the 2606.18323 lesson that WER canonicalization noise ~0.14 fully masked a real number/date failure mode.
- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: S2ER (T3 B_evaluator_signal; 3-round bidirectional voting, majority-3 optimal, Pearson r 0.8914 / 0.8280 / 0.9031 vs experts with std <= 0.0291) defines the cluster space over which entropy and diversity are computed AND defines 'the pool contains a better candidate' for the oracle itself — therefore the oracle is an ESTIMATE with error, and Delta = m_or - max_pi m_pi is a difference of two noisy quantities (2606.13156's own transfer warning). The estimator's error rate on a held-out human-adjudicated subset is reported next to every headroom number and a threshold-sensitivity sweep is mandatory. Note the T3 caveat that S2ER numbers are judge-protocol-dependent and non-comparable across papers. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: structural checks in the CFR shape of 2606.18323 Eq.1-2 (token-count floor tau_tok=25, word floor tau_w=1, threshold theta=0.5) generalized to understanding outputs — parseability, commit-token presence, refusal/non-commit detection, dropout/collapse — give a label-free LOWER BOUND on headroom ('does any candidate satisfy structural invariants that all others violate') that is exactly computable without labels. Run the 2607.09438 diagnosis order first: parseability -> token truncation -> policy, so an extraction artifact is never attributed to the estimator. (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: headroom is expected to be condition-dependent while REALIZATION is more so — KIT's oracle is broadly comparable across languages (SQA +14.84 en / +15.76 de / +13.76 it / +13.32 zh) yet realization is not (consistent gains only en/zh). The estimator is therefore fit and reported per stratum (MS-SNSD 8 noise types x L2-Arctic 6 L1s x MCIF 4 languages), and cross-stratum zero-shot transfer is the generalization test (2604.18419's cross-dataset probe-transfer design).
- 成本与方差：Cost axis: k>=20 draws per item per stratum is the dominant cost and must be priced in AUDIO-SECONDS encoded, model calls, and dollars — token-only accounting (2607.06503's efficiency metric) does not price audio acquisition. Report the estimator's value as recovered-headroom-per-dollar against the do-nothing row, and report the k-sweep showing where the estimate settles rather than assuming the text-domain k~20-30. Controller overhead reported as a fraction of generation cost (Weaver 2506.18203 template: 35.35 vs 1.01 exaFLOPs per query, 0.57% overhead). Variance: >=5 seeds per cell (ATLAS 2606.01667 reports n=5, GPQA 85.86 +/- 0.55 pp); cluster-bootstrap 95% CIs clustered by utterance (2607.13347); Wilson 95% CIs on every claim-bearing proportion and rule-of-three 3/n upper bounds for zero cells (2606.18323); refuse to rank overlapping intervals. Variance-budget allocation follows 2607.17136: a CROSSED condition-draw x sampling-seed grid, and never estimate sigma by subsampling one draw (that halved it, 0.18 vs 0.36, and erased the catastrophic-failure mode entirely, 0/24 vs 3/10 runs below 0.10).
- 击杀条件：Kill if (i) the estimator's per-stratum correlation with realized headroom is indistinguishable from zero on a held-out stratum — this reproduces the 2606.13156 outcome (no observable recovers the oracle), a legitimate negative result but it kills the CONTROLLER use; (ii) G_noise dominates so that O^repro is within CI of direct readout on all speech carriers, i.e. there is nothing for any selector to recover and budget-allocate should replace select per 2607.03436 Thm 2; (iii) the answer-equivalence sensitivity sweep changes the headroom ORDERING across strata, meaning we are measuring the estimator rather than the pool; (iv) the pre-registered assumption gates fail on the speech carriers (draws not exchangeable, overdispersion), in which case magnitudes are not licensed to report at all.

**P 槽位**：P9 (label-free headroom estimation is the half of P9 named explicitly in the D5 boundary; it also supplies the headroom precondition consumed by D5.2's E1 gate)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】

## D5.2 奖励可用性门禁（E1 准入门：何时允许 reward 掌舵）

**定义**：A runtime, per-instance, acoustic-condition-keyed gate that decides whether an external reward signal is licensed to change the decision at all; default-deny, with the direct-readout incumbent returned when any precondition fails. This is C1's measurement contract promoted into a deployed licence.

**语音域状态**：PARTIAL — hand-set gates exist, calibrated/label-free gates are ABSENT (read set: D5 nine papers + d0 quintet + T1/T3 rows, cut 2026-07-26). Occupied at the HAND-SET level: TalTech 2603.17822's 4-tier reliability arbiter is the closest deployed instance (T3 B row: LALM agreement predicts accuracy 94.5% at n=128 / 83.2% at n=565 / 58.0% at n=307; confidence bins monotonic 91.1% at >=0.80 n=237, 74.4% at 0.60-0.79 n=722, 39.4% at 0.40-0.59 n=33) — deployment-legal, but hand-tuned during development and never held-out validated; VISA 2606.07264's 27-category static routing table is likewise fit heuristically on MMAR with no held-out split. ABSENT at the calibrated level, and the field-level demonstration of why it matters is 2606.09925: a BELOW-RANDOM critic (Qwen2-Audio-7B step Avg 17.7 vs random 23.4) still gains +6.9 chain-aggregation points over the random aggregator, and the 51.5-point step-verification spread compresses to a 9.5-point selection spread with rank inversions (Step-Audio-R1 verifies better at 62.6 but selects worse at 55.6 vs Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B 61.5 / 57.5) — so evaluator quality is a poor licence. KIT 2606.04730 implements a gate but at LANGUAGE level, post hoc, using gold. The text/formal donors inside D5 leave it explicitly open: JitRL 2601.18510's stated limitation #2 (the step-reward evaluator is ungated and 'may degrade policy quality') is never resolved; ETS 2601.21484 proves Thm 2's validity condition p > q_max and never runtime-checks it; ATLAS 2606.01667's stop bar is literally three prompt sentences (App. H.1).

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED for the calibrated variant, in the vision-GUI donor lane. 2604.09155 CORA gives a conformal risk-controlled execute/abstain boundary for mobile GUI automation. 2604.06240 (Universal Verifier + CUAVerifierBench) gives FPR-as-currency and human-agreement-band scoping. 2606.24515 gives a judge-as-noisy-channel formalism for computer-use agents. 2606.25760 ARGUS benchmarks UQ across VLMs and grounding datasets. 2604.17768 BIRCH exposes informativeness bias in VLM judges; 2604.25235 finds VLM judges can rank but cannot score (task-dependent uncertainty); 2604.18164 MM-JudgeBias supplies BD/BC bias certificates. 2606.13156 supplies the pre-registered label-free policy set that MUST include single-shot. 2604.18860 (TOCTOU/PUSV) contributes the orthogonal admissibility axis this read set otherwise lacks: a signal can be declared inadmissible on AGE alone, independent of content, with the observation-to-action gap measured first (mean 6.51 s, sd 3.59, range 3.18-13.23) and deterministic layered checks that cost <0.1 s versus ~5 s for an LLM re-check. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: CORA's conformal calibration needs labelled calibration episodes; 2606.13156's self-verification is P(yes) from logits (illegal under our contract) and its oracle is IoU; PUSV rests on a cheap, deterministic, RE-SAMPLABLE static state (benign SSIM 0.97-1.00) which speech does not have — a speech re-verify could only re-verify a DERIVED artifact (transcript, diarization, VAD segmentation), reintroducing the stochastic model whose non-determinism the defence was designed to avoid.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED and it is the richest instrument lane. 2506.18203 Weaver: label-free verifier-accuracy estimation for m=33 heterogeneous weak verifiers from pairwise agreement plus marginal consistency (Eq. 1-5), gold needed only for a scalar class prior on ~1% of items; weighted beats naive ensembling by up to 11.2 points and fitting a supervised ensemble on the same 0.01n labels drops accuracy 20.1%. 2512.02304: verifier gain = precision - solver accuracy as the go/no-go statistic, validated against actual rejection sampling (r=0.796 at 3 attempts, r=0.892 at 10), with verifier ACCURACY explicitly disqualified as a predictor, plus the self / intra-family / cross-family partition and the similarity-vs-FPR law (cross-family FPR r=0.613 slope 3.521; gain r=-0.257 slope -0.528). 2605.05873 CITE: anytime-valid Type-I control of false certification uniform over all data-driven stopping times, via a three-component eps/3 union bound (pairwise e-process vs the empirical runner-up, a time-uniform lower confidence bound on p_r, and an UNSEEN-CATEGORY upper bound U_t) — the only donor in the read set that budgets for hypotheses the sampler has not yet produced; expected stopping time is category-size-free with a matching minimax lower bound. 2606.01034 gives a finite-calibration regime map for judge panels; 2603.21172 shows entropy alone is insufficient for safe selective prediction (TCE, E-AURC); 2606.07834 documents cherry-pick override under mixed evidence. 2604.04648 contributes the pessimism/LCB construct r_LCB = r_hat - lambda*alpha as an admissibility discount. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: Weaver's latent Y is binary exact-match correctness, undefined for free-form speech; worse, its conditional-independence assumption collapses precisely for us because our plausible verifiers (the re-prompted core, a sibling omni model, a text judge over a transcription) fail TOGETHER on the same acoustic conditions, and text-embedding similarity does not measure that shared front-end failure at all; 2512.02304's central design rule ('pick a dissimilar verifier') is therefore hardest to satisfy exactly where it matters. CITE's counts are defined over CATEGORICAL answer identity and its unseen-category term is the component most sensitive to clustering error (over-merging hides competitors; under-merging blocks certification forever), and its target must be fixed a priori — certifying the current empirical leader is its named open problem. 2604.04648's uncertainty is read from the reward model's layer-L hidden states, so it is only available if we own an open-weight judge.

**开放增量**：Construct the gate as a runtime, per-acoustic-stratum admissibility certificate over FREE-FORM speech outputs whose inputs are all deployment-legal, and measure whether gating (vs always-steer, vs never-steer) changes realized recovery and harm — reporting the gate as the deciding policy row rather than a footnote. Three scoped moves: (i) legalize Weaver's label-free verifier-accuracy estimator with an answer-equivalence estimator standing in for latent Y, and EXPLICITLY model the correlated-failure violation that speech verifiers sharing the audio front-end induce (measure verifier error correlation per acoustic stratum before assuming independence); (ii) instantiate CITE's unseen-mass term over a semantic-cluster space so the gate budgets for candidates not yet drawn; (iii) add the age/staleness admissibility axis from 2604.18860 for streaming or multi-turn speech, where the evidence a decision rests on has a measurable age.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：MMAU Test-mini (T2, metadata_only) as the closed-answer arm where the licence question is sharpest (a below-random critic still buys +6.9 there). MCIF test set (T2, metadata_only; augmented corpus released on HF) as the open-output arm — this is where the licence must survive without answer identity. LibriSpeech test-other x MS-SNSD (8 noise types @10dB) + L2-Arctic (6 L1s) (T2, metadata_only) as the stratification axis and as the regression cases ASR-TRA 2603.05231 exposed (Korean 13.42 -> 13.76 worse than no adaptation; SUTA beats it on NB 33.75 vs 35.81; SGEM on SD 19.50 vs 22.34). AQUA-Bench + Audio-Hallucination (T2, metadata_only) as the deliberately un-licensable probe (2604.25591 reports P(True) 0.52 and 0.44 there). ACQUISITION: all metadata_only — same bulk-download item as D5.1【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】. AudioProcessBench (T2, metadata_only, release promised, URL unconfirmed, CC BY-NC 4.0) flagged for the next-step proposal; its prompts are fully printed so the protocol is reproducible without the release.
- 冻结核：Same lane as D5.1【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】: Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (open weight) as the weak core; Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B via local llama.cpp GGUF (-ngl 28, input_audio) as CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY with the weak-core caveat and the ATLAS direction-instability statement attached to every number; Gemini-3-Flash (proprietary API, no logprobs) as the cross-family judge. Critical composition constraint from 2606.09925 G10: SelfGap is only defined for critics that are also generators, and among those the two STRONGEST show the largest self-blindness (Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B -24.6, Step-Audio-R1 -25.2 PRMScore points on their own traces; Qwen2.5-Omni-7B -17.8, Gemma-4-E4B -22.0), while Gemini-3-Flash (the strongest critic overall at 67.9) is not a generator in that pool so its SelfGap is undefined. Any gate whose inputs come from the same core that generated the candidates must therefore report SelfGap for that pair or be disallowed.
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows (direct readout / random-among-N / consensus-only / oracle) with the D5.1 anchors【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】, PLUS the three gate-specific policy rows that make the licence falsifiable: always-steer (gate removed, signal always allowed), never-steer (gate always denies = direct readout), and the tie-block permutation null of 2607.13347 App. I.3 in which the signal carries no information (that null caught an apparent win that came entirely from an earliest-tie rule exploiting a monotone quality decline). Published anchors: 2606.09925 best critic chain-Avg 60.5 (Gemini-3-Flash) / 57.5 (Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B) against the random-aggregator floor 44.1 and a below-random critic still at 51.0; 2602.00846 AVQA 77.7 -> 80.3 at N=10 over Self-consistency@N; 2607.13347 (document/vision) FinTabNet always-iter0 0.0000 recovery BEATING every judge policy (best executable -0.0030 = -16.2%, worst -0.0200 = -107.9%) and OmniDocBench best 27.7%.
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL gate inputs: MBR/chrF consensus and its dispersion (0 model calls, 2606.04730); discrete semantic entropy and vote-rate P(True), counts-only (2604.25591); AudioProcessBench reference-free per-step critic in [-1,+1] with its propagation-scoring rule (2606.09925 Fig. 10 — printed verbatim, usable unmodified); Omni-RRM released weights as an external audio-capable judge with known calibration (audio preference accuracy 66.8 / 65.0, ~17 pp above chance, 2602.00846); CLAP audio-text similarity (2603.05231; fidelity Spearman rho = -0.431 vs true WER — the authors present this as reliability evidence, our 'weak for a controller' reading is ours); TRACE textual-blueprint judge (T3 B, training-free and reference-free at inference). ILLEGAL under contract and excluded: predictive / length-normalized / probability-mass semantic entropy (need guaranteed logprobs, 2604.25591); ETS-IS dual-model shared-tokenizer logprobs (2601.21484); JitRL's token-level index-logit path — only its verbalized-confidence variant is admissible and it carries ZERO published numbers, so it must be MEASURED before adoption (2601.18510). FENCED to offline one-time calibration on a self-carved split: MMAR-Rubrics (offline-calibration-only, gold CoT + gold answer, paid Gemini-2.5-Pro); ASR round-trip WER-vs-reference (EVAL-ONLY-GOLD; 2606.18323's selector and scorer are the same function, which its own authors demote to an upper bound); Weaver's ~1% gold class prior; every per-stratum threshold fit; the verifier-gain computation of 2512.02304. Machine enforcement: 2607.13347's GT-access file-open hook with violation counts logged.
- 度量与门：Binding: G1, G2, G3, G4, G5, G9, G10, G11. G4 supplies the fidelity floor the gate consumes, with reference failure values to beat quoted from 2607.13347 and 2606.04730: tie rate on distinct-output pairs 49.1% / 37.5%, ties across gaps >= 0.05 on the target metric 42.2% / 23.6%, rank reproducibility Kendall's W = 0.36, 3-run exact agreement 68.6% at temperature 0, sequential-pairwise positional bias +35.3 pp A-bias (up to +56.8 pp for German) versus round-robin's +3.2 pp. G5 is the anti-no-op gate: 2607.13347's margins that avoided degradation accepted ZERO revisions (delta >= 60), so the accept count and the accepted-set composition are mandatory reporting. G8 'robustly beats' adopted verbatim: cluster-bootstrap 95% CI advantage over random on ALL carriers AND under ALL tie-breaking rules. Token metrics (WER, chrF) paired with semantic metrics (S2ER, BERTScore) on the same output. Per-bucket stratified deltas: acoustic stratum x task x language, never an aggregate mean (KIT's +3.71 portfolio mean hides -11.06 on SQA and -8.60 on SSUM).
- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: S2ER 3-round bidirectional voting, majority-3 (r 0.8914 / 0.8280 / 0.9031, std <= 0.0291) supplies the equality relation for every count the gate consumes (vote margin, semantic entropy, Weaver-style agreement, CITE-style category counts); its own error rate is measured on a held-out human-adjudicated subset and PROPAGATED into every count, because CITE's unseen-category term is the component most sensitive to clustering error. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: a structural detector in the 2606.18323 CFR shape (tau_tok = 25, tau_w = 1, theta = 0.5) generalized to understanding outputs (parseability, commit-token presence, refusal detection, dropout/collapse) plus the PUSV-style age check; the gate may DENY on structural or staleness grounds alone, independent of the soft signal — this is the hybrid deterministic-guard + soft-judge controller 2607.13347 explicitly asks for ('a verification signal that deterministically detects structural change, rather than judge scores alone'). (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: all thresholds (fidelity floor, margin bar, headroom h*) are fit PER STRATUM over MS-SNSD 8 noise types x L2-Arctic 6 L1s x MCIF 4 languages, and cross-stratum zero-shot transfer is a first-class generalization test (2604.18419 §6.3 design); a single global threshold is the named weakness of every donor here (2506.19248's single theta-dagger per dataset/RM pair; 2604.25591's tau=0.25 justified only by 'preliminary experiments').
- 成本与方差：Cost axis: model calls + audio-seconds encoded + wall-clock per decision, with the gate's own overhead reported as a fraction of generation cost (Weaver template, 0.57% for the distilled variant). Evaluate every signal on a COST-NORMALIZED axis using the KIT call budgets that KIT itself never normalizes: MBR 0 calls, Comparison 1, Likelihood N, Pairwise N-1, Round-Robin ~N(N-1)/2. Reference cost warnings: ASR-TRA's LLM-reward rows cost 7-9x latency (0.489 s -> 4.365 s per utterance) for ~1 WER point; Omni-RRM's BoN@10 is 10 generations + 9 rationale-generating RM passes with latency deferred to an unavailable supplement. Variance: >=5 seeds; cluster-bootstrap CIs clustered by utterance; Wilson CIs and rule-of-three for zero cells; paired Wilcoxon / exact McNemar for policy contrasts; crossed condition-draw x sampling-seed grid per 2607.17136 with the explicit warning that subsampling one draw halves sigma (0.18 vs 0.36) and hides the catastrophic-failure mode; report the wrong-sign stress test (at that variance regime a published-size single-run improvement has the wrong sign 33-44% of the time).
- 击杀条件：Kill if (i) on every acoustic stratum the gate's accept set is empty or its accept set gives no recovery improvement over always-steer — the 2607.13347 disguised-no-op failure; (ii) the answer-equivalence estimator's own error exceeds the effect size being gated on that stratum (then the gate is certifying the wrong quantity, the CITE/MARS hazard); (iii) per-stratum thresholds do not transfer zero-shot to a held-out stratum, meaning the gate fits noise; (iv) measured verifier error correlation across our candidate signals is so high that the label-free accuracy estimator is unidentifiable — the speech-specific violation of Weaver's conditional independence, which must be measured rather than assumed; (v) SelfGap for the deployed critic-generator pair exceeds the gate's own decision margin and no cross-family critic is affordable.

**P 槽位**：P9 (E1 decision-calibration / admissibility gate; this sub-direction is the concrete instantiation of C1 -> E1)

## D5.3 决策权扩展：reward 驱动的 repair 与 no-regression 弃权门

**定义**：Extend the exercised decision rights beyond select to REPAIR driven by the reward's DIAGNOSTIC content on an existing candidate. SPLIT WITH D4.3 (A1, binding): D5.3 owns the rule that turns a diagnosis into a repair ACTION; the abstain / no-regression GATE itself — the explicit selection right over {direct readout, post-evidence answer} as a system property, with per-bucket harm-reporting — is D4.3's architecture seat, and D5.3 explicitly DEFERS that gate's margin calibration to P9. D5.3 supplies the reward-diagnostic content that a repair decision consumes; it does not itself hold the abstain gate.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

**语音域状态**：Repair: PARTIAL (occupied but uncalibrated). Within the D5 nine-paper read set repair is ABSENT — every decision right is discard-and-redraw or one gradient step: ASR-TRA 2603.05231 consumes its n=4 candidates only as gradient samples and re-decodes; Omni-RRM 2602.00846 is SELECT ONLY and collapses five criterion-level judgments into one verdict; TTS 2606.18323 is select + dropout filter, and its own number/date diagnosis is 'a failure at tokenization rather than acoustics', i.e. text-normalization-repairable, yet nothing rewrites the input. The d0 quintet fills part of the surface: EChO-Agent 2606.15141 DOES repair (diagnostic feedback re-invokes the reasoner) and select (dual-pass arbitration), but verification gates only the final output while tool dispatch is deliberately static, and its ablation is confounded (full 71.0 vs w/o Verification 69.1 vs w/o Observation 69.2 vs baseline 68.7, single run, no seeds/CIs, and the authors themselves attribute part of it to best-of-2 sampling not judging); its verifier is DeepSeek-V3, a separate text LLM, so it is evidence about a HETEROGENEOUS external-judge loop over a frozen omni reasoner. Omni-Decision 2607.11433 exposes revise_answer / reground / stop_insufficient. Abstain as a NO-REGRESSION GATE: ABSENT, and actively contradicted — Omni-Decision's Answer Critic prompt BLOCKS abstention ('BLOCK when: the answer is a refusal or abstention ... Commit now'), and the same system loses to direct readout on OmniGAIA (45.56 vs Gemini-3-Pro 62.50, source 2607.11433 Table 1). The motivating regression evidence is field-wide: ASR-TRA Korean 13.42 -> 13.76; TTS distillation Orpheus 0.008 -> 0.033 and Llasa-3B 0.108 -> 0.142; KIT 8 of 10 reranking configurations net worse than greedy.

**视觉域对照**：OCCUPIED and largely NEGATIVE. 2606.13156 (VLM grounding) is the sharpest: the widely quoted 48.7% / -31pp collapse for naive iteration is itself a best-step/last-valid figure, while the DEPLOYABLE last-step number is 17.0%, i.e. 79.6 -> 17.0 = -63pp; GRPO training lifted honest gain g from -11.1pp to -3.8pp and halved per-step degradation WITHOUT ever beating single-shot ('defensive, not additive'); the counterfactual overlay shows the loop reacts to the PRESENCE of a rendered box, not its content. 2607.17136 (computer-use) decomposes ONE verifier into four roles (hint / teacher / reward / gate) of which verifier-as-hint and verifier-as-gate are pure 'renting' channels that need no weights and are contract-compatible; it supplies a two-tier repairability gradient (fixed-token done-detection installs at 0.97 +/- 0.06; field-targeting 0.71 +/- 0.26; grounding 0.53 +/- 0.35; generative field-fill 0.14 +/- 0.04) and the end-to-end finding that repair reaches task success exactly when the corrective action is the SOLE remaining blocker (LinkedIn 8/20 vs base 0/15, Fisher exact p=0.006). 2603.16664 Kestrel gives evidence-gated self-refinement for LVLM hallucination; 2606.24525 VisCritic turns a per-step scalar into accept/retry/backtrack; 2606.11078 HiViG gates each step with a history-aware visually grounded critic; 2607.09438 shows a PRM selector REGRESSING -2.16pp on the high-confidence tier (48 corrections vs 119 regressions). TRANSFER BOUNDARY: mutation-log / IoU oracles have no speech analogue and 2607.17136 explicitly reports that early pilots scoring success from screenshots were 'too ambiguous to use' — the weaker proxy was unusable, not merely noisier; the verifier judges 'from the same observation the policy sees', so a speech verifier must consume the waveform, and their capability gradient warns small open verifiers fail exactly the decisive frames (Qwen2.5-VL-7B 0/2 done-detection vs 1/2 at 72B); 'decision frames' presume a step boundary with a well-defined corrective next action, which a speech turn lacks.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED, with the fullest instrumentation in the document/text donor lane. 2607.13347 supplies the causal decomposition that must be inherited: degradation is NOT caused by wrong feedback content (paired feedback-content contribution +0.0001, n.s. on 99 shared tables) but by TARGET-PRESERVATION FAILURE under an unconstrained 'improve this'; a copy-preserving instruction cut the severe-loss rate from 17/476 = 3.6% to 4/476 = 0.8% (exact McNemar p=0.0023) and from 6/272 to 2/272 on OmniDocBench, WITHOUT producing any mean improvement; per-table harm was 41.6% worsen / 23.9% improve on FinTabNet with severe breakage (delta TEDS < -0.10) in 11.8% / 4.4% of tables 'and the judge fails to detect it'; 85.2% of severe declines involved structural changes; feedback raised churn (stagnant transitions 84% -> 54%). 2506.11930 Feedback Friction supplies the ceiling from the other end with a privileged-information feedback generator: solvers plateau below target and 62.8-100% of residual errors classify as feedback RESISTANCE rather than feedback quality; semantic entropy correlates with actionability (improvement rate rises from near-zero at low entropy to 0.4-0.8 at high entropy); and self-reported compliance is disqualified (>95% say they understand, >96% say they will update, then do not). 2604.22273 frames self-correction as feedback control with an explicit stability threshold above which repair is admissible. 2606.21724 DISC gives structured verification loops. 2303.11366 Reflexion supplies the FP-is-terminal / FN-is-recoverable asymmetry and the three-arm factorial (HumanEval-Rust: base 0.60, gate-only [self-reflection omitted] 0.60, verbal-only [test-generation omitted] 0.52 — WORSE than doing nothing — full 0.68)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】, i.e. the verbal channel is inert without a grounded gate and the gate alone is inert without the verbal channel. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: all anchor on deterministic oracles; 'preserve the target' is well-defined for structured HTML but 2607.13347 itself states speech-style outputs have no comparable structural invariant; Reflexion's three evaluator instantiations each need exact-match, executable unit tests, or symbolic repeat detection, none of which exists for a spoken response; 2506.11930's monotone-by-construction guarantee breaks under a proxy judge, which can flip an item from right to wrong unnoticed.

**开放增量**：Make repair REWARD-DECIDED rather than schedule-decided, on free-form speech outputs, with the copy-preserving contrast and severe-loss tail accounting inherited from 2607.13347 rather than re-derived. The scoping decision that carries the sub-direction: the repair ACTION SET must be derived from the reward's diagnostic content — Omni-RRM's five criterion-level judgments and AudioProcessBench's six-type audio error taxonomy (existence / semantic / temporal / acoustic-attribute / cross-modal binding / reasoning) route to targeted actions (re-segment, re-normalize text, re-ask with the failed criterion named, escalate to a tool), never a generic 'improve this'. The abstain/no-regression GATE that decides whether to keep the direct readout is D4.3's architecture seat (A1 split, 定义 above); D5.3's role there is limited to supplying the reward-diagnostic MARGIN signal that P9 calibrates into D4.3's admissibility band. The corrected field fact motivating why the gate must expose rather than presume a sign: wrapper-vs-direct is task-conditioned (OmniGAIA 45.56 vs 62.50 losing; MMAR agent track winning by +11.07 Rubrics in the two-papers-one-leaderboard framing — TalTech 69.83 vs VISA-reported best direct 58.76, the same caveat D4 carries, not the earlier ~+11.4 figure).【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：MCIF test set (T2, metadata_only) as the primary open-output repair carrier — its greedy anchors and 17-candidate oracle map are published and the no-task-identity constraint is adopted as a standing rule. MMAU Test-mini + MMSU (T2, metadata_only) as the closed-answer control where majority voting is mechanically strong, so repair must beat consensus rather than beat greedy. AQUA-Bench + Audio-Hallucination (T2, metadata_only) as abstain-warranted probes. LibriSpeech test-other x MS-SNSD + L2-Arctic (T2, metadata_only) as the per-condition regression cases ASR-TRA exposed. ACQUISITION: all metadata_only, same bulk item. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION and flagged for the proposal: AudioProcessBench (release promised, URL unconfirmed) for the six-type taxonomy, the step-segmentation prompt and the reference-free critic prompt; MELD / MELD-Hard1k (T2: MELD needs_download, Hard1k must be RECONSTRUCTED, not byte-identical) for paralinguistic repair; the 26-prompt TTS hard set (T2: not_released, must be authored by us) if the generation-side repair arm is run.
- 冻结核：Repair needs only re-prompting, so the local llama.cpp Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B lane (-ngl 28, input_audio) IS usable for mechanism smoke — but only as cheap verification, never load-bearing, and every rate measured there must carry the weak-core caveat plus the ATLAS 2606.01667 direction instability (LCB +12.0 weak vs +1.15 strong; BabyVision +6.2 vs +2.32; HLE-Verified reverses, strong +8.00 vs weak +5.0). Qwen2.5-Omni-7B as the weak-core floor; Gemini-3-Flash as the cross-family critic so the repair diagnosis does not come from the generator itself (G10). 2607.17136's two-tier repairability gradient predicts installability depends on core capability, so a local-lane repair rate is not a prediction about a strong core.
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows (direct readout / random-among-N / consensus-only / oracle) with the D5.1 anchors【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】, PLUS three repair-specific controls that are not optional. (B) no-feedback UNCONSTRAINED regeneration and (C) no-feedback COPY-PRESERVING regeneration, both re-forked from the SAME stored first output so the contrast is paired (2607.13347's frozen-baseline B/C design). (D) the Reflexion 2303.11366 three-arm factorial: grounded gate only / verbal critique only / both, which separates 'the diagnosis was useful' from 'having a pass-fail gate was useful'. Published anchors carried with domain labels: 2607.13347 FinTabNet always-iter0 0.0000 beating every judge policy and OmniDocBench best 27.7% recovery (document/vision); EChO-Agent 68.7 -> 71.0 accuracy / 58.7 -> 63.0 rubric with w/o-Verification at 69.1, confounded with best-of-2 (audio); 2607.17136 LinkedIn 8/20 vs 0/15 (vision-GUI).
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL diagnostic sources: Omni-RRM's five criterion-level judgments as released weights (2602.00846); AudioProcessBench's reference-free per-step critic with the six-type taxonomy and the propagation-scoring rule (2606.09925); consensus disagreement / MBR dispersion (0 calls, 2606.04730); CLAP-vs-LM committee disagreement (2603.05231, with the rho=-0.431 fidelity caveat); TRACE textual-blueprint judge (T3 B). FENCED to offline one-time calibration on a self-carved split: the NEAR-ORACLE FEEDBACK CEILING arm, which follows 2506.11930's design by giving the feedback generator the gold answer — this is explicitly a diagnostic that separates 'our signal is bad' from 'the core cannot act on any signal', never a deployable number and never reported as a gain; MMAR-Rubrics; the stability-threshold fit of 2604.22273; the per-stratum abstain threshold. GT-access hook enforced with violations logged (2607.13347).
- 度量与门：Binding: G2 (the direct readout as a first-class policy row IS the abstain gate's purpose), G5 (margin honesty — report how many repairs the gate actually accepted; a rule accepting 12 of 476 is a disguised no-op), G6 (harm: severe-loss threshold, worsen/improve share, up-mass vs down-mass, paired exact McNemar / Wilcoxon — a statistically neutral mean concealed 25.7% worsened instances in the document study), G7 (task x language x acoustic-stratum decomposition), G1, G3, G11. Report a CORRECTIONS-VS-REGRESSIONS LEDGER instead of a net delta (2607.09438: 233 vs 250, 51 vs 55, 37 vs 59; and its scorer-discrimination test showing the top-vs-runner-up score gap is 0.049 on corrections and 0.048 on degradations, i.e. no discriminative signal). Report the stagnation statistic (unchanged-transition share) as a churn measure. Token and semantic metrics MANDATORY on the same output — WER canonicalization noise ~0.14 (raw 0.155 vs normalized 0.143) fully masked a real number/date failure mode in 2606.18323, so token metrics distort in both directions.
- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR: S2ER decides both 'did the repair change the answer at all' (feeding the stagnation statistic) and 'did it change it for the better' (feeding the corrections/regressions ledger); its error is reported and swept. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: define a speech TARGET-PRESERVATION invariant as the set of deterministically checkable properties of the incumbent that a repair must not silently drop — speaker-attribution set, named-entity set, timestamp set, numeric/date set, plus the CFR-shaped dropout/collapse thresholds (2606.18323 tau_tok=25, tau_w=1, theta=0.5). This is the direct speech analogue of the copy-preserving instruction, and it is motivated by the measured fact that 85.2% of severe declines involved structural changes and large declines were ~3x more frequent in structural than content-only transitions (26.9% vs 8.2%). (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: repair admissibility and the abstain margin are fit PER STRATUM, because ASR-TRA's regressions are condition-specific (Korean; loses to SUTA on NB, to SGEM on SD and Hindi) and 2604.22273's stability threshold is a per-regime quantity; cross-stratum transfer is reported.
- 成本与方差：Cost: extra generations + judge passes per repair round, reported as recovery-per-call and as a REPAIR-ROUND SWEEP curve (1..R), never a point — 2502.00271 shows guided selection wins at n=1 (+2.5 to +23.0) and loses at n=32 (-3.1 to -43.8, OOD -25.8 to -46.1), so any repair claim must be a curve over budget on a true-quality axis. Audio-seconds and wall-clock alongside tokens. Variance: >=5 seeds; crossed condition-draw x sampling-seed grid (2607.17136), with sigma never estimated by subsampling one draw; Hartigan dip test plus two-component-mixture BIC when the outcome distribution looks bimodal, and IQM with a stratified bootstrap as the summary when it is; Fisher exact for small-n end-to-end cells; the wrong-sign stress test reported as a referee-facing statistic; explicit split between EXISTENCE claims (k<=5 seeds) and SEPARATION claims (the 2607.17136 power curve puts k~46 for Delta=0.18 at sigma=0.3).
- 击杀条件：Kill repair if (i) the copy-preserving control (C) matches or beats reward-driven repair on realized recovery while having a strictly smaller severe-loss tail — then our gain is target preservation, not signal content, i.e. we reproduced 2607.13347 rather than beating it; (ii) the corrections-vs-regressions ledger is negative on every stratum at every budget; (iii) the near-oracle feedback-ceiling arm shows the speech core is feedback-resistant (the analogue of 62.8-100% resistance), making repair core-bounded rather than signal-bounded. Kill the abstain gate if it is a disguised no-op (accept/deny counts degenerate) OR if its recall of the known regression cases (Korean, NB, SD, de/it reranking) falls below a pre-registered floor — a gate that never fires where the wrapper regresses has no licence value.

**P 槽位**：P8 (reward-as-controller: repair is the least-occupied decision right in the read set). Split with D4.3/P9 (A1, binding, reciprocal to D4.3's P-slot line): the abstain/no-regression GATE itself is D4.3's architecture seat, not D5.3's; D5.3 supplies the reward-diagnostic margin signal, and that margin is thresholded by P9/D5.2's E1 gate.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，含 A7 重编号】

## D5.4 预算/停止/分配作为被校准且随部署演化的决策权

**定义**：Turn the allocation statistics the field already computes and discards into an EXERCISED decision right whose thresholds are calibrated with a stated validity target and then RE-calibrated as deployment statistics accumulate. This is the 'evolving statistics' half of the dimension: not a fixed N, and not a fixed threshold either.

**语音域状态**：PARTIAL — the statistics are OCCUPIED as computation and ABSENT as calibrated policy (D5 nine-paper read set + T1 rows, cut 2026-07-26). Three speech papers each compute an allocation statistic and throw it away: ASR-TRA 2603.05231 computes adv_i = r_i - r_bar (Eq. 7) and spends it as a gradient coefficient; Omni-RRM 2602.00846 defines hard pairs by margin Delta = |sA - sB| < 2 for TRAINING and never uses the margin at inference; TTS 2606.18323 names saturation-N a per-input difficulty signal and still runs a uniform sweep, with its own curves quantifying the waste (73.1% of hard-set and 94.2% of LibriSpeech prompts already pass at N=1, so uniform N=4 pays 4x on 73-94% of the population). Deployed stop exists in audio-omni but is never calibrated (per the campaign field-fact: AOP-Agent's reflector; hand thresholds elsewhere), and budget can actively hurt: Omni-DeepSearch 2605.08762 budget (15,5) DEGRADES versus (10,3) on VIDEO/AMBIENT via over-search drift. 2604.25591 routes on ONE global tau=0.25 justified only by unspecified 'preliminary experiments', prices adaptive inference at 24-64% of full-reasoning token cost, and NEVER prices the K=10 sampling that enables the routing.

**视觉域对照**：PARTIAL. 2607.17136 deploys a verifier-as-gate with NO calibrated threshold — no recall or precision target — and the dossier flags this in sharp contrast to text-domain Clopper-Pearson machinery, so the calibrated version of exactly that gate is an open slot. 2604.09155 CORA supplies a conformal risk-controlled execute/abstain boundary for mobile GUI agents. 2606.28864 (LVLM test-time scaling) shows the regime split that motivates per-instance budget routing — extra compute HELPS reasoning benchmarks and HURTS perception-only ones, demonstrated by a 300-vs-1024 token-cap ablation with extraction preserved so truncation does not confound — and explicitly notes no controller exploits it. 2607.09438 supplies the SKIP RULE as a budget primitive (>=5/8 agreement passes through unrescored; only the <=4/8 tier (23.4% of questions, 41.7% accuracy — the source paper calls this tier "below-chance", which does not hold for EXAMS-V's 5-option format where chance is 20%; the transferable fact is that accuracy there is FLAT IN N, not that it is below chance【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】), plus the all-unparseable tier are rescored) and the budget-axis dominance result (token budget 1k->2k = +3.7pp / 171 questions versus chain count 8->16 = +0.15pp / 7 questions). TRANSFER BOUNDARY: the token-budget knob assumes a controllable max_new_tokens; guided_choice constrained decoding is unavailable black-box; agreement tiers need answer identity.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED and the richest donor lane in the whole read set. 2606.12935 MARS: stop when the margin exceeds worst-case adversarial vote erosion for EVERY challenger including a SYNTHETIC UNSEEN CHALLENGER with zero current votes, Hoeffding plus union bound giving P(leader changes) <= delta, gamma calibrated per question from 16 warmup traces, 25-47% self-consistency token savings and 14-29% on top of DeepConf across 18 settings with accuracy preserved within 0.6pp; crucially the guarantee is relative to the FULL-BUDGET VOTE, not to ground truth, and the certified variant (gamma=1, true q) is separated from the deployed calibrated variant with no theorem. 2607.06503: per-round Clopper-Pearson-calibrated abort gates on a user-specified recall budget, the NON-COMPOSITION argument (two gates each passing 98% give 98% if their false-abort sets coincide but only 96% if disjoint), joint budget-vector search over 46,656 candidates under a GLOBAL episode-level recall constraint with margin delta=0.02 sized from the binomial standard error, freeze-then-certify with an independent post-selection bound, abstain-on-infeasible as the conservative default, and the sample-complexity curve (~114 successful certification episodes support ~0.974; 0.98 needs ~149; 0.99 needs ~299, INDEPENDENT of scorer quality). 2604.18419: abstain iff V < r_bot, with J = alpha*r_bot + (1-alpha)*S, alpha-quantile threshold calibration achieving target abstention within 1.2 pp MAE, and the monotone-invariance result licensing RANKING-ONLY (uncalibrated) signals whenever the controller targets an abstention RATE. 2606.28733: the (w_i, tau_i) timeliness formalism with premature abstention counted as FAILURE, AbsRec@K as a curve, SPL as a delay-penalized score, and over-abstention on a paired solvable-contrast set as the mandatory second axis. 2607.03436 Theorem 2: best-of-K on the committed model provably dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle — i.e. the budget-allocate right DOMINATES the select right wherever single-draw noise is the binding term. Also 2605.26849 (UAB), 2604.14853 (the dual variable IS the price), 2602.16699, 2605.05701. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: MARS needs mid-generation probing of partial traces (a black-box API returning only completed responses has no checkpoint surface), exact answer identity for votes, and confidence/logprob features; 2607.06503's load-bearing signal is residual-stream activations, flatly excluded, and its own comparison says the behavior-only substitute is near chance at round 1 and only informative by rounds 3-4 by which time much recoverable compute is gone; it also needs labelled binary outcomes and EXCHANGEABILITY, which is doubtful across acoustic strata — so recall control must be per-stratum, multiplying the ~114-299 certification successes PER STRATUM; 2604.18419's value estimator is a gradient-trained MLP on final-layer hidden states.

**开放增量**：A calibrated, per-acoustic-stratum stop/allocate rule whose inputs are exclusively deployment-legal observables (semantic-cluster vote counts, MBR dispersion, judge margin, saturation-N observed so far), whose VALIDITY TARGET is stated honestly as the full-budget decision rather than truth (MARS's framing), and whose thresholds are REFIT as deployment statistics accumulate — the evolving-statistics element. Two disciplines adopted verbatim rather than reinvented: the certified-versus-calibrated variant separation (theorem scoped only to the un-relaxed case), and the non-composition check (report the GLOBAL rate, never the product of per-gate rates). The single cheapest concrete step visible across three speech papers is that all three allocation statistics already exist and no allocator consumes them.

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：MCIF (T2, metadata_only) for per-segment allocation — KIT tabulates per-strategy call budgets and never normalizes by them, so the cost-normalized axis is directly available. LibriSpeech test-clean 120x3 (T2, metadata_only) plus the 26-prompt hard set (T2, NOT RELEASED — must be authored by us) for the within-corpus per-prompt saturation-N dose-response that 2606.18323's own authors name as their most important next step. MMAU Test-mini + MMSU (T2, metadata_only) for closed-answer stopping where vote counts are exact. MS-SNSD 8 noise types x L2-Arctic 6 L1s (T2, metadata_only) as the calibration strata. AQUA-Bench + Audio-Hallucination (T2, metadata_only) as the abstain-warranted contrast set. ACQUISITION: all metadata_only (bulk item); the hard set is an authoring item, not a download.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B via the local llama.cpp lane (cheap verification only; weak-core caveat and ATLAS direction instability restated on every number). NOTE a hard constraint on this sub-direction specifically: the llama.cpp lane's tool-call capability is recorded as UNVERIFIED in T3, and MARS-style mid-generation probing is not available through a request-level interface at all — so the stop rule must be defined at UTTERANCE / TURN / CANDIDATE boundaries, which is the checkpoint redefinition ETS 2601.21484's own theory permits but its paper does not perform.
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows, INSTANTIATED explicitly at the level of a stop/allocate rule rather than left as a bare count (A6)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】: (1) DIRECT READOUT — no allocation policy, a single fixed-budget pass per item at the incumbent's own default N; (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N — at matched EXPECTED budget, draw a random stop point/budget level per item instead of a calibrated rule, the honest null separating 'the right stop' from 'having any stop at all'; (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY — run to a FIXED uniform budget cap (the fixed-N sweep, below) and aggregate by unweighted majority/MBR with no adaptive stopping and no calibration; (4) ORACLE — per-instance minimum-sufficient N / best stop point computed OFFLINE from gold (the saturation-N ceiling, e.g. 2606.18323's CFR=0 point), gold-consuming, ceiling only, with recovery = realized/oracle reported (feeds G1). PLUS the two structural stop baselines from 2607.06503 (single-gate with the entire budget at one searched checkpoint, and uniform) and the fixed-N sweep as the incumbent. ATLAS 2606.01667 supplies the control that isolates the stopping decision from aggregation ability: LLM-Selection at a FIXED N=8 (cost $/q 3.71 / 1.51 / 0.56 / 0.97 vs ATLAS 1.59 / 0.53 / 0.27 / 0.35), plus the stop-time-gap histogram against a uniform-stop null with the exact-stop enrichment statistic (88.9% exact stop, 7.1x enrichment) and the evidence-quality stratification Reliable / Majority / Minority / None with the minority-extraction rate (36.7% recovery when the correct answer is NOT the majority — the part of the gain a majority-vote metric cannot see). Speech anchors: 2606.18323 hard-set CFR 0.269 (N=1) -> 0.154 (N=2) -> 0.038 (N=3) -> 0.000 (N>=4) with pass rates 73.1% / 94.2% at N=1; 2606.04730 per-strategy call budgets (MBR 0, Comparison 1, Likelihood N, Pairwise N-1, RR ~N(N-1)/2).
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL: MBR dispersion (0 calls), discrete semantic entropy and vote-rate P(True) counts-only (2604.25591), vote margin over the semantic-cluster space, external-judge margin (Omni-RRM / AudioProcessBench critic), observed saturation-N. Contract note carried from 2606.04730: Likelihood needs log p(candidate|audio) from an EXTERNAL scoring model, which is contract-legal because it is not the frozen core being wrapped — but the llama.cpp lane's logprob stability must be verified before any reliance. ILLEGAL: MARS's probe-confidence and confidence-trend features; 2607.06503's hidden-state probes; 2604.18419's final-layer MLP probe. FENCED to offline one-time calibration on a self-carved split: the per-stratum Clopper-Pearson calibration set (needs labelled outcomes), the alpha-quantile threshold fit, the gamma contraction fit, and the oracle-q diagnostic ablation (2606.12935's retrospective indicator upper-bounding what better switch estimation could buy).
- 度量与门：Binding: G1, G5 (report what the rule actually stopped and accepted), G6, G7, G2, G3. Plus four donor-specific requirements. (1) Report savings AND accuracy at matched settings with the baseline's WHOLE tradeoff curve (MARS Table 2 discipline: Parallel-Probe at u=3 saves 20-51% but loses 6-45pp; at u=14 preserves accuracy but saves <=4%). (2) AbsRec@K as a curve with premature stop counted as failure, plus over-abstention on a PAIRED solvable-contrast set (2606.28733). (3) The NON-COMPOSITION check as an explicit correctness test: report the global episode-level rate, never the product of per-gate rates. (4) Achieved-vs-target rate MAE as a first-class number (2604.18419 reports 1.2 pp). Token and semantic metrics paired; per-stratum stratified deltas mandatory since exchangeability across acoustic conditions is exactly what is in doubt.
- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR IS the vote-counting relation here, which makes it maximally load-bearing: its error enters the margin M_k(t) completely unmodelled, and a Hoeffding-style bound would then certify the wrong quantity — so the estimator's error is measured per stratum, propagated into the bound, and swept; MARS's synthetic UNSEEN CHALLENGER is adopted precisely because the candidate set is not closed for free-form speech. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: structural CFR-shaped checks (dropout/collapse/parseability/commit token) supply a HARD stop trigger that is independent of the soft margin and cannot be gamed by rewording — the hybrid deterministic-guard + soft-judge controller 2607.13347 asks for, and the analogue of PUSV's argument that a deterministic check at <0.1 s beats a ~5 s model re-check. (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: gamma, the switch model, and every recall floor are calibrated PER STRATUM, since MARS calibrates per QUESTION with no condition key and 2607.06503's exchangeability assumption fails across SNR / accent / channel; cross-stratum transfer of the calibrated thresholds is reported as the generalization test and, if it fails, the certified claim is downgraded to calibrated.
- 成本与方差：Cost: warmup counted INSIDE the savings denominator (2606.12935 §3.1); budget unit reported in BOTH audio-seconds encoded and generated tokens, because a token-only efficiency metric does not price audio acquisition (the 2607.06503 gap); per-utterance latency (s/utt) as in ASR-TRA; dollar accounting per ATLAS's $/q with an explicit statement of what is excluded. Report recovery-per-call for every strategy on the KIT call-budget grid, which KIT never does. Variance: >=5 seeds (ATLAS n=5 GPQA 85.86 +/- 0.55 pp); achieved recall/abstention reported with its deviation from target across seeds; cluster-bootstrap CIs; the k-sweep for any fitted component; explicit existence-vs-separation seed budgeting (2607.17136 power curve).
- 击杀条件：Kill if (i) per-stratum certification is infeasible at our achievable sample sizes (the ~114-299 successful episodes per stratum requirement is INDEPENDENT of scorer quality), in which case the certified claim must be dropped and only the calibrated variant reported — reporting a certified claim we cannot support is itself the failure; (ii) adaptive allocation does not beat a uniform-N sweep at matched TOTAL cost on any stratum; (iii) the semantic-equivalence estimator's error exceeds the margin the rule certifies against; (iv) the non-composition check shows the global rate falls outside the promised band, i.e. the multi-gate design is unsound as built; (v) the stop signal's advantage disappears once warmup cost is inside the denominator.

**P 槽位**：NEW_SLOT_CANDIDATE — 'stop/abstain/budget calibration' is a cross-cutting slot in the D6 donor rollup with 6 first-listed donors (2604.18419, 2606.12935, 2606.28733, 2607.06503, 2607.17136, 2605.26849) and is not identical to any of P1-P10; it feeds P8 (budget-allocate as an exercised decision right) and P9 (the calibration machinery) but deserves its own charter

## D5.5 跨实例经验驱动的改进（TFRL 学习规则）

**定义**：The LEARNING RULE that converts per-instance outcomes into an advantage estimate which changes the NEXT instance's decision, with no weight updates: the advantage estimator, its eligibility key, credit assignment across multiple contributing experiences, utility update and decay, and the improvement-curve readout. D2 owns the STORE; D5 owns the RULE.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT (D5 nine-paper read set + d0 quintet, cut 2026-07-26). ASR-TRA 2603.05231 explicitly RESTORES theta and the soft prompt after every utterance (Alg. 1 lines 12-14) so updates never accumulate, and names 'implicit few-shot learning where temporally accumulated feedback across utterances provides supervision' as future work. Omni-RRM 2602.00846, TTS 2606.18323, AudioProcessBench 2606.09925 and KIT 2606.04730 all carry zero state across instances. In the d0 quintet, EChO-Agent 2606.15141 and Omni-Decision 2607.11433 are query-scoped by construction (Omni-Decision's evidence state is explicitly query-scoped). No paper in this read set reports a cross-instance improvement CURVE for a speech/omni system at all, which is the primary readout this sub-direction needs.

**视觉域对照**：PARTIAL, and mostly on D2's side of the seam. 2606.17929 PreAct (computer-use agents get faster on repeated tasks) is the write-gate-plus-reuse instance; 2605.24785 PANDO does online skill distillation including RETIREMENT; 2602.20323 PhysMem scales test-time memory for embodied physical reasoning with symbolic-state keys; 2606.31612 ATMem supplies a marginal-utility measurement for what memory is actually worth; 2605.18652 MementoGUI supplies gated retrieval; 2606.18448 VISUALSKILL supplies artifact structure and on-demand loading. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: every one of these keys on task text, symbolic state, or screen state; NONE keys on acoustic condition, which is not recoverable from the query and must itself be estimated by a component that can be wrong — an error channel with no analogue in these donors. Also, most of what they contribute is storage and retrieval, which is D2's; D5 takes only the update rule, the eligibility semantics, and the curve.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED — this is the nearest occupied position to the whole dimension. 2510.08191 Training-Free GRPO: a SEMANTIC ADVANTAGE computed only for groups containing BOTH winners and losers (the natural-language analogue of GRPO's A_i=0 when std(r)=0), an Add/Delete/Modify/Keep write-gate vocabulary, 3 epochs x 100 items for $8-18 total; AIME24 +2.7 / AIME25 +5.4 on a strong backbone but QwQ-32B WebWalkerQA pass@1 DROPS 27.5 -> 25.5 while pass@3 rises 43.1 -> 45.1; count-matched directly-generated experiences (no group, no reward) HURT (66.7 -> 64.7), so the effect is attributed to reward-driven comparison rather than tips-in-context; the no-ground-truth variant reaches 80.7/68.9 vs 82.7/73.3 with gold. 2601.18510 JitRL: retrieval-estimated V-hat / Q-hat / A-hat with an optimism bonus alpha/|N(s)|, and z' = z + beta*A proved to be the exact closed-form KL-constrained optimum (Thm 4.1) — but the DEPLOYED rule uses the max-normalized A-tilde (Eq. 25), so the shipped update is not literally what is proved, and beta is never reported or ablated; cross-task memory supplies ~47% of retrieved context (Table 6) and cross-task cold-start still beats all baselines (Table 5); the logit-update-vs-prompt-injection ablation (52.31 vs 49.46 Admin; 57.64 vs 53.02 Reddit) is the control that separates 'reward steered the action' from 'context helped'; the verbalized-confidence path, the ONLY black-box-legal variant, carries ZERO reported numbers. 2601.03192 MemRL: Q <- Q + alpha(r - Q) with variance bound alpha/(2-alpha)*Var(r|s,m), a Q-calibration reliability diagram at Pearson r=0.861 (bin 0.2-0.3 -> 21.5% success; bin 0.9-1.0 -> 88.1%), a Forgetting Rate FR = N_lost/N_fail of 0.041 (vs MemP 0.051) that spikes to 0.073 when normalization and the similarity gate are removed, ~12% 'failure' memories retained in the highest-Q bins, and the HLE warning that cross-task retrieval ties single-task reflection at intra-dataset similarity 0.186. 2604.13552 TF-TTCL: label-free positive/negative construction with a WRITE-ABSTENTION rule (discard the sample entirely when all agents disagree) and the RANDOM-RULE control showing retrieval quality is undetectable on math (GSM8k 87.41 random vs 87.49 real) but decisive on unstructured output (Finance 0.2665 vs 0.2863). 2303.11366 Reflexion is the unscored-append baseline both improve on. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: every reward here is a verifiable answer check or a programmatic success bit; the no-oracle fallbacks all need answer identity; JitRL's Jaccard similarity over text states has no waveform surface and its own limitation #3 ('key information hard to represent in text') names exactly the acoustic case (speaker identity, prosody, SNR, overlap, code-switch); MemRL's unbiasedness proof converges Q to E[proxy | s,m], not to true utility, so a Q-calibration curve can look excellent while tracking a hacked proxy; TF-TTCL's min-perplexity selection silently reintroduces a logprob dependency our contract does not grant.

**开放增量**：Define the advantage estimator and its ELIGIBILITY KEY for a speech deployment, and report the improvement curve no paper in the D5 read set reports.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 Four scoped moves: (i) construct the state abstraction for waveform observations (ASR hypothesis + acoustic descriptors + tool/observation history) — JitRL's own limitation #3 makes this load-bearing design, not a port detail; (ii) MEASURE the verbalized-confidence variant, the only black-box-legal advantage-to-action path, which JitRL announces and never quantifies; (iii) make the advantage CONDITION-KEYED so 'in low-SNR far-field audio, do X' is expressible — no donor supports this, and it is the D5 half of the D2 seam; (iv) adopt Avg-vs-Final over L sequential episodes as the primary readout, since the Final-minus-Avg gap IS the memory effect and no speech paper reports it. Credit assignment across several contributing experiences is explicitly open (MemRL §6 names Shapley / value decomposition as the fix).

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：A SEQUENTIAL-EPISODE carrier is required, and this dimension needs the instance stream, not just the items. MCIF long-form segments (T2, metadata_only) give an instance stream within a language/task. MS-SNSD 8 noise types (T2, metadata_only) give a repeated-CONDITION stream where each noise type is a recurring condition — the natural test of condition-keyed advantage. L2-Arctic 6 L1s (T2, metadata_only) give a speaker/accent stream. Dynamic-SUPERB (T2, metadata_only; recorded as living/version-drifting, so a snapshot must be pinned) gives a 55-task cross-task stream for the cross-task cold-start isolation gate. ACQUISITION: all metadata_only, same bulk item; Dynamic-SUPERB additionally needs a pinned snapshot because of version drift.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (weak core, where JitRL-style gains would be largest if the ATLAS asymmetry held — but it does not hold reliably, see caveat) and Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B via the local llama.cpp lane, CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY. WEAK-CORE CAVEAT with a second edge specific to this sub-direction: Training-Free GRPO's QwQ-32B pass@1 REGRESSION (27.5 -> 25.5) shows the mechanism transfers unevenly even WITHIN text across core strengths, so a positive curve on the local lane is not a prediction about a strong frozen omni core, and the ATLAS direction instability (weak-core advantage on 2 of 3 benchmarks, reversed on HLE-Verified) must be stated explicitly.
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows, INSTANTIATED explicitly over the sequential instance stream rather than left as a bare count (A6)【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】: (1) DIRECT READOUT — a memoryless run at the same per-item budget, no memory consulted; (2) RANDOM-AMONG-N — random retrieval from the memory library at matched injection budget, the honest null separating 'the right experience' from 'having any experience injected'; (3) CONSENSUS-ONLY — majority over retrieved-and-injected variants WITHOUT utility scores, i.e. inject but do not rank by advantage; (4) ORACLE — per-instance best choice over the memory-variant ladder below at matched episode count, computed OFFLINE from gold (gold-consuming, ceiling only, never in the loop), with recovery = realized/oracle reported. PLUS the memory-specific ladder, since the interesting contrast here is against weaker memory designs rather than against no wrapper: Static / no-memory; FIFO-transcript memory; unscored append (Reflexion shape); utility-scored retrieval (MemRL shape); reward-driven contrastive library (Training-Free GRPO shape). PLUS a TRAINED_MIRROR_COMPARATOR row, cited never as our number, so 'zero-gradient parity' (总集成/维度纲领 owner ruling) is a measurable claim: 2606.19341 LVBench 43.0 base → 48.7 agentic-SFT → 49.8 vanilla-GRPO → 50.5 TAURA, with non-agentic SFT regressing to 41.6 — the gradient-based evolution ladder D5's training-free rule must match at zero gradient.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A8】 Isolation gates copied verbatim: JitRL's CROSS-TASK COLD-START (retrieve ONLY from disjoint tasks/conditions, Table 5) and cross-task utilization share (Table 6, ~47%); Training-Free GRPO's group-size-1-vs-G ablation (isolates whether COMPARISON carries the effect) and its COUNT-MATCHED directly-generated-experience placebo; TF-TTCL's RANDOM-RULE control; JitRL's logit-update-vs-prompt-injection control adapted to our black-box form (advantage-conditioned action choice vs plain context injection of the same retrieved text). Anchors with domain labels: JitRL WebArena Final 51.35 vs Static 36.30 vs best training-free baseline EvoTest 42.49 (text); Training-Free GRPO AIME24 +2.7 with the QwQ-32B regression as the honest counterweight (text); MemRL Q-calibration r=0.861 and FR 0.041 -> 0.073 (text).
- 信号与合法性：DEPLOYMENT-LEGAL outcome proxies for the group ranking: consensus/MBR agreement, semantic-cluster vote margin, external-judge margin (Omni-RRM / AudioProcessBench critic / TRACE), CLAP with its rho=-0.431 fidelity caveat. The GROUP-COMPARISON GATE (produce an advantage only when the group contains both clear winners and losers) is deployment-legal and free, and it is the cheapest way to avoid burning the controller on uninformative groups. FENCED to offline one-time calibration on a self-carved split: any gold-scored fit or validation of the Q-reliability diagram; the WITH-GOLD versus WITHOUT-GOLD contrast arm (the 82.7/73.3 vs 80.7/68.9 shape from 2510.08191) run offline to bound how much survives losing the oracle. The GT-access hook is mandatory here specifically — a memory that accumulates across instances is the classic vector for information-boundary leakage, and this is my own recorded failure mode.
- 度量与门：Binding: G1, G2, G3, G7, G9, G10, G11. Primary readout: the Avg-vs-Final metric pair over L sequential episodes, with the Final-minus-Avg gap reported as the memory effect and CIs computed over PERMUTED instance orderings (order is a nuisance variable no donor controls). Memory-health instruments adopted: MemRL's Forgetting Rate FR = N_lost/N_fail and the Cumulative-vs-current success-rate gap as catastrophic-forgetting detectors; the Q-calibration reliability diagram as the P9 instrument for any learned utility. Token and semantic metrics paired on the same output. Per-condition stratified deltas are the point of the sub-direction, not a robustness check.
- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR does double duty: it defines 'the outcomes in this group actually differ' for the group-comparison gate, and 'this instance is the same case as that one' is decided by the eligibility key, not by it — keeping those two relations distinct is a design requirement, since conflating them is how a text-similarity key silently becomes an outcome-similarity key. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: structural CFR-shaped checks supply a component of the outcome proxy that is NOT judge-derived, so the accumulated advantage is not a pure function of one judge's opinion — this is the direct defence against MemRL's exposure that Q converges to E[proxy], not true utility. (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING IS the sub-direction's crux, not a refinement: the eligibility key must express the acoustic stratum (noise type, SNR band, L1/accent, language, speaker/overlap regime), and the cross-condition cold-start test (retrieve only from disjoint conditions) is the isolation gate that proves the key carries transferable content rather than instance memorization. MemRL's HLE result (advantage vanishes at intra-dataset similarity 0.186) sets the expectation that heterogeneous conditions degrade utility-keyed retrieval toward plain per-item reflection.
- 成本与方差：Cost: token-and-dollar accounting on the Training-Free GRPO template ($8-18 total learning; 38M input / 6.6M output tokens; ~60K input + 8K output per inference request with cache-hit pricing), extended with audio-seconds since re-listening is not free and, unlike text resampling, does not produce i.i.d. diversity. Memory-growth latency curve as a scaling reference (JitRL Table 23: 0-2500 entries -> 15-47 ms). Report the learning-dynamics plot alongside accuracy (Training-Free GRPO's average tool calls DECREASING across steps is an efficiency effect worth reporting even when accuracy is flat). Variance: >=5 seeds; CIs over permuted instance orderings as well as over seeds; report Mean@K rather than a single greedy run with train/eval temperature separated (0.7 learn / 0.3 eval in the donor); crossed condition-draw x seed grid.
- 击杀条件：Kill if (i) the Final-minus-Avg gap is within CI of zero across all condition streams — no accumulation, no dimension; (ii) the COUNT-MATCHED placebo (directly-generated experiences, no reward, no group) matches the learned library, which would mean the effect is context rather than advantage, exactly the control 2510.08191 runs on itself; (iii) cross-condition cold-start shows advantage transfers no better than TF-TTCL's random-rule control on the unstructured-output carrier; (iv) the verbalized-confidence variant carries no usable signal, since it is the only black-box-legal advantage-to-action path and JitRL never measured it; (v) the Forgetting Rate rises with accumulation, i.e. the rule is poisoning itself and the fix belongs to D2's write-gate rather than to D5's update rule.

**P 槽位**：P8 (reward-as-controller across instances), with an explicit D2 SEAM: D2 owns the store, retrieval index, write-gate mechanics and poisoning defence; D5 owns the advantage estimator, the eligibility-key semantics, credit assignment, and the improvement-curve readout

## D5.6 选择压力下的 reward hacking 与保真度衰减

**定义**：Measure and bound the regime in which increasing selection or repair pressure on a proxy signal makes TRUE quality worse, establish the honest baseline gap an unhacked speech system already shows under acoustic held-out shift, and build the deployment-time detector the donor lane names but does not construct.

**语音域状态**：ABSENT — no direct measurement of reward hacking exists in the D5 nine-paper read set, so P10 must carry its OWN first measurement design. Only PRE-CURSOR risk indicators are present: SelfGap self-bias (2606.09925 — among critics that are also generators, the two STRONGEST show the largest self-blindness, Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B -24.6 and Step-Audio-R1 -25.2 PRMScore points on their own traces versus others'; Qwen2.5-Omni-7B -17.8, Gemma-4-E4B -22.0; SelfGap is undefined for Gemini-3-Flash, the strongest critic overall at 67.9, because it is not a generator in that pool); non-monotonicity under verification noise (2601.21484 §6.3 — baseline test-time-scaling methods DEGRADE with more samples due to verification noise, while ETS itself claims not to); proxy fidelity CLAP Spearman rho = -0.431 against true WER, presented by its authors as reliability evidence with the 'weak for a controller' reading being ours (2603.05231); Omni-RRM audio preference accuracy 66.8 / 65.0, roughly 17 pp above chance (2602.00846). The compression result in 2606.09925 (51.5-point step-verification spread collapsing to a 9.5-point selection spread, with a below-random critic still gaining +6.9) is the audio-domain statement that selection utility is largely carried by the candidate distribution, which is exactly the regime where a weak proxy under pressure can dominate.

**视觉域对照**：PARTIAL. 2606.15608 RobustMLLMJudge supplies the three-family judge taxonomy (token-probability scoring / fine-grained decomposition / free-form prompting), a threat-model discipline that states exactly what the pressure may modify while holding reference, policy and protocol immutable, and an ESCALATING LADDER (benign -> semantically neutral degradation -> cheap handcrafted shortcut -> optimized attack) with per-protocol deltas reported against the benign score for every protocol; its attention-map diagnosis separates 'genuinely deceived' from 'took a shortcut', and it documents that a perturbation can be read as a DEFECT and produce NEGATIVE deltas. 2602.12276 CATTS supplies a dose-response protocol for harmful override under selection pressure in web agents. 2604.18164 MM-JudgeBias supplies compositional-bias certificates. 2607.09438 supplies a concrete instance of selection pressure doing net harm: PRM-BAS regresses -2.16pp on the high-confidence tier (48 corrections vs 119 regressions) and its scorer's top-vs-runner-up score gap is 0.049 on corrections and 0.048 on degradations, i.e. no discriminative signal. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: the pixel-space additive perturbation has NO analogue — in our program there is no adversary, the pressure comes from our own selection among candidates the frozen core produced, so no gradient-optimized delta exists and only the taxonomy and measurement structure survive; the token-probability family is excluded by contract; and critically the speech analogue of 'Gaussian blur as a semantically neutral control' is a channel/SNR perturbation that is NOT semantically neutral (it changes what is recoverable from the signal), so the degradation rung does not port as a control condition.

**文本域对照**：OCCUPIED and formalized. 2506.19248 (Best-of-Poisson / HedgeTune): Definition 1 gives the hacking threshold — the parameter beyond which expected TRUE reward decreases while proxy reward and KL keep increasing; Theorem 1 proves that for any strictly-TP2 family (covering Best-of-n, Best-of-Poisson and exponential tilts) the true-reward curve is monotone or has exactly ONE interior extremum, so the operating point is findable by one-dimensional root-finding; the SBoN stationarity condition Cov(r_true(u), u) = 0 is an interpretable estimable calibration statistic; and when the proxy is always at odds with truth the optimum is lambda=0, i.e. fall back to the reference policy — an abstain expressed as a selection parameter. The binding limitation is stated by the authors: 'we require black-box access to the true reward to perform a one-time calibration'. 2604.04648 (BASELINE_ANCHOR): the reporting triple Peak / Final at max N / Degradation with N swept 2^0..2^9 (GSM8K RM 79.3/71.5/7.7, Pessimism-only 81.3/80.3/1.0, RM+Pessimism 82.6/81.1/1.5; BBH RM 17.3/1.7/15.6 vs 22.9/22.1/0.9), plus the LCB construct r_hat - lambda*alpha; with the honest caveats that on MATH-500 and BBH the Pessimism-only arm wins (so the headline is closer to 'typicality selection beats this reward model'), that a MORE accurate predictor gives WORSE caution, and that the uncertainty is read from the reward model's hidden states. 2502.00271 (Scaling Flaws): guided search beats repeated sampling at n=1 by +2.5 to +23.0 coverage and LOSES at n=32 by -3.1 to -43.8 (OOD -25.8 to -46.1), with 77.1-88.6% of guided failures attributed to SELECTION rather than generation, plus valid-path-sparsity stratification and softmax-temperature hedging whose optimal tau FLIPS between in-distribution and OOD; the paper names the missing detector as future work. 2605.21384 SpecBench: Delta = s_val - s_test under a strict no-new-requirements constraint, reported at IQM and P90, tracked at EVERY search step, with a validation-coverage ladder and a failure taxonomy in which deliberate exploits are the MINORITY and compositional failure the majority; its case study is the sharpest available exhibit of selection actively preferring the hack (AIDE discarded a genuine 7,900-line compiler at 53% val / 43% held-out for a 2,900-line lookup table at 97% val / 0% held-out). 2603.05739 supplies the BoN suboptimality theory with an explicit hacking term. TRANSFER BOUNDARY: HedgeTune needs a true-reward calibration set and its own proposed fallbacks (verifiable domains, LLM-as-judge, a stronger RM) are precisely what speech lacks — a text judge over a transcription is not a 'stronger true reward' but a differently-biased proxy that SHARES failure modes with the controller's proxy, silently violating the true/proxy separation the theory assumes; the single global theta-dagger is calibrated per (dataset, reward model) and would be mis-set exactly on the conditions that matter, so condition-keyed thresholds are required; the TP2 family assumes i.i.d. candidates from a FIXED reference policy while our repair/re-observe loop changes the candidate distribution between rounds, putting us outside the proven family so unimodality is a HYPOTHESIS for us, not an inherited theorem; SpecBench's Delta=0 no-hacking reference point requires exchangeable suites, whereas our held-out acoustic stratum is a genuine distribution shift and, with metric-based scoring, a nonzero baseline gap exists even for an honest system and must be ESTIMATED rather than assumed zero.

**开放增量**：A selection-pressure measurement on speech carriers — the D5 nine-paper read set contains only precursor indicators (cut 2026-07-26), not a prior measurement to improve on or a primacy claim to make here — designed around the two constraints the donors expose rather than importing their instruments unchanged:【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 (i) the 'true reward' must be a one-time OFFLINE gold-consuming instrument on a self-carved split, and the proxy/true separation must be ARGUED and measured (verifier error correlation per stratum) rather than assumed, because our natural judges share the audio front-end; (ii) the HONEST-DELTA baseline — what gap an unhacked system shows under acoustic held-out shift — must be estimated FIRST, because unlike SpecBench's exchangeable suites our held-out stratum is a real distribution shift and would otherwise be laundered as hacking. Downstream, the estimated hacking threshold and the SBoN stationarity statistic become inputs to D5.2's E1 margin precondition.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】

**实验设计**：

- 载体数据集：MMAU Test-mini + MMSU (T2, metadata_only) as the closed-answer carrier, where an N-sweep to 2^9 is affordable and answer identity is exact so the equivalence estimator is not confounding the measurement. MCIF (T2, metadata_only) as the open-output carrier where the equivalence estimator IS load-bearing and the contrast between the two carriers isolates its contribution. MS-SNSD 8 noise types x L2-Arctic 6 L1s (T2, metadata_only) as the condition-keyed theta-dagger axis, with a deliberate proxy/true separation stress: calibrate theta-dagger on 6 noise types and test on the 2 held out. AQUA-Bench + Audio-Hallucination (T2, metadata_only) as the 'proxy confidently wrong' probe, since 2604.25591 reports P(True) at 0.52 and 0.44 there. ACQUISITION: all metadata_only, same bulk item. NEXT_STEP_ACQUISITION: a speech analogue of SpecBench's spec / visible-proxy / held-out triple does not exist in T2 and would be a CONSTRUCTION item, not a download — flagged as such and not assumed available.
- 冻结核：Qwen2.5-Omni-7B (weak core, where hacking is easiest to induce and therefore the honest place to look for it first); Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B via the local llama.cpp lane as CHEAP VERIFICATION ONLY with the weak-core caveat and the ATLAS direction-instability statement; Gemini-3-Flash (T3, proprietary API) as a cross-family judge specifically to break the SelfGap coupling, since 2606.09925 shows the strongest same-family critics are the most self-blind and 2512.02304 shows FPR rises with solver-verifier similarity (cross-family FPR r=0.613, slope 3.521).
- 基线阶梯：Four fixed rows (direct readout / random-among-N / consensus-only / oracle), reported at EVERY point of the N-sweep rather than once — the consensus-only null in particular must appear at every N, because MV beats BoN for every real critic in 2606.09925 and a below-random critic still gained +6.9 there. The sweep itself is reported as the 2604.04648 triple per stratum: Peak accuracy / Final accuracy at max N / Degradation = Peak - Final. Anchors with domain labels: 2604.04648 GSM8K RM 79.3/71.5/7.7 and BBH RM 17.3/1.7/15.6 (text); 2502.00271 guided +2.5..+23.0 at n=1 collapsing to -3.1..-43.8 at n=32, OOD worse (text); 2607.09438 PRM-BAS 80.74 vs SC majority 81.13 at 8.7x cost with a -2.16pp high-confidence-tier regression (vision); 2607.13347 all judge policies at negative recovery with always-iter0 winning (document/vision).
- 信号与合法性：PROXY signals under pressure (all deployment-legal): MBR/chrF consensus, discrete semantic entropy and vote-rate P(True) counts-only, the AudioProcessBench reference-free critic, Omni-RRM released weights, CLAP audio-text similarity, TRACE. TRUE-REWARD instruments, ALL fenced to a one-time offline calibration on a self-carved split and never in the loop: gold transcripts and WER, MMAR-Rubrics (offline-calibration-only, gold CoT + gold answer, paid Gemini-2.5-Pro), and human-adjudicated adequacy on the carved split. Enforcement is mechanical, not procedural: the 2607.13347 file-open hook with violation counts logged, because this sub-direction is the one that most tempts a gold leak. Explicit pre-registration required: BEFORE the sweep, measure the agreement between the true-reward instrument and each proxy on the carved split, so a shared failure mode is detected rather than assumed away — this is the speech-specific violation of the true/proxy separation HedgeTune's theory needs.
- 度量与门：Binding: G1, G3, G4, G6, G7, G8, G10 (SelfGap is a P10 pre-cursor and must be reported for every critic-generator pair actually deployed), G11. Additional instruments: the hacking threshold of Definition 1 estimated PER STRATUM; the SBoN stationarity statistic Cov(r_true, u) = 0 reported as a calibration readout; the corrections-vs-regressions ledger rather than a net delta; the tie-block permutation null; IQM and P90 of Delta rather than means (SpecBench: the P90 is where the severe cases live and the mean hides them); the gap-vs-pressure curve tracked at every step of the selection loop, not only at the end. The HONEST-DELTA baseline is a first-class reported row: the gap an unhacked system shows across the same held-out acoustic split, without which Delta is uninterpretable. Token and semantic metrics paired on the same output — a proxy that hacks a token metric while a semantic metric holds (or vice versa) is exactly the phenomenon.
