- 三件设计件：(a) ANSWER-EQUIVALENCE ESTIMATOR is itself a HACKING SURFACE here: an over-merging clusterer hides competitors and inflates apparent consensus, an under-merging one deflates it, so the estimator's error rate is reported alongside every N-sweep point and a threshold-sensitivity sweep is mandatory — if the sweep flips the sign of Delta, the measurement is measuring the estimator. (b) DETERMINISTIC-ORACLE SUBSTITUTE: structural CFR-shaped checks are the ONE signal component a proxy cannot hack by rewording, and they supply the VETO channel SpecBench says a selection plane needs — the AIDE case is precisely a structurally degenerate candidate scoring 97% on the proxy, and the speech analogue is a fluent, high-judge-score response that drops speakers, entities, timestamps or numbers. (c) ACOUSTIC CONDITION KEYING: theta-dagger is fit per stratum and the cross-stratum MIS-SETTING is measured explicitly (calibrate on 6 noise types, test on 2), because a single global threshold is the named weakness of the donor and speech's condition shift is exactly the case where it fails.
- 成本与方差：Cost: N swept 2^0..2^9 on the closed-answer carrier and 2^0..2^5 on the open-output carrier (cost-bounded), with cost per decision on the x-axis so every curve is a cost-quality frontier rather than an N-curve; audio-seconds encoded reported alongside tokens and dollars. Note the transfer hazard that our budgets may never reach the over-optimization regime the code/text donors reach cheaply (SpecBench's agent iterates against its visible suite thousands of times at near-zero marginal cost), so a null result must be reported as budget-scoped. Variance: >=4 seeds (matching the HedgeTune two-arm 4-seed design) and >=5 where affordable; bootstrap CIs; IQM and P90 rather than means for Delta; paired Wilcoxon / exact McNemar for policy contrasts; report the per-stratum sample sizes, since the strata partition the data and thin strata carry no resolution.
- 击杀条件：Kill if (i) no stratum shows a hacking threshold within the affordable N range AND the honest-Delta baseline is indistinguishable from the measured Delta — we then cannot separate hacking from acoustic shift at our effect sizes, and the honest report is a budget-scoped null, not a claim; (ii) the equivalence-estimator sensitivity sweep flips the sign of Delta; (iii) the true-reward instrument's agreement with the proxies on the carved split shows a shared failure mode at a level we can detect, in which case the contrast is invalid and a genuinely independent true-reward instrument must be found before any P10 claim; (iv) per-stratum sample sizes are too thin to distinguish Peak from Final at the observed variance.

**P 槽位**：P10 (reward hacking under selection pressure), feeding P9 — the estimated hacking threshold and the stationarity statistic become the margin precondition of D5.2's E1 gate【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】

## D5 维度综合

【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7：本段以下全部子方向编号已从 D5.2..D5.1（旧方案）改回标题编号 D5.1..D5.6，映射＝D5.1→D5.1、D5.2→D5.2、D5.3→D5.3、D5.4→D5.4、D5.5→D5.5、D5.6→D5.6】ORDERING LOGIC. The six sub-directions are a control loop read in dependency order, and each is a precondition for the honest interpretation of the next. D5.1 (label-free headroom) asks whether there is anything to win at all, and supplies the headroom precondition; without it every downstream recovery number is uninterpretable, which is exactly the state of the speech evidence today (AudioProcessBench publishes no oracle row; Omni-RRM publishes no pass@N ceiling; KIT publishes an oracle but resolves the decision with labels). D5.2 (E1 admissibility gate) asks whether THIS signal may steer THIS instance under THIS acoustic condition, and consumes D5.1's estimate as one of four preconditions. D5.3 (repair + no-regression abstain) is what the licence permits the plane to do beyond selection, and is the least-occupied decision-right pair in the read set (its abstain/no-regression GATE itself is D4.3's architecture seat per A1; D5.3 supplies the reward-diagnostic repair action and the margin signal P9/D5.2 calibrates). D5.4 (budget/stop/allocate) sets how much is spent and when to commit, and is the sharpest cheap step because three speech papers already compute an allocation statistic (ASR-TRA's adv_i, Omni-RRM's Delta<2 margin, TTS's saturation-N) and none consumes it. D5.5 (cross-instance advantage) is what makes the system EVOLVE rather than merely act, and is where the dimension's name is earned; it is also where nothing exists on the speech side at all. D5.6 (hacking under selection pressure) is the failure mode all five generate, and it must ship its own first measurement because the read set contains only precursor indicators.\n\nHOW THEY RELATE, CONCRETELY. D5.1 -> D5.2: headroom is a gate precondition, and both share the same fenced gold split and the same GT-access hook. D5.6 -> D5.2: the estimated hacking threshold and the SBoN stationarity statistic ARE the gate's margin precondition, so D5.6 is the offline pressure characterization and D5.2 is its deployed consumer; they share the true-reward instrument and its fencing. D5.4 <-> D5.2: the stop rule is a gate with a different validity target (the full-budget decision rather than truth), so the certified-versus-calibrated separation discipline propagates from D5.4 into D5.2's reporting. D5.3 <-> D5.4: repair rounds ARE a budget, so the repair-round sweep and the stop rule are one curve, not two; the abstain gate (D4.3's seat, calibrated via D5.3's margin signal and P9/D5.2) and the abstain calibration of D5.4 must be reported on the same accuracy-versus-abstention-rate axis. D5.5 -> D5.2/D5.4: as deployment statistics accumulate, the gate's per-stratum thresholds and the stop rule's contraction should be REFIT, which is the operational meaning of 'the system gets stronger without weight updates'; D5.5 supplies the accumulation rule, the other two supply the things that get refit.\n\nSHARED INFRASTRUCTURE (build once, used six times). One answer-equivalence estimator (S2ER 3-round bidirectional, majority-3), whose error is measured on a held-out human-adjudicated subset and propagated everywhere — it defines clusters for entropy, votes for consensus and stopping, correctness for the ledger, and pool membership for the oracle; a sensitivity sweep over its threshold is mandatory in all six because it is simultaneously the enabling instrument and the largest single confound. One deterministic-oracle substitute (CFR-shaped structural checks generalized to understanding outputs, plus the target-preservation invariant of speaker/entity/timestamp/numeric sets), which supplies the un-hackable veto channel, the label-free headroom lower bound, the hard stop trigger, and the repair guard. One acoustic stratification (MS-SNSD 8 noise types x L2-Arctic 6 L1s x MCIF 4 languages), which is the keying axis for every threshold and the generalization test for every fitted component. One baseline harness with the four fixed rows and the G1-G11 reporting obligations. One mechanically enforced information boundary (file-open hook, violations logged) and one fenced offline calibration split carrying every gold-consuming instrument.\n\nCROSS-DIMENSION SEAMS. D2 (memory): D2 owns the store, schema, retrieval index, write-gate mechanics and poisoning defence; D5.5 owns the advantage estimator, the eligibility-key SEMANTICS (what the key must express: acoustic stratum), credit assignment across contributing entries, and the Avg-vs-Final improvement curve. The shared artifact is the condition key — D2 stores and retrieves it, D5 specifies and validates it. Donors 2601.03192, 2510.08191, 2604.13552, 2606.17929, 2602.20323 are jointly relevant and should be cited on both sides with the split stated. RECIPROCAL SEAM WITH D2.3 (write-gate, stated on D2's side as seam iii, restated here so D5.2 does not calibrate independently): the same statistic family (consensus concentration, judge margin, vote-rate P(True), counts-only semantic entropy) is used by D5.2's E1 admissibility gate to decide abstain AND by D2.3's write gate to decide admission/retrieval — both uses MUST share one calibration on one split, not two independent fits on two splits, or the two thresholds can contradict each other on the same statistic.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9】 D1 (knowledge/observation supply, P1): D1 owns whether to acquire another observation and its price; D5.4 owns whether to spend another generation/verification unit and when to commit. They consume ONE budget, so the cost axis (audio-seconds + calls + dollars) must be shared or the two dimensions will double-count; donors 2605.05701, 2602.16699, 2604.14853, 2605.01345, 2606.03054 feed D1 primarily. D3 (tools/skills, P5): D3 owns tool induction, retirement and per-tool credit artifacts; D5.5 owns the credit-assignment RULE when several tools or memories contributed, which MemRL names as unsolved.【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】 D4 (agentic system, P6): D4 owns composition and topology and holds the task-conditioned wrapper-versus-direct-readout fact; every D5 experiment REUSES D4's direct-readout floor row, and the division is that D4 asks whether the architecture wins while D5 asks whether the win is DYNAMIC — larger on instance 50 than on instance 1, and larger where the signal was licensed than where it was not.\n\nWHAT IS DELIBERATELY NOT CLAIMED. No sub-direction asserts that speech is empty at the field level; every status is scoped to the read set (D5 nine papers + d0 quintet + D6 73 donors, cut 2026-07-26). No cross-modality effectiveness conclusion is drawn from any vision or text donor; they are METHOD and PROTOCOL sources with domain labels, and their effect sizes stay labelled. No novelty verdict is issued anywhere. Three of the six (D5.1, D5.6, D5.4) carry a serious probability of a negative or budget-scoped-null result【复核更正 wf_8bb251b9，A7 重编号】, and their kill criteria are written so that the null is reportable rather than laundered — 2606.13156's 'no observable recovers the oracle' and 2607.13347's non-replication of its own headline mean are the models for how such a null is disclosed.
